    float p = (g->q1 * g->q1) + (g->q2 * g->q2) - (coeff * g->q1 * g->q2);
    if (p < 0.0f) p = 0.0f;
    return sqrtf(p);
}

// ============================================================
// Impedance check
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_biopot_impedance_check(const nocti_biopot_imp_cfg_t *cfg,
                                      nocti_biopot_imp_result_t *out_opt)
{
    if (!cfg || !out_opt) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    esp_err_t err = ensure_idle();
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    nocti_biopot_imp_cfg_t c = *cfg;

    if (c.n_samples == 0) c.n_samples = 250;
    if (c.fs_hz <= 0.0f) c.fs_hz = 250.0f;

    uint8_t reg_loff = 0;
    uint8_t reg_sensp = 0;
    uint8_t reg_sensn = 0;
    uint8_t reg_c4 = 0;

    err = ads1299_send_cmd(ADS1299_CMD_SDATAC);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    err = reg_read_u8(ADS1299_REG_LOFF, &reg_loff);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    err = reg_read_u8(ADS1299_REG_LOFF_SENSP, &reg_sensp);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    err = reg_read_u8(ADS1299_REG_LOFF_SENSN, &reg_sensn);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    err = reg_read_u8(ADS1299_REG_CONFIG4, &reg_c4);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    uint8_t loff_new = build_loff_reg(c.i_mag, c.f_sel);
    err = reg_write_u8(ADS1299_REG_LOFF, loff_new);
    if (err != ESP_OK) goto restore;

    err = reg_write_u8(ADS1299_REG_LOFF_SENSP, c.loff_sensp_mask);
    if (err != ESP_OK) goto restore;

    err = reg_write_u8(ADS1299_REG_LOFF_SENSN, c.loff_sensn_mask);
    if (err != ESP_OK) goto restore;

    // slash: disable comparator agar tidak ganggu pembacaan amplitude
    uint8_t c4_new = (uint8_t)(reg_c4 & (uint8_t)~0x02u);
    err = reg_write_u8(ADS1299_REG_CONFIG4, c4_new);
    if (err != ESP_OK) goto restore;

    err = ads1299_send_cmd(ADS1299_CMD_START);
    if (err != ESP_OK) goto restore;

    err = ads1299_send_cmd(ADS1299_CMD_RDATAC);
    if (err != ESP_OK) goto restore;

    float f0 = 0.0f;
    if (c.f_sel == NOCTI_BIOPOT_LOFF_F_AC_7P8)  f0 = 7.8f;
    if (c.f_sel == NOCTI_BIOPOT_LOFF_F_AC_31P2) f0 = 31.2f;

    const bool do_goertzel = (f0 > 0.0f);
    float coeff = 0.0f;

    if (do_goertzel) {
        float w = (2.0f * 3.1415926f * f0) / c.fs_hz;
        coeff = 2.0f * cosf(w);
    } else {
        NOCTI_LOGW(TAG, "impedance_check DC: skip amplitude estimate, z_ohm diisi 0");
    }

    goertzel_t g[NOCTI_BIOPOT_NUM_CH];
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < NOCTI_BIOPOT_NUM_CH; i++) {
        goertzel_reset(&g[i]);
    }

    uint16_t discard = 10;

    memset(out_opt, 0, sizeof(*out_opt));

    for (uint16_t n = 0; n < (uint16_t)(c.n_samples + discard); n++) {
        err = wait_drdy_low(s.cfg.drdy_timeout_us);
        if (err != ESP_OK) goto stop_temp;

        memset(s.frame_tx, 0, sizeof(s.frame_tx));
        err = spi_xfer(s.frame_tx, s.frame_rx, NOCTI_BIOPOT_FRAME_BYTES);
        if (err != ESP_OK) goto stop_temp;

        if (n < discard) continue;
        if (!do_goertzel) continue;

        for (uint8_t ch = 0; ch < NOCTI_BIOPOT_NUM_CH; ch++) {
            uint32_t v24 = u24_be(&s.frame_rx[NOCTI_BIOPOT_STATUS_BYTES + (ch * 3u)]);
            int32_t x = s24_to_s32(v24);
            goertzel_step(&g[ch], (float)x, coeff);
        }
    }

    if (c.read_loff_status) {
        uint8_t sp = 0;
        uint8_t sn = 0;
        (void)reg_read_u8(ADS1299_REG_LOFF_STATP, &sp);
        (void)reg_read_u8(ADS1299_REG_LOFF_STATN, &sn);
        out_opt->loff_statp = sp;
        out_opt->loff_statn = sn;
    }

    if (do_goertzel) {
        const float fullscale = 8388607.0f;
        float i_amp = lead_off_current_amp(c.i_mag);

        for (uint8_t ch = 0; ch < NOCTI_BIOPOT_NUM_CH; ch++) {
            float mag = goertzel_mag(&g[ch], coeff);
            float amp_counts = (c.n_samples > 0) ? (2.0f * mag / (float)c.n_samples) : 0.0f;

            float gain = gain_to_float(s.cfg.ch[ch].gain);
            float v_amp = 0.0f;
            if (gain > 0.0f) {
                v_amp = (amp_counts / fullscale) * (s.cfg.vref_volts / gain);
            }

            float z = 0.0f;
            if (i_amp > 0.0f) z = v_amp / i_amp;
            out_opt->z_ohm[ch] = z;
        }
    } else {
        for (uint8_t ch = 0; ch < NOCTI_BIOPOT_NUM_CH; ch++) {
            out_opt->z_ohm[ch] = 0.0f;
        }
    }

stop_temp:
    (void)ads1299_send_cmd(ADS1299_CMD_SDATAC);
    (void)ads1299_send_cmd(ADS1299_CMD_STOP);

restore:
    (void)ads1299_send_cmd(ADS1299_CMD_SDATAC);
    (void)reg_write_u8(ADS1299_REG_LOFF, reg_loff);
    (void)reg_write_u8(ADS1299_REG_LOFF_SENSP, reg_sensp);
    (void)reg_write_u8(ADS1299_REG_LOFF_SENSN, reg_sensn);
    (void)reg_write_u8(ADS1299_REG_CONFIG4, reg_c4);

    return err;
}


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_ble\include\nocti_ble.h ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_ble/include/nocti_ble.h
// ============================================================

#pragma once
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>

#include "esp_err.h"
#include "freertos/FreeRTOS.h"

// forward decl supaya header ini ringan
typedef struct nocti_stream_t nocti_stream_t;

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

// ============================================================
// NoctiNIRS BLE (NimBLE, ESP-IDF v5.5.x)
// // belajar: module ini fokus ke BLE transport (GATT, advertising, notify)
// // belajar: producer data tetap nocti_stream, BLE hanya consumer + command RX
// ============================================================

// batas aman command dari central ke device
#define NOCTI_BLE_MAX_CMD_BYTES (256u)

// header fragment BLE untuk mengirim 1 stream frame yang ter-fragment
// total 8 byte supaya ringan
typedef struct __attribute__((packed)) {
    uint16_t frame_seq;   // slash: ID frame untuk grouping fragment (biasanya ambil dari nocti_stream_hdr.seq)
    uint16_t frame_len;   // slash: total panjang frame asli (byte)
    uint8_t  frag_idx;    // slash: index fragment [0..frag_cnt-1]
    uint8_t  frag_cnt;    // slash: total fragment untuk frame ini
    uint16_t frag_off;    // slash: offset data fragment di frame asli
} nocti_ble_frag_hdr_t;

// callback saat ada command dari central (WRITE ke characteristic CMD)
typedef void (*nocti_ble_cmd_cb_t)(const uint8_t *data, size_t len, void *user_ctx);

// callback event koneksi
typedef void (*nocti_ble_conn_cb_t)(bool connected, uint16_t conn_handle, void *user_ctx);

typedef struct {
    // basic identity
    const char *device_name;        // slash: nama BLE di iklan (advertising)
    uint16_t preferred_mtu;         // slash: request MTU via ATT exchange (misal 247 atau 256)

    // advertising interval (unit 0.625 ms sesuai BLE spec)
    uint16_t adv_itvl_min;          // slash: contoh 160 => 100 ms
    uint16_t adv_itvl_max;          // slash: contoh 240 => 150 ms
    bool adv_include_name;          // slash: taruh nama di advertising packet

    // command RX callback
    nocti_ble_cmd_cb_t  cmd_cb;     // slash: dipanggil saat ada WRITE pada CMD char
    void               *cmd_cb_ctx;

    // connection callback
    nocti_ble_conn_cb_t conn_cb;    // slash: inform connect / disconnect
    void               *conn_cb_ctx;

    // streaming from nocti_stream via notify
    nocti_stream_t *stream;              // slash: kalau NULL, BLE tidak auto kirim stream
    uint32_t stream_read_timeout_ms;     // slash: timeout read dari ringbuffer stream
    bool enable_stream_tx;              // slash: create task TX jika true dan stream non-NULL
    uint16_t tx_task_stack_words;        // slash: stack task TX (word, bukan byte)
    UBaseType_t tx_task_prio;            // slash: priority task TX
} nocti_ble_cfg_t;

typedef struct {
    bool inited;
    bool connected;
    bool notify_enabled;      // slash: central sudah enable notify untuk DATA char

    uint8_t  addr_type;       // slash: BLE address type yang dipilih stack
    uint16_t conn_handle;     // slash: handle koneksi aktif (valid kalau connected)

    // runtime config
    nocti_ble_cfg_t cfg;

    // internal scratch untuk command
    uint8_t cmd_buf[NOCTI_BLE_MAX_CMD_BYTES];

    // task handle untuk TX stream
    TaskHandle_t tx_task;
} nocti_ble_t;

// ============================================================
// API
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_ble_init(nocti_ble_t *b, const nocti_ble_cfg_t *cfg);
esp_err_t nocti_ble_start_advertising(nocti_ble_t *b);
esp_err_t nocti_ble_stop(nocti_ble_t *b);

// kirim 1 stream frame mentah (akan di-fragment sesuai MTU)
esp_err_t nocti_ble_send_stream_frame(nocti_ble_t *b, const uint8_t *frame, size_t frame_len);

// helper state
bool     nocti_ble_is_connected(const nocti_ble_t *b);
uint16_t nocti_ble_conn_handle(const nocti_ble_t *b);
bool nocti_ble_can_tx_stream(const nocti_ble_t *b);


#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_ble\nocti_ble.c ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_ble/nocti_ble.c
//
// Revisi fokus (stability untuk Web Bluetooth):
// 1) Gate stream TX sampai MTU runtime siap (>= 247)
// 2) Fix potensi mbuf leak saat notify gagal
// 3) Backoff ENOMEM lebih stabil
// 4) Coalescing target lebih kecil agar burst tidak bikin queue penuh
//
// slash:
// /belajar: drop_fnirs naik biasanya karena stream ring buffer overflow
// /belajar: overflow sering dipicu stall sesaat di central (Web Bluetooth)
// /belajar: mbuf leak dan retry agresif bikin stall jadi makin parah
// ============================================================

#include "nocti_ble.h"

#include <string.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stdint.h>

#include "freertos/FreeRTOS.h"
#include "freertos/task.h"

#include "nocti_log.h"
#include "nocti_stream.h"

#include "nvs_flash.h"

// NimBLE (ESP-IDF)
#include "nimble/nimble_port.h"
#include "nimble/nimble_port_freertos.h"
#include "host/ble_hs.h"
#include "host/ble_att.h"
#include "host/ble_uuid.h"
#include "host/util/util.h"
#include "services/gap/ble_svc_gap.h"
#include "services/gatt/ble_svc_gatt.h"
#include "os/os_mbuf.h"
#include "host/ble_gatt.h"

static const char *TAG = "nocti_ble";

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// ble_store_config_init dipakai banyak contoh NimBLE
// slash: deklarasi saja cukup, implementasi ada di util NimBLE pada ESP-IDF
// ------------------------------------------------------------
void ble_store_config_init(void);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// UUID custom NoctiNIRS (128-bit)
// slash:
// /belajar: BLE_UUID128_INIT memakai urutan little endian byte
// /belajar: byte ke 7 diset menjadi 0x01,0x02,0x03 untuk SVC,CMD,DATA
// ------------------------------------------------------------

static const ble_uuid128_t UUID_NOCTI_SVC =
    BLE_UUID128_INIT(0xa5,0x00,0x00,0x00,0x00,0x00,0x00,0x01, 0x53,0x52,0x49,0x4e,0x69,0x74,0x63,0x6f);

static const ble_uuid128_t UUID_NOCTI_CMD =
    BLE_UUID128_INIT(0xa5,0x00,0x00,0x00,0x00,0x00,0x00,0x02, 0x53,0x52,0x49,0x4e,0x69,0x74,0x63,0x6f);

static const ble_uuid128_t UUID_NOCTI_DATA =
    BLE_UUID128_INIT(0xa5,0x00,0x00,0x00,0x00,0x00,0x00,0x03, 0x53,0x52,0x49,0x4e,0x69,0x74,0x63,0x6f);

// ============================================================
// Internal state
// ============================================================

static nocti_ble_t *s_self = NULL;

static uint16_t s_cmd_val_handle  = 0;
static uint16_t s_data_val_handle = 0;

static bool s_adv_requested = false;
static bool s_adv_running   = false;

// slash: MTU runtime hasil negosiasi, awalnya 23 lalu naik saat MTU event datang
static volatile uint16_t s_mtu_runtime = 23;
#define NOCTI_BLE_STREAM_MTU_READY   (247u)

// slash: debug kesehatan notify
static volatile uint32_t s_notify_ok = 0;
static volatile uint32_t s_notify_enomem = 0;
static volatile uint32_t s_notify_fail = 0;

// ============================================================
// Helpers
// ============================================================

static inline uint16_t u16_le_read(const uint8_t *p)
{
    return (uint16_t)p[0] | ((uint16_t)p[1] << 8);
}

static inline uint16_t clamp_u16(uint16_t v, uint16_t lo, uint16_t hi)
{
    if (v < lo) return lo;
    if (v > hi) return hi;
    return v;
}

static inline uint16_t att_payload_max_from_mtu(uint16_t mtu)
{
    // slash: ATT notify payload max = mtu - 3
    if (mtu <= 3) return 0;
    return (uint16_t)(mtu - 3);
}

// ============================================================
// GATT access callback
// ============================================================

static int gatt_access_cb(uint16_t conn_handle,
                          uint16_t attr_handle,
                          struct ble_gatt_access_ctxt *ctxt,
                          void *arg)
{
    (void)arg;
    (void)conn_handle;

    if (!s_self) return BLE_ATT_ERR_UNLIKELY;

    // CMD characteristic (write only)
    if (attr_handle == s_cmd_val_handle) {

        if (ctxt->op != BLE_GATT_ACCESS_OP_WRITE_CHR) {
            return BLE_ATT_ERR_READ_NOT_PERMITTED;
        }

        size_t len = (size_t)OS_MBUF_PKTLEN(ctxt->om);
        if (len == 0) return 0;

        if (len > NOCTI_BLE_MAX_CMD_BYTES) {
            return BLE_ATT_ERR_INVALID_ATTR_VALUE_LEN;
        }

        int rc = os_mbuf_copydata(ctxt->om, 0, len, s_self->cmd_buf);
        if (rc != 0) return BLE_ATT_ERR_UNLIKELY;

        if (s_self->cfg.cmd_cb) {
            s_self->cfg.cmd_cb(s_self->cmd_buf, len, s_self->cfg.cmd_cb_ctx);
        }
        return 0;
    }

    // DATA characteristic (notify only)
    if (attr_handle == s_data_val_handle) {
        return BLE_ATT_ERR_READ_NOT_PERMITTED;
    }

    return BLE_ATT_ERR_UNLIKELY;
}

// ============================================================
// GATT service table
// ============================================================

static const struct ble_gatt_svc_def gatt_svcs[] = {
    {
        .type = BLE_GATT_SVC_TYPE_PRIMARY,
        .uuid = &UUID_NOCTI_SVC.u,
        .characteristics = (struct ble_gatt_chr_def[]) {
            {
                .uuid = &UUID_NOCTI_CMD.u,
                .access_cb = gatt_access_cb,
                .val_handle = &s_cmd_val_handle,
                .flags = BLE_GATT_CHR_F_WRITE | BLE_GATT_CHR_F_WRITE_NO_RSP,
            },
            {
                .uuid = &UUID_NOCTI_DATA.u,
                .access_cb = gatt_access_cb,
                .val_handle = &s_data_val_handle,
                .flags = BLE_GATT_CHR_F_NOTIFY,
            },
            { 0 }
        },
    },
    { 0 }
};

// ============================================================
// Advertising
// ============================================================

static void start_advertising_internal(void);

static int gap_event_cb(struct ble_gap_event *event, void *arg)
{
    (void)arg;

    nocti_ble_t *b = s_self;
    if (!b) return 0;

    switch (event->type) {

    case BLE_GAP_EVENT_CONNECT: {

        s_adv_running = false;

        if (event->connect.status == 0) {

            b->connected = true;
            b->conn_handle = event->connect.conn_handle;
            b->notify_enabled = false;

            // slash: reset state MTU dan counter saat connect baru
            s_mtu_runtime = clamp_u16(ble_att_mtu(b->conn_handle), 23, 517);
            s_notify_ok = 0;
            s_notify_enomem = 0;
            s_notify_fail = 0;

            NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "connected, handle=%u", (unsigned)b->conn_handle);

            // slash: info runtime
            NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "mtu runtime now=%u", (unsigned)s_mtu_runtime);

            struct ble_gap_conn_desc desc;
            if (ble_gap_conn_find(b->conn_handle, &desc) == 0) {
                NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "conn itvl=%u latency=%u timeout=%u",
                           (unsigned)desc.conn_itvl,
                           (unsigned)desc.conn_latency,
                           (unsigned)desc.supervision_timeout);
            }

            if (b->cfg.conn_cb) {
                b->cfg.conn_cb(true, b->conn_handle, b->cfg.conn_cb_ctx);
            }

        } else {
            NOCTI_LOGW(TAG, "connect failed, status=%d", event->connect.status);
            b->connected = false;
            b->notify_enabled = false;
            b->conn_handle = 0;
            start_advertising_internal();
        }
        return 0;
    }

    case BLE_GAP_EVENT_DISCONNECT: {

        NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "disconnect, reason=%d", event->disconnect.reason);

        b->connected = false;
        b->notify_enabled = false;
        b->conn_handle = 0;

        // slash: reset MTU state
        s_mtu_runtime = 23;

        if (b->cfg.conn_cb) {
            b->cfg.conn_cb(false, 0, b->cfg.conn_cb_ctx);
        }

        start_advertising_internal();
        return 0;
    }

    case BLE_GAP_EVENT_SUBSCRIBE: {

        if (event->subscribe.attr_handle == s_data_val_handle) {

            b->notify_enabled = (event->subscribe.cur_notify != 0);
            NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "subscribe DATA notify=%d", (int)b->notify_enabled);

            // slash: reset counter saat mulai notify untuk observability
            if (b->notify_enabled) {
                s_notify_ok = 0;
                s_notify_enomem = 0;
                s_notify_fail = 0;
            }
        }
        return 0;
    }

    case BLE_GAP_EVENT_MTU: {

        s_mtu_runtime = clamp_u16(event->mtu.value, 23, 517);
        NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "mtu updated, mtu=%u", (unsigned)s_mtu_runtime);
        return 0;
    }

    case BLE_GAP_EVENT_ADV_COMPLETE: {

        s_adv_running = false;
        if (s_adv_requested) {
            start_advertising_internal();
        }
        return 0;
    }

    default:
        return 0;
    }
}

static void start_advertising_internal(void)
{
    nocti_ble_t *b = s_self;
    if (!b) return;

    if (!s_adv_requested) return;

    if (!ble_hs_synced()) {
        NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "adv postponed, host not synced yet");
        return;
    }

    if (b->connected) return;
    if (s_adv_running) return;

    struct ble_hs_adv_fields adv;
    memset(&adv, 0, sizeof(adv));

    adv.flags = BLE_HS_ADV_F_DISC_GEN | BLE_HS_ADV_F_BREDR_UNSUP;

    if (b->cfg.adv_include_name) {
        const char *name = ble_svc_gap_device_name();
        size_t nlen = strlen(name);

        if (nlen <= 18) {
            adv.name = (uint8_t *)name;
            adv.name_len = (uint8_t)nlen;
            adv.name_is_complete = 1;
        } else {
            adv.name = (uint8_t *)name;
            adv.name_len = 18;
            adv.name_is_complete = 0;
        }
    }

    int rc = ble_gap_adv_set_fields(&adv);
    if (rc != 0) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "adv_set_fields rc=%d", rc);
        return;
    }

    struct ble_hs_adv_fields rsp;
    memset(&rsp, 0, sizeof(rsp));

    rsp.uuids128 = (ble_uuid128_t *)&UUID_NOCTI_SVC;
    rsp.num_uuids128 = 1;
    rsp.uuids128_is_complete = 1;

    rc = ble_gap_adv_rsp_set_fields(&rsp);
    if (rc != 0) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "adv_rsp_set_fields rc=%d", rc);
        return;
    }

    struct ble_gap_adv_params adv_params;
    memset(&adv_params, 0, sizeof(adv_params));

    adv_params.conn_mode = BLE_GAP_CONN_MODE_UND;
    adv_params.disc_mode = BLE_GAP_DISC_MODE_GEN;
    adv_params.itvl_min = b->cfg.adv_itvl_min;
    adv_params.itvl_max = b->cfg.adv_itvl_max;

    rc = ble_gap_adv_start(
        b->addr_type,
        NULL,
        BLE_HS_FOREVER,
        &adv_params,
        gap_event_cb,
        NULL
    );

    if (rc == 0) {
        s_adv_running = true;
        NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "advertising started");
        return;
    }

    if (rc == BLE_HS_EALREADY) {
        s_adv_running = true;
        NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "advertising already running");
        return;
    }

    NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "adv_start rc=%d", rc);
}

// ============================================================
// NimBLE host lifecycle
// ============================================================

static void on_reset(int reason)
{
    NOCTI_LOGW(TAG, "reset reason=%d", reason);
}

static void on_sync(void)
{
    nocti_ble_t *b = s_self;
    if (!b) return;

    int rc = ble_hs_id_infer_auto(0, &b->addr_type);
    if (rc != 0) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "ble_hs_id_infer_auto rc=%d", rc);
        return;
    }

    start_advertising_internal();
}

static void nimble_host_task(void *param)
{
    (void)param;

    nimble_port_run();

    nimble_port_freertos_deinit();
    (void)nimble_port_deinit();

    vTaskDelete(NULL);
}

// ============================================================
// Notify helpers
// ============================================================

static esp_err_t notify_one(const uint8_t *data, size_t len)
{
    // slash:
    // /belajar: notify_one hanya untuk 1 ATT notif payload
    // /belajar: kalau gagal, pastikan mbuf di free untuk mencegah leak
    nocti_ble_t *b = s_self;
    if (!b || !b->connected || !b->notify_enabled) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
    if (!data || len == 0) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    uint16_t mtu = clamp_u16(ble_att_mtu(b->conn_handle), 23, 517);
    uint16_t maxp = att_payload_max_from_mtu(mtu);

    if (maxp == 0) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_SIZE;
    if (len > (size_t)maxp) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_SIZE;

    struct os_mbuf *om = ble_hs_mbuf_from_flat(data, (uint16_t)len);
    if (!om) {
        s_notify_enomem++;
        return ESP_ERR_NO_MEM;
    }

    int rc = ble_gatts_notify_custom(b->conn_handle, s_data_val_handle, om);

    if (rc == 0) {
        s_notify_ok++;
        return ESP_OK;
    }

    // slash: penting, cegah mbuf leak saat notify gagal
    os_mbuf_free_chain(om);

    if (rc == BLE_HS_ENOTCONN) {
        s_notify_fail++;
        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
    }
    if (rc == BLE_HS_ENOMEM) {
        s_notify_enomem++;
        return ESP_ERR_NO_MEM;
    }

    s_notify_fail++;
    return ESP_FAIL;
}

esp_err_t nocti_ble_send_stream_frame(nocti_ble_t *b, const uint8_t *frame, size_t frame_len)
{
    if (!b || !b->inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
    if (!frame || frame_len == 0) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!b->connected || !b->notify_enabled) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

    uint16_t mtu = clamp_u16(ble_att_mtu(b->conn_handle), 23, 517);
    size_t att_payload_max = (size_t)att_payload_max_from_mtu(mtu);

    const size_t hdr_sz = sizeof(nocti_ble_frag_hdr_t);
    if (att_payload_max <= hdr_sz) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_SIZE;

    size_t frag_data_max = att_payload_max - hdr_sz;

    size_t frag_cnt_sz = (frame_len + frag_data_max - 1) / frag_data_max;
    if (frag_cnt_sz == 0) frag_cnt_sz = 1;
    if (frag_cnt_sz > 255) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_SIZE;

    uint8_t frag_cnt = (uint8_t)frag_cnt_sz;

    uint16_t frame_seq = 0;
    if (frame_len >= 6 && frame[0] == 0xA5) {
        frame_seq = u16_le_read(&frame[4]);
    }

    uint8_t pkt[520];

    for (uint8_t frag_idx = 0; frag_idx < frag_cnt; frag_idx += 1) {

        size_t off = (size_t)frag_idx * frag_data_max;
        size_t remain = frame_len - off;
        size_t n = (remain < frag_data_max) ? remain : frag_data_max;

        nocti_ble_frag_hdr_t fh;
        fh.frame_seq = frame_seq;
        fh.frame_len = (uint16_t)frame_len;
        fh.frag_idx  = frag_idx;
        fh.frag_cnt  = frag_cnt;
        fh.frag_off  = (uint16_t)off;

        memcpy(&pkt[0], &fh, hdr_sz);
        memcpy(&pkt[hdr_sz], &frame[off], n);

        int tries = 0;
        esp_err_t err = ESP_FAIL;

        while (tries < 6) {
            err = notify_one(pkt, hdr_sz + n);
            if (err == ESP_OK) break;

            if (err == ESP_ERR_NO_MEM) {
                // slash: backoff bertahap agar host dan central sempat drain
                uint32_t d_ms = 5u + (uint32_t)tries * 5u; // 5,10,15,20...
                vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(d_ms));
                tries += 1;
                continue;
            }

            break;
        }

        if (err != ESP_OK) return err;
    }

    return ESP_OK;
}

// ============================================================
// TX task (coalescing)
// ============================================================

static void tx_task_fn(void *param)
{
    (void)param;

    nocti_ble_t *b = s_self;
    if (!b) {
        vTaskDelete(NULL);
        return;
    }

    // slash:
    // /belajar: target lebih kecil lebih stabil untuk Web Bluetooth
    #define NOCTI_BLE_COALESCE_MAX_BYTES     (2048u)
    #define NOCTI_BLE_COALESCE_TARGET_BYTES  (800u)
    #define NOCTI_BLE_COALESCE_MAX_FRAMES    (64u)

    uint8_t blob[NOCTI_BLE_COALESCE_MAX_BYTES];
    size_t  blob_len = 0;

    uint8_t frame[NOCTI_STREAM_MAX_FRAME_BYTES];

    uint32_t last_dbg_ms = 0;

    while (s_self && b->inited) {

        if (!b->cfg.enable_stream_tx || !b->cfg.stream) {
            blob_len = 0;
            vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(50));
            continue;
        }

        if (!b->connected || !b->notify_enabled) {
            blob_len = 0;
            vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(20));
            continue;
        }

        // slash: debug ringan tiap 10 detik
        uint32_t now_ms = (uint32_t)(xTaskGetTickCount() * portTICK_PERIOD_MS);
        if ((now_ms - last_dbg_ms) >= 10000u) {
            last_dbg_ms = now_ms;
            NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "tx dbg mtu=%u ok=%u enomem=%u fail=%u",
                       (unsigned)s_mtu_runtime,
                       (unsigned)s_notify_ok,
                       (unsigned)s_notify_enomem,
                       (unsigned)s_notify_fail);
        }

        // 1) Ambil minimal 1 frame dengan timeout normal
        size_t out_len = 0;
        esp_err_t r = nocti_stream_read(
            b->cfg.stream,
            frame,
            sizeof(frame),
            &out_len,
            b->cfg.stream_read_timeout_ms
        );

        if (r == ESP_OK && out_len > 0) {

            // kalau blob kosong tapi frame lebih besar dari blob, kirim frame langsung
            if (blob_len == 0 && out_len > sizeof(blob)) {
                (void)nocti_ble_send_stream_frame(b, frame, out_len);
                continue;
            }

            // kalau tidak muat, flush blob dulu
            if (blob_len > 0 && (blob_len + out_len) > sizeof(blob)) {
                (void)nocti_ble_send_stream_frame(b, blob, blob_len);
                blob_len = 0;
            }

            if ((blob_len + out_len) <= sizeof(blob)) {
                memcpy(&blob[blob_len], frame, out_len);
                blob_len += out_len;
            }

            // 2) Ambil frame tambahan tanpa blocking sampai target tercapai
            uint32_t n_frames = 1;

            while (blob_len < (size_t)NOCTI_BLE_COALESCE_TARGET_BYTES &&
                   n_frames < (uint32_t)NOCTI_BLE_COALESCE_MAX_FRAMES) {

                out_len = 0;
                r = nocti_stream_read(
                    b->cfg.stream,
                    frame,
                    sizeof(frame),
                    &out_len,
                    0
                );

                if (!(r == ESP_OK && out_len > 0)) break;
                if ((blob_len + out_len) > sizeof(blob)) break;

                memcpy(&blob[blob_len], frame, out_len);
                blob_len += out_len;
                n_frames += 1;
            }

            // 3) Kirim blob jika sudah cukup penuh
            if (blob_len >= (size_t)NOCTI_BLE_COALESCE_TARGET_BYTES) {
                (void)nocti_ble_send_stream_frame(b, blob, blob_len);
                blob_len = 0;
            }

            continue;
        }

        if (r == ESP_ERR_TIMEOUT) {
            // slash: flush blob agar latency tidak membesar
            if (blob_len > 0) {
                (void)nocti_ble_send_stream_frame(b, blob, blob_len);
                blob_len = 0;
            }
            continue;
        }

        // error lain
        if (blob_len > 0) {
            (void)nocti_ble_send_stream_frame(b, blob, blob_len);
            blob_len = 0;
        }
        vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(10));
    }

    vTaskDelete(NULL);
}

// ============================================================
// Public API
// ============================================================

static esp_err_t nvs_init_safe(void)
{
    esp_err_t err = nvs_flash_init();
    if (err == ESP_ERR_NVS_NO_FREE_PAGES || err == ESP_ERR_NVS_NEW_VERSION_FOUND) {
        esp_err_t e2 = nvs_flash_erase();
        if (e2 != ESP_OK) return e2;
        err = nvs_flash_init();
    }
    return err;
}

esp_err_t nocti_ble_init(nocti_ble_t *b, const nocti_ble_cfg_t *cfg)
{
    if (!b || !cfg) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    memset(b, 0, sizeof(*b));
    b->cfg = *cfg;

    s_adv_requested = false;
    s_adv_running = false;

    if (!b->cfg.device_name) b->cfg.device_name = "NoctiNIRS";
    if (b->cfg.preferred_mtu == 0) b->cfg.preferred_mtu = 247;

    if (b->cfg.adv_itvl_min == 0) b->cfg.adv_itvl_min = 160;
    if (b->cfg.adv_itvl_max == 0) b->cfg.adv_itvl_max = 240;

    if (b->cfg.tx_task_stack_words == 0) b->cfg.tx_task_stack_words = 4096;
    if (b->cfg.tx_task_prio == 0) b->cfg.tx_task_prio = 6;

    s_self = b;

    esp_err_t err = nvs_init_safe();
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    err = nimble_port_init();
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    ble_svc_gap_init();
    ble_svc_gatt_init();

    ble_svc_gap_device_name_set(b->cfg.device_name);

    uint16_t mtu = clamp_u16(b->cfg.preferred_mtu, 23, 517);
    ble_att_set_preferred_mtu(mtu);

    int rc = ble_gatts_count_cfg(gatt_svcs);
    if (rc != 0) return ESP_FAIL;

    rc = ble_gatts_add_svcs(gatt_svcs);
    if (rc != 0) return ESP_FAIL;

    ble_store_config_init();

    ble_hs_cfg.reset_cb = on_reset;
    ble_hs_cfg.sync_cb  = on_sync;

    BaseType_t ok = xTaskCreate(nimble_host_task, "nimble_host", 4096, NULL, 6, NULL);
    if (ok != pdPASS) return ESP_ERR_NO_MEM;

    if (b->cfg.enable_stream_tx && b->cfg.stream) {
        BaseType_t ok2 = xTaskCreate(
            tx_task_fn,
            "nocti_ble_tx",
            b->cfg.tx_task_stack_words,
            NULL,
            b->cfg.tx_task_prio,
            &b->tx_task
        );
        if (ok2 != pdPASS) return ESP_ERR_NO_MEM;
    }

    b->inited = true;

    // slash: init state MTU default
    s_mtu_runtime = 23;

    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "ble inited, name=%s, mtu=%u", b->cfg.device_name, (unsigned)mtu);
    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t nocti_ble_start_advertising(nocti_ble_t *b)
{
    if (!b || !b->inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

    s_adv_requested = true;
    start_advertising_internal();
    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t nocti_ble_stop(nocti_ble_t *b)
{
    if (!b || !b->inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

    s_adv_requested = false;
    s_adv_running = false;

    (void)ble_gap_adv_stop();

    if (b->connected) {
        (void)ble_gap_terminate(b->conn_handle, BLE_ERR_REM_USER_CONN_TERM);
    }

    b->connected = false;
    b->notify_enabled = false;
    b->conn_handle = 0;

    s_mtu_runtime = 23;

    (void)nimble_port_stop();
    return ESP_OK;
}

bool nocti_ble_is_connected(const nocti_ble_t *b)
{
    return (b && b->connected);
}

bool nocti_ble_can_tx_stream(const nocti_ble_t *b)
{
    // slash:
    // /belajar: gate TX sampai MTU runtime sudah siap
    // /belajar: ini mencegah backlog saat awal connect MTU masih 23
    return (b && b->connected && b->notify_enabled && (s_mtu_runtime >= NOCTI_BLE_STREAM_MTU_READY));
}

uint16_t nocti_ble_conn_handle(const nocti_ble_t *b)
{
    return b ? b->conn_handle : 0;
}


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_board\include\nocti_board.h ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_board/include/nocti_board.h
// ============================================================
//
// Tujuan file ini
// - Menjadi "single source of truth" untuk pin mapping dan bring-up NoctiNIRS board
// - Board yang memiliki ownership untuk HAL (I2C dan SPI), sehingga modul lain tidak hardcode GPIO
//
// Catatan desain
// - Tipe i2c_port_t dan spi_host_device_t wajib ter-include eksplisit
// - Builder config hanya mengisi default cfg berbasis pin, tidak memulai driver
//

#pragma once
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>

#include "esp_err.h"
#include "driver/gpio.h"
#include "driver/i2c_master.h" 
#include "driver/spi_common.h" // slash: untuk spi_host_device_t

#include "nocti_hal_i2c.h"
#include "nocti_hal_spi.h"
#include "nocti_fnirs.h"       // slash: builder fnirs butuh nocti_fnirs_cfg_t

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

// ============================================================
// Identitas board
// ============================================================

#define NOCTI_BOARD_NAME   "Noctic_v1"

// ============================================================
// Pin map NoctiNIRS board Noctic_v1
// slash: pin mapping disentralisasi di board supaya modul lain tidak hardcode GPIO
// ============================================================

typedef struct {
    // I2C
    int i2c_sda; // default GPIO15
    int i2c_scl; // default GPIO16

    // ADS1299 SPI bus and control
    int ads1299_drdy;   // default GPIO10 (DRDY#)
    int ads1299_miso;   // default GPIO11
    int ads1299_sclk;   // default GPIO12
    int ads1299_cs;     // default GPIO13
    int ads1299_start;  // default GPIO14
    int ads1299_reset;  // default GPIO21
    int ads1299_pwdn;   // default GPIO47
    int ads1299_mosi;   // default GPIO48

    // ADS131M04 SPI bus and control
    int ads131_sync_reset; // default GPIO6 (SYNC#/RESET# digabung, pull up 10K ke 3.3V)
    int ads131_cs;         // default GPIO7 (pull up 10K ke 3.3V)
    int ads131_drdy;       // default GPIO35
    int ads131_sclk;       // default GPIO36
    int ads131_miso;       // default GPIO37
    int ads131_mosi;       // default GPIO38

    // Charger enable BQ24295
    int bq_ce; // default GPIO8 (CE pin BQ24295, default active low di datasheet)

    // PCA9956B reset
    int pca_reset; // default GPIO18 (aktif low)

    // USB native
    int usb_dp; // default GPIO20
    int usb_dm; // default GPIO19
} nocti_board_pins_t;

// ============================================================
// I2C addresses
// slash: gunakan 7 bit address untuk driver ESP IDF
// ============================================================

typedef struct {
    uint8_t pca9956b_7bit;   // default 0x01 (AD0 AD1 AD2 semua GND)
    uint8_t max17048_7bit;   // default 0x36 (alamat tipikal MAX17048 fuel gauge)
    uint8_t bq24295_7bit;    // default 0x6B (alamat datasheet BQ24295)
} nocti_board_i2c_addrs_t;

// ============================================================
// SPI policy (flexible)
// slash: board tidak mengunci clock dan mode, bisa reconfig saat idle
// ============================================================

typedef struct {
    uint32_t clock_hz;       // 0 berarti gunakan fallback board
    uint8_t  spi_mode;       // 0 sampai 3, 0xFF berarti gunakan fallback board
    bool     use_dma;        // default false
    int      input_delay_ns; // -1 berarti default 0 di HAL
    uint8_t  queue_size;     // 0 berarti fallback 1
} nocti_board_spi_policy_t;

// ============================================================
// Board config
// slash: host SPI tidak diasumsikan, wajib diisi eksplisit oleh pemanggil
// ============================================================

typedef struct {
    // I2C
    i2c_port_t i2c_port;              // I2C_NUM_0 atau I2C_NUM_1
    uint32_t   i2c_clk_hz;            // contoh 400000 atau 100000
    bool       i2c_enable_internal_pullups; // disarankan false kalau ada pullup eksternal

    // SPI host selection
    spi_host_device_t spi_host_ads1299; // wajib diisi
    spi_host_device_t spi_host_ads131;  // wajib diisi

    // Initial SPI policies (boleh diubah nanti via reconfig)
    nocti_board_spi_policy_t spi_ads1299;
    nocti_board_spi_policy_t spi_ads131;

    // Power path options
    bool charger_ce_active_high;
    // slash: false  berarti CE active low (seperti BQ24295 datasheet, enable charge jika CE low)
    // slash: true   berarti CE active high (dipakai jika di hardware ada inverter)

    // Addresses
    nocti_board_i2c_addrs_t i2c_addrs;
} nocti_board_cfg_t;

// ============================================================
// Board runtime handle
// slash: board memiliki ownership untuk HAL handle
// ============================================================

typedef struct {
    bool inited;

    nocti_board_pins_t pins;
    nocti_board_cfg_t  cfg;

    // HAL handles owned by board
    nocti_hal_i2c_t i2c;
    nocti_hal_spi_t spi_ads1299;
    nocti_hal_spi_t spi_ads131;
} nocti_board_t;

// ============================================================
// API
// ============================================================

// slash: helper agar cfg punya nilai aman sebelum init
void      nocti_board_cfg_set_defaults(nocti_board_cfg_t *cfg);

// Bring up dasar: GPIO + ISR + I2C + SPI
esp_err_t nocti_board_init(nocti_board_t *b, const nocti_board_cfg_t *cfg);
esp_err_t nocti_board_deinit(nocti_board_t *b);

// Bring up lengkap termasuk power path BQ24295
// slash: panggil ini dari app_main supaya
// slash: 1) board_init
// slash: 2) disable watchdog BQ24295
// slash: 3) enable charger via CE sesuai polarity cfg
esp_err_t nocti_board_init_all(nocti_board_t *b, nocti_board_cfg_t *cfg);

// Getters
const nocti_board_pins_t      *nocti_board_pins(const nocti_board_t *b);
const nocti_board_i2c_addrs_t *nocti_board_i2c_addrs(const nocti_board_t *b);
const nocti_board_cfg_t       *nocti_board_get_cfg(const nocti_board_t *b);

nocti_hal_i2c_t *nocti_board_i2c(nocti_board_t *b);
nocti_hal_spi_t *nocti_board_spi_ads1299(nocti_board_t *b);
nocti_hal_spi_t *nocti_board_spi_ads131(nocti_board_t *b);

// Board level controls
esp_err_t nocti_board_set_charger_enable(nocti_board_t *b, bool enable);

// SPI reconfig
// slash: panggil hanya saat idle, tidak ada transaksi SPI yang berjalan
// slash: reconfig dilakukan dengan deinit lalu init ulang device handle
esp_err_t nocti_board_spi_reconfig_ads1299(nocti_board_t *b, const nocti_board_spi_policy_t *p);
esp_err_t nocti_board_spi_reconfig_ads131(nocti_board_t *b, const nocti_board_spi_policy_t *p);

// Optional debug
esp_err_t nocti_board_i2c_probe_all(nocti_board_t *b, uint32_t timeout_ms);

// Debug khusus charger BQ24295
// slash: baca register 0x00 sampai 0x09 dan print ke log
esp_err_t nocti_board_bq24295_dump(nocti_board_t *b);

// ============================================================
// Builder config (preset pabrik)
// slash: builder hanya mengisi cfg default berbasis pin board, tidak start driver
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_board_build_fnirs_cfg(const nocti_board_t *b,
                                      nocti_fnirs_cfg_t *out_cfg,
                                      nocti_hal_spi_t *spi_ads131);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_board\nocti_board.c ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_board/nocti_board.c
// ============================================================
//
// Tujuan file ini
// - Bring up board Noctic_v1: init GPIO dasar, install ISR service, init HAL I2C dan SPI
// - Menyediakan getter untuk pin map dan handle HAL
// - Menyediakan reconfig SPI policy saat idle
// - Menyediakan init power path dasar untuk BQ24295 (matikan watchdog, enable charger)
//

#include "nocti_board.h"

#include <string.h>

#include "nocti_log.h"

static const char *TAG = "nocti_board";

// ============================================================
// Internal defaults
// ============================================================

static nocti_board_pins_t default_pins_noctic_v1(void)
{
    nocti_board_pins_t p;
    memset(&p, 0, sizeof(p));

    // I2C
    p.i2c_sda = 15;
    p.i2c_scl = 16;

    // ADS1299
    p.ads1299_drdy  = 10;
    p.ads1299_miso  = 11;
    p.ads1299_sclk  = 12;
    p.ads1299_cs    = 13;
    p.ads1299_start = 14;
    p.ads1299_reset = 21;
    p.ads1299_pwdn  = 47;
    p.ads1299_mosi  = 48;

    // ADS131M04
    p.ads131_sync_reset = 6;
    p.ads131_cs         = 7;
    p.ads131_drdy       = 35;
    p.ads131_sclk       = 36;
    p.ads131_miso       = 37;
    p.ads131_mosi       = 38;

    // Charger enable BQ24295
    p.bq_ce = 8;

    // PCA reset
    p.pca_reset = 18;

    // USB native
    p.usb_dp = 20;
    p.usb_dm = 19;

    return p;
}

static bool host_ok(spi_host_device_t h)
{
    return (h == SPI2_HOST) || (h == SPI3_HOST);
}

static nocti_board_spi_policy_t spi_policy_default(void)
{
    nocti_board_spi_policy_t p;
    memset(&p, 0, sizeof(p));
    p.clock_hz       = 0;      // slash: 0 berarti fallback board
    p.spi_mode       = 0xFFu;  // slash: 0xFF berarti fallback board
    p.use_dma        = false;
    p.input_delay_ns = -1;     // slash: -1 berarti fallback HAL (0 ns)
    p.queue_size     = 0;      // slash: 0 berarti fallback 1
    return p;
}

static uint8_t choose_spi_mode(uint8_t requested, uint8_t fallback)
{
    // slash: mode 0 sampai 3 valid, sentinel "unset" adalah 0xFF
    return (requested == 0xFFu) ? fallback : requested;
}

static uint32_t choose_spi_clock(uint32_t requested, uint32_t fallback)
{
    // slash: 0 berarti "unset"
    return (requested == 0u) ? fallback : requested;
}

static uint8_t choose_queue(uint8_t requested, uint8_t fallback)
{
    // slash: 0 berarti "unset"
    return (requested == 0u) ? fallback : requested;
}

// ============================================================
// GPIO init
// ============================================================

static esp_err_t init_gpios(const nocti_board_pins_t *p)
{
    if (!p) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    // slash: set hanya pin yang kita kontrol sebagai output
    int out_pins[] = {
        p->bq_ce,
        p->pca_reset,
        p->ads131_sync_reset,
        p->ads1299_start,
        p->ads1299_reset,
        p->ads1299_pwdn,
        p->ads1299_cs,
        p->ads131_cs,
    };

    uint64_t out_mask = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(out_pins) / sizeof(out_pins[0]); i++) {
        if (out_pins[i] >= 0) out_mask |= (1ULL << (uint32_t)out_pins[i]);
    }

    if (out_mask != 0) {
        gpio_config_t io = {0};
        io.intr_type    = GPIO_INTR_DISABLE;
        io.mode         = GPIO_MODE_OUTPUT;
        io.pin_bit_mask = out_mask;
        io.pull_down_en = GPIO_PULLDOWN_DISABLE;
        io.pull_up_en   = GPIO_PULLUP_DISABLE;

        esp_err_t err = gpio_config(&io);
        if (err != ESP_OK) return err;
    }

    // slash: input pins (DRDY). Driver biopot/fnirs akan set interrupt edge sesuai kebutuhan.
    int in_pins[] = {
        p->ads1299_drdy,
        p->ads131_drdy,
    };

    uint64_t in_mask = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(in_pins) / sizeof(in_pins[0]); i++) {
        if (in_pins[i] >= 0) in_mask |= (1ULL << (uint32_t)in_pins[i]);
    }

    if (in_mask != 0) {
        gpio_config_t ii = {0};
        ii.intr_type    = GPIO_INTR_DISABLE;
        ii.mode         = GPIO_MODE_INPUT;
        ii.pin_bit_mask = in_mask;
        ii.pull_down_en = GPIO_PULLDOWN_DISABLE;
        ii.pull_up_en   = GPIO_PULLUP_DISABLE;

        esp_err_t err = gpio_config(&ii);
        if (err != ESP_OK) return err;
    }

    // Default output states
    // slash: guard pin supaya aman kalau suatu saat dibuat -1
    if (p->ads1299_cs >= 0) (void)gpio_set_level(p->ads1299_cs, 1); // slash: CS idle high
    if (p->ads131_cs  >= 0) (void)gpio_set_level(p->ads131_cs, 1);

    if (p->ads131_sync_reset >= 0) (void)gpio_set_level(p->ads131_sync_reset, 1); // slash: aktif low, jadi idle high

    if (p->ads1299_start >= 0) (void)gpio_set_level(p->ads1299_start, 0);
    if (p->ads1299_reset >= 0) (void)gpio_set_level(p->ads1299_reset, 1);
    if (p->ads1299_pwdn  >= 0) (void)gpio_set_level(p->ads1299_pwdn, 1);

    if (p->pca_reset >= 0) (void)gpio_set_level(p->pca_reset, 1); // slash: aktif low, idle high

    // slash: CE default kita set high, artinya "charger off" kalau CE active low
    // slash: nanti nocti_board_init_all akan panggil nocti_board_set_charger_enable(true)
    if (p->bq_ce >= 0) (void)gpio_set_level(p->bq_ce, 1);

    return ESP_OK;
}

// ============================================================
// HAL init
// ============================================================

static esp_err_t init_i2c(nocti_board_t *b)
{
    nocti_hal_i2c_cfg_t i2c_cfg = {
        .i2c_port               = b->cfg.i2c_port,
        .gpio_sda               = b->pins.i2c_sda,
        .gpio_scl               = b->pins.i2c_scl,
        .clk_speed_hz           = b->cfg.i2c_clk_hz,
        .enable_internal_pullups = b->cfg.i2c_enable_internal_pullups,
        .delete_driver_on_deinit = true, // slash: board owns driver lifetime
    };

    return nocti_hal_i2c_init(&b->i2c, &i2c_cfg);
}

static esp_err_t init_spi_ads1299(nocti_board_t *b, const nocti_board_spi_policy_t *p)
{
    // slash: fallback ini hanya agar init jalan, bisa diubah via reconfig saat idle
    const uint8_t  fallback_mode = 1;
    const uint32_t fallback_clk  = 2000000u;

    nocti_hal_spi_cfg_t c = {
        .host   = b->cfg.spi_host_ads1299,
        .gpio_mosi = b->pins.ads1299_mosi,
        .gpio_miso = b->pins.ads1299_miso,
        .gpio_sclk = b->pins.ads1299_sclk,
        .gpio_cs   = b->pins.ads1299_cs,

        .clock_hz  = choose_spi_clock(p->clock_hz, fallback_clk),
        .spi_mode  = choose_spi_mode(p->spi_mode, fallback_mode),
        .queue_size = choose_queue(p->queue_size, 1),

        .use_dma        = p->use_dma,
        .input_delay_ns = p->input_delay_ns,
        .free_bus_on_deinit = true,
    };

    return nocti_hal_spi_init(&b->spi_ads1299, &c);
}

static esp_err_t init_spi_ads131(nocti_board_t *b, const nocti_board_spi_policy_t *p)
{
    const uint8_t  fallback_mode = 1;
    const uint32_t fallback_clk  = 2000000u;

    nocti_hal_spi_cfg_t c = {
        .host   = b->cfg.spi_host_ads131,
        .gpio_mosi = b->pins.ads131_mosi,
        .gpio_miso = b->pins.ads131_miso,
        .gpio_sclk = b->pins.ads131_sclk,
        .gpio_cs   = b->pins.ads131_cs,

        .clock_hz  = choose_spi_clock(p->clock_hz, fallback_clk),
        .spi_mode  = choose_spi_mode(p->spi_mode, fallback_mode),
        .queue_size = choose_queue(p->queue_size, 1),

        .use_dma        = p->use_dma,
        .input_delay_ns = p->input_delay_ns,
        .free_bus_on_deinit = true,
    };

    return nocti_hal_spi_init(&b->spi_ads131, &c);
}

// ============================================================
// BQ24295 basic init (internal)
// ============================================================
//
// Target konfigurasi awal:
// - Matikan I2C watchdog supaya host tidak wajib kick tiap 40 detik
//   Data sheet: REG05[5:4] = 00 berarti watchdog disable.
//
// Slash: kita tidak utak atik limit arus, safety timer, SYS_MIN, dll di sini.
//        Fokus dulu ke hal yang bisa ganggu firmware (watchdog).
//

static esp_err_t init_bq24295_basic(nocti_board_t *b)
{
    if (!b || !b->inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

    uint8_t addr = b->cfg.i2c_addrs.bq24295_7bit;
    if (addr == 0) {
        // slash: tidak ada alamat, anggap charger tidak dipakai
        NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "bq24295: i2c addr 0, skip charger init");
        return ESP_OK;
    }

    uint8_t reg5 = 0;
    esp_err_t err = nocti_hal_i2c_read_u8(&b->i2c, addr, 0x05, &reg5, 50);
    if (err != ESP_OK) {
        NOCTI_LOGW(TAG, "bq24295: read REG05 failed: %s", esp_err_to_name(err));
        return err;
    }

    // slash: REG05[5:4] adalah WATCHDOG, 00 disable, 01 40 detik, 10 80, 11 160
    uint8_t new5 = reg5 & (uint8_t)~(uint8_t)(0x3u << 4); // clear bit 5 dan 4

    if (new5 != reg5) {
        err = nocti_hal_i2c_write_u8(&b->i2c, addr, 0x05, new5, 50);
        if (err != ESP_OK) {
            NOCTI_LOGW(TAG, "bq24295: write REG05 failed: %s", esp_err_to_name(err));
            return err;
        }
        NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "bq24295: REG05 0x%02X -> 0x%02X (watchdog disabled)", reg5, new5);
    } else {
        NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "bq24295: REG05 already 0x%02X (watchdog disabled)", reg5);
    }

    return ESP_OK;
}

// ============================================================
// Public helper
// ============================================================

void nocti_board_cfg_set_defaults(nocti_board_cfg_t *cfg)
{
    if (!cfg) return;

    memset(cfg, 0, sizeof(*cfg));

    // I2C
    cfg->i2c_port                   = I2C_NUM_0;
    cfg->i2c_clk_hz                 = 400000;
    cfg->i2c_enable_internal_pullups = false;

    // SPI host wajib diisi pemanggil (diset 0 untuk memaksa validasi di init)
    cfg->spi_host_ads1299 = (spi_host_device_t)0;
    cfg->spi_host_ads131  = (spi_host_device_t)0;

    // SPI policy default netral
    cfg->spi_ads1299 = spi_policy_default();
    cfg->spi_ads131  = spi_policy_default();

    // Power path
    cfg->charger_ce_active_high = false; // slash: default ikut datasheet BQ24295 (CE active low)

    // I2C address default
    cfg->i2c_addrs.pca9956b_7bit = 0x01u;
    cfg->i2c_addrs.max17048_7bit = 0x36u;
    cfg->i2c_addrs.bq24295_7bit  = 0x6Bu;
}

// ============================================================
// Public
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_board_init(nocti_board_t *b, const nocti_board_cfg_t *cfg)
{
    if (!b || !cfg) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    // slash: host SPI wajib diset eksplisit
    if (!host_ok(cfg->spi_host_ads1299) || !host_ok(cfg->spi_host_ads131)) {
        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    }

    memset(b, 0, sizeof(*b));
    b->pins = default_pins_noctic_v1();
    b->cfg  = *cfg;

    // slash: kalau pemanggil lupa isi address, isi default aman
    if (b->cfg.i2c_addrs.pca9956b_7bit == 0) {
        b->cfg.i2c_addrs.pca9956b_7bit = 0x01u;
    }
    if (b->cfg.i2c_addrs.max17048_7bit == 0) {
        b->cfg.i2c_addrs.max17048_7bit = 0x36u;
    }
    if (b->cfg.i2c_addrs.bq24295_7bit == 0) {
        b->cfg.i2c_addrs.bq24295_7bit = 0x6Bu;
    }

    esp_err_t err = init_gpios(&b->pins);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    // slash: ISR service harus sekali saja, modul fnirs dan biopot akan add handler setelah ini
    err = gpio_install_isr_service(0);
    if (err == ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE) {
        err = ESP_OK; // slash: service sudah terpasang, itu aman
    }
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    err = init_i2c(b);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    // slash: SPI di init dengan policy awal, bisa diganti saat idle via API reconfig
    err = init_spi_ads1299(b, &b->cfg.spi_ads1299);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    err = init_spi_ads131(b, &b->cfg.spi_ads131);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    b->inited = true;

    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "board inited: %s", NOCTI_BOARD_NAME);
    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t nocti_board_deinit(nocti_board_t *b)
{
    if (!b) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!b->inited) return ESP_OK;

    (void)nocti_hal_spi_deinit(&b->spi_ads131);
    (void)nocti_hal_spi_deinit(&b->spi_ads1299);
    (void)nocti_hal_i2c_deinit(&b->i2c);

    // slash: gpio ISR service tidak dihapus di sini, biar tidak ganggu modul lain
    b->inited = false;
    return ESP_OK;
}

// ============================================================
// Init lengkap: board + charger
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_board_init_all(nocti_board_t *b, nocti_board_cfg_t *cfg)
{
    if (!b || !cfg) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    esp_err_t err = nocti_board_init(b, cfg);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    // slash: matikan watchdog BQ supaya firmware tidak wajib kick terus
    err = init_bq24295_basic(b);
    if (err != ESP_OK) {
        // slash: jangan bikin boot gagal hanya karena charger, cukup warning
        NOCTI_LOGW(TAG, "bq24295 basic init error: %s", esp_err_to_name(err));
    }

    // slash: setelah konfigurasi dasar, enable charger lewat CE
    (void)nocti_board_set_charger_enable(b, true);

    return ESP_OK;
}

// ============================================================
// Getters
// ============================================================

const nocti_board_pins_t *nocti_board_pins(const nocti_board_t *b)
{
    if (!b) return NULL;
    return &b->pins;
}

const nocti_board_i2c_addrs_t *nocti_board_i2c_addrs(const nocti_board_t *b)
{
    if (!b) return NULL;
    return &b->cfg.i2c_addrs;
}

const nocti_board_cfg_t *nocti_board_get_cfg(const nocti_board_t *b)
{
    if (!b) return NULL;
    return &b->cfg;
}

nocti_hal_i2c_t *nocti_board_i2c(nocti_board_t *b)
{
    if (!b) return NULL;
    return &b->i2c;
}

nocti_hal_spi_t *nocti_board_spi_ads1299(nocti_board_t *b)
{
    if (!b) return NULL;
    return &b->spi_ads1299;
}

nocti_hal_spi_t *nocti_board_spi_ads131(nocti_board_t *b)
{
    if (!b) return NULL;
    return &b->spi_ads131;
}

// ============================================================
// Board level controls
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_board_set_charger_enable(nocti_board_t *b, bool enable)
{
    if (!b || !b->inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
    if (b->pins.bq_ce < 0) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

    // slash: interpretasi enable ke level CE tergantung polarity di cfg
    // slash: default BQ24295: CE active low, jadi enable berarti CE low
    bool active_high = b->cfg.charger_ce_active_high;

    int level;
    if (enable) {
        level = active_high ? 1 : 0;
    } else {
        level = active_high ? 0 : 1;
    }

    return gpio_set_level(b->pins.bq_ce, level);
}

// ============================================================
// SPI reconfig
// ============================================================
// slash: panggil hanya saat idle, tidak ada transaksi SPI yang berjalan
// slash: reconfig dengan cara deinit lalu init ulang device handle
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_board_spi_reconfig_ads1299(nocti_board_t *b, const nocti_board_spi_policy_t *p)
{
    if (!b || !b->inited || !p) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    // slash: simpan policy baru untuk konsistensi debug
    b->cfg.spi_ads1299 = *p;

    (void)nocti_hal_spi_deinit(&b->spi_ads1299);
    return init_spi_ads1299(b, &b->cfg.spi_ads1299);
}

esp_err_t nocti_board_spi_reconfig_ads131(nocti_board_t *b, const nocti_board_spi_policy_t *p)
{
    if (!b || !b->inited || !p) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    b->cfg.spi_ads131 = *p;

    (void)nocti_hal_spi_deinit(&b->spi_ads131);
    return init_spi_ads131(b, &b->cfg.spi_ads131);
}

// ============================================================
// Optional debug
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_board_i2c_probe_all(nocti_board_t *b, uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    if (!b || !b->inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

    for (uint8_t a = 1; a < 0x7F; a++) {
        esp_err_t err = nocti_hal_i2c_probe(&b->i2c, a, timeout_ms);
        if (err == ESP_OK) {
            NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "i2c ack: 0x%02X", (unsigned)a);
        }
    }
    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t nocti_board_bq24295_dump(nocti_board_t *b)
{
    if (!b || !b->inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

    uint8_t addr = b->cfg.i2c_addrs.bq24295_7bit;
    if (addr == 0) {
        NOCTI_LOGW(TAG, "bq24295_dump: addr 0, skip");
        return ESP_OK;
    }

    for (uint8_t reg = 0; reg <= 0x09; reg++) {
        uint8_t val = 0;
        esp_err_t err = nocti_hal_i2c_read_u8(&b->i2c, addr, reg, &val, 50);
        if (err != ESP_OK) {
            NOCTI_LOGW(TAG, "bq24295_dump: read reg 0x%02X failed: %s", reg, esp_err_to_name(err));
            return err;
        }
        NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "bq24295 REG0x%02X = 0x%02X", reg, val);
    }

    return ESP_OK;
}

// ============================================================
// Builder config
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_board_build_fnirs_cfg(const nocti_board_t *b,
                                      nocti_fnirs_cfg_t *out_cfg,
                                      nocti_hal_spi_t *spi_ads131)
{
    if (!b || !b->inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
    if (!out_cfg || !spi_ads131) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    memset(out_cfg, 0, sizeof(*out_cfg));

    // slash: SPI handle di inject, supaya builder tidak asumsi ownership
    out_cfg->spi = spi_ads131;

    // GPIO
    out_cfg->gpio_drdy = b->pins.ads131_drdy;

    // slash: ADS131M04 pakai 1 pin gabungan SYNC dan RESET (aktif low)
    // slash: jadi kedua field cfg menunjuk ke pin yang sama
    out_cfg->gpio_reset = b->pins.ads131_sync_reset;
    out_cfg->gpio_sync  = b->pins.ads131_sync_reset;

    // DRDY timeout:
    // Timeout yang terlalu kecil mudah bikin ESP_ERR_TIMEOUT walau ADC sebenarnya jalan,
    // karena task bisa miss edge DRDY (interrupt latency, scheduling).
    // Stand-alone bring-up menunggu DRDY sampai ratusan ms.
    // Untuk path library, set lebih longgar.
    out_cfg->drdy_timeout_us = 20000; // 20 ms
    out_cfg->spi_timeout_ms  = 20;

    // Queue internal default
    // slash: ini hanya buffer, bukan storage permanen
    out_cfg->queue_len = 64;

    // Register init:
    // Untuk menyamakan dengan stand-alone (yang sudah terbukti bisa baca frame tanpa
    // setting register), defaultnya kita OFF dulu. Kalau nanti perlu data-rate/gain,
    // baru diaktifkan.
    out_cfg->reg_mode  = 0;
    out_cfg->reg_clock = 0;
    out_cfg->reg_gain  = 0;

    out_cfg->do_adc_register_init = false;

    return ESP_OK;
}

// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_common\include\nocti_crc.h ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_common/include/nocti_crc.h
// ============================================================
//
// Tujuan file ini
// - CRC untuk menjaga integritas packet di transport stream (BLE) dan storage
//
// Catatan
// - Kita pakai CRC16-CCITT-FALSE yang umum dan ringan
// - Implementasi ada di nocti_crc.c

#pragma once

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

// CRC16-CCITT-FALSE
// - poly: 0x1021
// - init: 0xFFFF
// - xorout: 0x0000
// - refin: false, refout: false
uint16_t nocti_crc16_ccitt_false(const void *data, size_t len);

// Helper untuk update CRC dengan chunk bertahap
uint16_t nocti_crc16_ccitt_false_update(uint16_t crc, const void *data, size_t len);

// Nilai init standar
static inline uint16_t nocti_crc16_init(void)
{
    return (uint16_t)0xFFFFu;           // slash: init CRC16-CCITT-FALSE
}

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_common\include\nocti_err.h ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_common/include/nocti_err.h
// ============================================================
//
// Tujuan file ini
// - Menyatukan model error dan result NoctiNIRS
// - Menghindari tiap modul bikin enum error sendiri-sendiri
//
// Prinsip desain
// - Ringkas
// - Bisa dipakai tanpa ESP-IDF
// - Bisa di-map ke esp_err_t jika diperlukan

#pragma once

#include <stdint.h>

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Result code umum
// ------------------------------------------------------------

typedef enum {
    NOCTI_OK = 0,                 // slash: operasi sukses
    NOCTI_ERR_GENERIC = -1,       // slash: error tidak spesifik
    NOCTI_ERR_INVALID_ARG = -2,   // slash: argumen tidak valid
    NOCTI_ERR_TIMEOUT = -3,       // slash: timeout menunggu event
    NOCTI_ERR_NO_MEM = -4,        // slash: kehabisan memori atau buffer
    NOCTI_ERR_BUSY = -5,          // slash: resource sedang dipakai
    NOCTI_ERR_NOT_READY = -6,     // slash: modul belum siap
    NOCTI_ERR_IO = -7,            // slash: error I/O (SPI, I2C, dll)
    NOCTI_ERR_CRC = -8,           // slash: CRC mismatch
    NOCTI_ERR_OVERFLOW = -9,      // slash: buffer overflow / data drop
    NOCTI_ERR_UNSUPPORTED = -10,  // slash: fitur tidak didukung
} nocti_err_t;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Helper macro sederhana
// ------------------------------------------------------------

// Cek result dan langsung return jika error
#define NOCTI_RETURN_ON_ERR(expr)        \
    do {                                 \
        nocti_err_t __e = (expr);        \
        if (__e != NOCTI_OK) {           \
            return __e;                  \
        }                                \
    } while (0)

// Boolean helper
#define NOCTI_SUCCEEDED(e) ((e) == NOCTI_OK)
#define NOCTI_FAILED(e)    ((e) != NOCTI_OK)

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_common\include\nocti_log.h ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_common/include/nocti_log.h

//
// Tujuan file ini

// Catatan desain

// Cara pakai

#pragma once
