#endif

#else

// slash: log dimatikan
#define NOCTI_LOGI(tag, fmt, ...) do { (void)(tag); } while (0)
#define NOCTI_LOGW(tag, fmt, ...) do { (void)(tag); } while (0)
#define NOCTI_LOGE(tag, fmt, ...) do { (void)(tag); } while (0)

#endif


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_common\include\nocti_packet.h ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_common/include/nocti_packet.h
// ============================================================
//
// Tujuan file ini
// - Mendefinisikan "envelope" universal untuk semua data NoctiNIRS
// - Dipakai bersama oleh BLE dan storage, supaya replay mudah
//
// Prinsip
// - 1 packet = 1 record (fnirs atau biopot atau status atau event)
// - Timestamp utama pakai t_rec_us (reset tiap START)
// - pkt_seq naik tiap packet untuk deteksi loss

#pragma once

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>

#include "nocti_types.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

// Magic untuk mengenali packet NoctiNIRS di stream byte
#define NOCTI_PKT_MAGIC  (0x4E4F4354u)   // slash: 'NOCT' dalam ASCII

// Versi format packet, beda dari versi firmware
#define NOCTI_PKT_FMT_VER (1u)           // slash: naik jika struktur header berubah

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Jenis pesan
// ------------------------------------------------------------

typedef enum {
    NOCTI_MSG_INVALID = 0,

    // Data utama
    NOCTI_MSG_FNIRS_OD = 1,              // slash: payload berisi OD fixed point
    NOCTI_MSG_BIOPOT_UV = 2,             // slash: payload berisi microvolt per channel

    // Status dan info
    NOCTI_MSG_STATUS = 10,               // slash: payload berisi nocti_device_status_t ringkas
    NOCTI_MSG_INFO = 11,                 // slash: payload info device, versi, konfigurasi

    // Control plane
    NOCTI_MSG_CMD = 20,                  // slash: host ke device (START, STOP, dsb)
    NOCTI_MSG_ACK = 21,                  // slash: device ke host sebagai balasan command

    // Event
    NOCTI_MSG_EVENT = 30,                // slash: event seperti disconnect, overflow, fault
} nocti_msg_type_t;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Flag header (bitmask)
// ------------------------------------------------------------

typedef enum {
    NOCTI_PF_CRC_PRESENT = (1u << 0),    // slash: crc16 field valid
    NOCTI_PF_ENDIAN_LE   = (1u << 1),    // slash: semua integer little-endian (target kita)
} nocti_pkt_flags_t;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Header packet
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Catatan packing
// - Kita buat header fixed size agar parsing cepat
// - Payload mengikuti setelah header
// - CRC16 opsional ditempatkan di akhir payload (lebih fleksibel)

#pragma pack(push, 1)
typedef struct {
    uint32_t        magic;              // slash: NOCTI_PKT_MAGIC
    uint8_t         fmt_ver;            // slash: NOCTI_PKT_FMT_VER
    uint8_t         msg_type;           // slash: nocti_msg_type_t
    uint16_t        flags;              // slash: nocti_pkt_flags_t

    nocti_session_id_t session_id;      // slash: naik tiap START recording
    nocti_pkt_seq_t    pkt_seq;         // slash: naik tiap packet global
    nocti_time_us_t    t_rec_us;         // slash: timestamp sejak recording start

    uint16_t        payload_len;        // slash: panjang payload dalam byte
    uint16_t        header_len;         // slash: panjang header ini, untuk forward compatibility
} nocti_pkt_hdr_t;
#pragma pack(pop)

// Helper untuk isi header default
static inline void nocti_pkt_hdr_init(nocti_pkt_hdr_t *h)
{
    if (!h) return;
    h->magic = NOCTI_PKT_MAGIC;
    h->fmt_ver = (uint8_t)NOCTI_PKT_FMT_VER;
    h->msg_type = (uint8_t)NOCTI_MSG_INVALID;
    h->flags = (uint16_t)NOCTI_PF_ENDIAN_LE;
    h->session_id = 0;
    h->pkt_seq = 0;
    h->t_rec_us = 0;
    h->payload_len = 0;
    h->header_len = (uint16_t)sizeof(nocti_pkt_hdr_t);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Payload minimal yang sering dipakai
// ------------------------------------------------------------

// Command id
typedef enum {
    NOCTI_CMD_NOP = 0,                  // slash: no-op untuk test
    NOCTI_CMD_START = 1,                // slash: mulai recording, reset t_rec_us ke 0
    NOCTI_CMD_STOP = 2,                 // slash: stop recording
    NOCTI_CMD_INFO = 3,                 // slash: minta info device
    NOCTI_CMD_PING = 4,                 // slash: cek koneksi
    NOCTI_CMD_SET_CFG = 5,              // slash: set config ringan (interval status, dsb)
} nocti_cmd_id_t;

// Payload CMD
#pragma pack(push, 1)
typedef struct {
    uint16_t cmd_id;                    // slash: nocti_cmd_id_t
    uint16_t cmd_seq;                   // slash: urutan command dari host, untuk matching ACK
    uint32_t arg0;                      // slash: argumen generik, arti tergantung cmd_id
    uint32_t arg1;                      // slash: argumen generik
} nocti_cmd_t;
#pragma pack(pop)

// Payload ACK
#pragma pack(push, 1)
typedef struct {
    uint16_t cmd_id;                    // slash: command yang dibalas
    uint16_t cmd_seq;                   // slash: harus sama dengan cmd_seq dari host
    int32_t  result;                    // slash: nocti_err_t atau hasil operasi
    uint32_t info0;                     // slash: info tambahan (misal session_id baru)
} nocti_ack_t;
#pragma pack(pop)

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_common\include\nocti_ringbuf.h ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_common/include/nocti_ringbuf.h
// ============================================================
//
// Tujuan file ini
// - Ring buffer byte oriented untuk buffering ringan yang deterministik (tanpa malloc)
// - Cocok untuk util internal: parser, queue byte kecil, bridging antar modul sederhana
// - Catatan: nocti_stream memakai FreeRTOS Ringbuffer (xRingbufferCreate), bukan modul ini
//
// Catatan desain
// - Single-producer single-consumer (SPSC) tanpa mutex
// - Paling aman jika producer dan consumer berada di core yang sama
// - Jika beda core, pin task ke core yang sama atau tambahkan proteksi di level atas
//
// Batasan
// - Ini ring buffer byte stream, tidak ada batas record (record boundary)
// - Kalau butuh record boundary, pakai FreeRTOS Ringbuffer atau framing di level atas


#pragma once

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdbool.h>

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

typedef struct {
    uint8_t *buf;   // slash: pointer ke storage eksternal
    size_t   cap;   // slash: kapasitas total dalam byte
    size_t   head;  // slash: index tulis berikutnya
    size_t   tail;  // slash: index baca berikutnya
    bool     full;  // slash: pembeda full ketika head == tail
} nocti_ringbuf_t;

// ============================================================
// Init and reset
// ============================================================

// Init ring buffer dengan buffer eksternal
// return true jika sukses
bool   nocti_ringbuf_init(nocti_ringbuf_t *rb, uint8_t *storage, size_t capacity);

// Reset ring buffer (mengosongkan isi)
void   nocti_ringbuf_reset(nocti_ringbuf_t *rb);

// ============================================================
// Stats
// ============================================================

size_t nocti_ringbuf_capacity(const nocti_ringbuf_t *rb);
size_t nocti_ringbuf_size(const nocti_ringbuf_t *rb);
size_t nocti_ringbuf_free(const nocti_ringbuf_t *rb);

bool   nocti_ringbuf_empty(const nocti_ringbuf_t *rb);
bool   nocti_ringbuf_full(const nocti_ringbuf_t *rb);

// ============================================================
// IO basic
// ============================================================

// Tulis data ke ring buffer
// return jumlah byte yang berhasil ditulis (bisa < len jika tidak cukup ruang)
size_t nocti_ringbuf_write(nocti_ringbuf_t *rb, const void *data, size_t len);

// Tulis data harus utuh, kalau tidak cukup ruang maka gagal
// return true jika semua byte berhasil ditulis
bool   nocti_ringbuf_write_all(nocti_ringbuf_t *rb, const void *data, size_t len);

// Baca data dari ring buffer
// return jumlah byte yang berhasil dibaca
size_t nocti_ringbuf_read(nocti_ringbuf_t *rb, void *out, size_t len);

// Peek data tanpa menggeser tail
// return jumlah byte yang berhasil di-copy ke out
size_t nocti_ringbuf_peek(const nocti_ringbuf_t *rb, void *out, size_t len);

// ============================================================
// Optional helpers (berguna untuk streaming realtime)
// ============================================================

// Tulis data, kalau tidak cukup ruang maka drop data paling lama sampai cukup
// return jumlah byte yang berhasil ditulis (biasanya == len kalau rb->cap memadai)
// slash: ini cocok untuk producer realtime, daripada gagal terus saat buffer penuh
size_t nocti_ringbuf_write_drop_oldest(nocti_ringbuf_t *rb, const void *data, size_t len);

// Drop (buang) N byte dari sisi tail, tanpa membaca
// return jumlah byte yang benar benar ter-drop
size_t nocti_ringbuf_drop(nocti_ringbuf_t *rb, size_t len);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_common\include\nocti_status.h ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_common/include/nocti_status.h
// ============================================================
//
// Tujuan file ini
// - Menyatukan format status device, terutama battery
// - Status ini boleh dipush periodik via BLE atau dipull oleh host
//
// Catatan desain
// - Hanya "format data", bukan cara membaca hardware battery

#pragma once

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>

#include "nocti_types.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Battery status
// ------------------------------------------------------------

typedef struct {
    uint16_t        mv;            // slash: tegangan baterai dalam millivolt
    uint8_t         percent;       // slash: 0-100 estimasi SoC
    bool            is_charging;   // slash: true jika sedang charge
    bool            is_valid;      // slash: false jika sensor belum siap atau nilai tidak dipercaya
    nocti_time_us_t t_rec_us;      // slash: timestamp recording time saat status dibuat
} nocti_battery_status_t;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Device status flags (bitmask)
// ------------------------------------------------------------

typedef enum {
    NOCTI_STATF_STREAMING       = (1u << 0),  // slash: sedang streaming data
    NOCTI_STATF_BLE_CONNECTED   = (1u << 1),  // slash: BLE link aktif
    NOCTI_STATF_LOW_BATT        = (1u << 2),  // slash: baterai rendah
    NOCTI_STATF_OVERFLOW        = (1u << 3),  // slash: buffer overflow / data drop
    NOCTI_STATF_OVERTEMP        = (1u << 4),  // slash: over temperature terdeteksi
    NOCTI_STATF_SENSOR_FAULT    = (1u << 5),  // slash: fault sensor (fnirs/biopot) terdeteksi
    NOCTI_STATF_STORAGE_FAULT   = (1u << 6),  // slash: fault terkait storage/logging
} nocti_status_flags_t;

// Snapshot status device
typedef struct {
    nocti_battery_status_t battery;   // slash: status baterai terbaru
    uint32_t               flags;     // slash: gabungan nocti_status_flags_t
    nocti_time_us_t        t_rec_us;  // slash: timestamp snapshot dibuat
} nocti_device_status_t;

// Helper set/clear flag biar tidak typo
static inline void nocti_status_set_flag(nocti_device_status_t *st, uint32_t flag, bool on)
{
    if (!st) return;
    if (on) st->flags |= flag;
    else    st->flags &= ~flag;
}

static inline bool nocti_status_has_flag(const nocti_device_status_t *st, uint32_t flag)
{
    if (!st) return false;
    return ((st->flags & flag) != 0u);
}

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_common\include\nocti_time.h ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_common/include/nocti_time.h
// ============================================================
//
// Tujuan file ini
// - Mengunci konsep dua waktu: t_boot_us dan t_rec_us
// - Menyediakan state kecil untuk mengubah "boot time" menjadi "recording time"
//
// Catatan desain
// - Tidak memanggil nocti_timebase di sini
// - Modul lain boleh mengisi now_boot_us dari mana saja (misal timebase now_us)

#pragma once

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>

#include "nocti_types.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

// State konversi waktu untuk sesi recording
typedef struct {
    bool            rec_active;         // slash: true jika recording sedang berjalan
    nocti_time_us_t rec_start_boot_us;   // slash: t_boot_us saat START, dipakai sebagai nol sesi
    nocti_time_us_t last_boot_us;        // slash: debug atau validasi monotonik
} nocti_time_state_t;

// Init state waktu
static inline void nocti_time_init(nocti_time_state_t *st)
{
    if (!st) return;
    st->rec_active = false;
    st->rec_start_boot_us = 0;
    st->last_boot_us = 0;
}

// Dipanggil saat START recording
static inline void nocti_time_on_rec_start(nocti_time_state_t *st, nocti_time_us_t now_boot_us)
{
    if (!st) return;
    st->rec_active = true;
    st->rec_start_boot_us = now_boot_us;
    st->last_boot_us = now_boot_us;
}

// Dipanggil saat STOP recording
static inline void nocti_time_on_rec_stop(nocti_time_state_t *st, nocti_time_us_t now_boot_us)
{
    if (!st) return;
    st->rec_active = false;
    st->last_boot_us = now_boot_us;
}

// Hitung t_rec_us dari now_boot_us
// - Jika belum recording, return 0
static inline nocti_time_us_t nocti_time_rec_us(const nocti_time_state_t *st, nocti_time_us_t now_boot_us)
{
    if (!st) return 0;
    if (!st->rec_active) return 0;
    return (now_boot_us - st->rec_start_boot_us);
}

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_common\include\nocti_types.h ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_common/include/nocti_types.h
// ============================================================
//
// Tujuan file ini
// - Menjadi "bahasa dasar" NoctiNIRS: semua tipe data inti ditetapkan di sini
// - Dipakai lintas modul (fnirs, biopot, ble, storage) agar format konsisten
//
// Aturan desain
// - Tidak bergantung ke driver ESP-IDF
// - Hanya pakai tipe standar C (stdint, stdbool)
// - Semua timestamp dan counter pakai integer agar deterministik

#pragma once

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Waktu dan counter global
// ------------------------------------------------------------

// Timestamp mikrodetik
// - t_boot_us: monoton sejak boot (tidak reset)
// - t_rec_us : sejak START recording (reset ke 0 setiap sesi)
typedef int64_t  nocti_time_us_t;      // slash: signed supaya aman untuk selisih waktu (delta) yang bisa negatif
typedef uint32_t nocti_session_id_t;   // slash: id sesi recording, naik setiap START
typedef uint32_t nocti_pkt_seq_t;      // slash: urutan packet global, naik setiap packet terkirim
typedef uint32_t nocti_sample_id_t;    // slash: urutan sample per modality (fnirs, biopot, dsb)

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Unit data sains utama
// ------------------------------------------------------------

// fNIRS Optical Density (OD)
// Kita pakai fixed point agar hemat bandwidth dan konsisten antar platform
// od_u1e6 = OD * 1,000,000
typedef int32_t nocti_od_u1e6_t;       // slash: OD bisa negatif setelah detrend atau koreksi, jadi signed

// Biopotential microvolt
// uV = mikrovolt (1e-6 V)
typedef int32_t nocti_uV_t;            // slash: sinyal EEG, EOG, EMG bisa positif dan negatif

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Kanal dan label sederhana
// ------------------------------------------------------------

// Wavelength fNIRS, sesuai sistem kamu: 735 dan 850 nm
typedef enum {
    NOCTI_WL_735 = 0,
    NOCTI_WL_850 = 1,
} nocti_wavelength_t;

// Modality id untuk menandai sumber data
typedef enum {
    NOCTI_MOD_FNIRS  = 0,             // slash: fNIRS OD samples
    NOCTI_MOD_BIOPOT = 1,             // slash: ADS1299 uV samples
    NOCTI_MOD_STATUS = 2,             // slash: status seperti battery, suhu, flag
    NOCTI_MOD_EVENT  = 3,             // slash: event log (disconnect, overflow, dll)
} nocti_modality_t;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Skala dan helper fixed point
// ------------------------------------------------------------

// Konstanta skala OD
#define NOCTI_OD_SCALE_U1E6  (1000000) // slash: OD * 1e6 disimpan sebagai int32

// Convert float ke fixed point OD, hanya dipakai kalau memang ada float di modul tertentu
// Catatan: ini macro sederhana, pastikan input sudah dalam range yang aman
#define NOCTI_OD_TO_U1E6(od_float) ((nocti_od_u1e6_t)((od_float) * (double)NOCTI_OD_SCALE_U1E6))

// Convert fixed point OD ke double
#define NOCTI_OD_FROM_U1E6(od_u1e6) ((double)(od_u1e6) / (double)NOCTI_OD_SCALE_U1E6)

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Batas aman umum (opsional, bisa dipakai modul lain)
// ------------------------------------------------------------

// Untuk menghindari magic number di banyak tempat
#define NOCTI_MAX_FNIRS_PD_CH        (4)   // slash: ADS131M04 bisa mapping PD CH0-CH3
#define NOCTI_MAX_FNIRS_SOURCES      (10)  // slash: 10 sumber fisik yang kamu pakai
#define NOCTI_MAX_FNIRS_WAVELENGTHS  (2)   // slash: 735 dan 850

// ============================================================
// Stream payload structs (v1)
// // belajar: stream hanya membungkus payload, jadi format payload harus stabil
// // belajar: semua struct di bawah ini dipakai sebagai payload untuk nocti_stream_push
//
// Konvensi endian
// - ESP32 adalah little endian
// - Payload struct ini dikirim apa adanya (binary)
// - Decoder di PC harus parse sebagai little endian
// ============================================================

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Payload: fNIRS record v1 (NOCTI_REC_FNIRS)
// ------------------------------------------------------------

#define NOCTI_PAYLOAD_FNIRS_V1_VERSION      (1u)

typedef enum {
    NOCTI_FNIRS_F_NONE          = 0x00,
    NOCTI_FNIRS_F_UNDER_K       = 0x01, // slash: n_used < k_fixed
    NOCTI_FNIRS_F_CRC_DROPPED   = 0x02, // slash: ada sample dalam window yang drop karena CRC
} nocti_fnirs_flags_t;

typedef struct __attribute__((packed)) {
    uint8_t  version;        // = NOCTI_PAYLOAD_FNIRS_V1_VERSION
    uint8_t  flags;          // nocti_fnirs_flags_t

    uint8_t  led_id;         // 0..NOCTI_MAX_FNIRS_SOURCES-1
    uint8_t  wavelength;     // nocti_wavelength_t (0=735, 1=850)

    uint8_t  current_mA;     // 0..57 (sesuai clamp driver)
    uint8_t  n_used;         // 0..k_fixed
    uint8_t  k_fixed;        // contoh 3
    uint8_t  _rsv0;          // slash: padding untuk future use

    uint16_t settle_us;      // settle yang dipakai slot
    uint16_t window_us;      // panjang window averaging

    int32_t  avg[4];         // avg raw ADC counts CH0..CH3
} nocti_payload_fnirs_v1_t;

// slash: compile time check untuk jaga kompatibilitas decoder
typedef char nocti_payload_fnirs_v1_size_check[(sizeof(nocti_payload_fnirs_v1_t) == 28) ? 1 : -1];

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Payload: status record v1 (NOCTI_REC_STATUS)
// ------------------------------------------------------------

#define NOCTI_PAYLOAD_STATUS_V1_VERSION     (1u)

typedef struct __attribute__((packed)) {
    uint8_t  version;        // = NOCTI_PAYLOAD_STATUS_V1_VERSION
    uint8_t  flags;          // reserved
    uint16_t battery_mv;     // contoh: 3700
    int16_t  temp_c_x100;    // contoh: 2534 berarti 25.34 C
    uint16_t vled_mv;        // optional rail monitor
    uint16_t vcore_mv;       // optional rail monitor
} nocti_payload_status_v1_t;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Payload: event record v1 (NOCTI_REC_EVENT)
// ------------------------------------------------------------

#define NOCTI_PAYLOAD_EVENT_V1_VERSION      (1u)

typedef struct __attribute__((packed)) {
    uint8_t  version;        // = NOCTI_PAYLOAD_EVENT_V1_VERSION
    uint8_t  level;          // 0=info, 1=warn, 2=err
    uint16_t code;           // event code
    uint32_t arg;            // event arg (counter, id, dsb)
} nocti_payload_event_v1_t;

// ============================================================
// Payload: fNIRS config v1 (NOCTI_REC_FNIRS_CFG)
// slash:
// /belajar: config dikirim sekali saat mulai stream, agar slot record tidak perlu ulang ulang konstanta
// ============================================================

#define NOCTI_PAYLOAD_FNIRS_CFG_V1_VERSION      (1u)

typedef enum {
    NOCTI_FNIRS_MAP_LEDID2_WL = 0,  // slash: slot_index = led_id*2 + wl(735=0,850=1)
} nocti_fnirs_map_mode_t;

typedef struct __attribute__((packed)) {
    uint8_t  version;          // = NOCTI_PAYLOAD_FNIRS_CFG_V1_VERSION
    uint8_t  map_mode;         // nocti_fnirs_map_mode_t

    uint8_t  frame_fs_hz;      // contoh 10
    uint8_t  slot_count;       // contoh 20

    uint8_t  k_fixed;          // contoh 3
    uint8_t  current_735_mA;   // contoh 10
    uint8_t  current_850_mA;   // contoh 10
    uint8_t  _rsv0;

    uint16_t settle_us;        // contoh 200
    uint16_t window_us;        // contoh 3200
} nocti_payload_fnirs_cfg_v1_t;

typedef char nocti_payload_fnirs_cfg_v1_size_check[(sizeof(nocti_payload_fnirs_cfg_v1_t) == 12) ? 1 : -1];


// ============================================================
// Payload: fNIRS frame v1 (NOCTI_REC_FNIRS_FRAME)
// slash:
// /belajar: 1 payload mewakili 1 frame (100 ms) berisi 20 slot
// /belajar: mapping slot tidak dikirim, karena sudah dijelaskan lewat FNIRS_CFG
// ============================================================

#define NOCTI_PAYLOAD_FNIRS_FRAME_V1_VERSION    (1u)

typedef enum {
    NOCTI_FNIRS_FRAME_F_NONE     = 0x00,
    NOCTI_FNIRS_FRAME_F_PARTIAL  = 0x01, // slash: ada slot yang belum terkumpul saat frame di-flush
} nocti_fnirs_frame_flags_t;

typedef struct __attribute__((packed)) {
    uint8_t  version;        // = NOCTI_PAYLOAD_FNIRS_FRAME_V1_VERSION
    uint8_t  flags;          // nocti_fnirs_frame_flags_t

    uint16_t frame_id;       // slash: monotonik, wrap ok

    uint32_t valid_mask;     // slash: bit i = slot i punya data (n_used > 0), pakai 20 bit terbawah
    uint32_t underk_mask;    // slash: bit i = slot i n_used < k_fixed
    uint32_t crc_mask;       // slash: bit i = slot i pernah drop sample karena CRC di window

    int32_t  avg[20][4];     // slash: [slot][ch], raw ADC counts CH0..CH3
    uint8_t  n_used[20];     // slash: 0..k_fixed, untuk debug kualitas window
} nocti_payload_fnirs_frame_v1_t;

// slash: pastikan muat dalam NOCTI_STREAM_MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES (512)
typedef char nocti_payload_fnirs_frame_v1_size_check[(sizeof(nocti_payload_fnirs_frame_v1_t) <= 512) ? 1 : -1];


// ============================================================
// Payload: biopot config v1 (NOCTI_REC_BIOPOT_CFG)
// slash:
// /belajar: config dikirim sekali saat mulai stream, biar decoder tahu fs, gain, dan mask channel
// ============================================================

#define NOCTI_PAYLOAD_BIOPOT_CFG_V1_VERSION      (1u)

typedef enum {
    NOCTI_BIOPOT_FMT_RAW_COUNTS = 0, // slash: raw ADC code 24-bit yang sudah sign-extend ke int32
    NOCTI_BIOPOT_FMT_UV         = 1, // slash: microvolt (opsional, biasanya tidak dipakai di MCU)
} nocti_biopot_fmt_t;

typedef enum {
    NOCTI_BIOPOT_CFG_F_NONE      = 0x00,
    NOCTI_BIOPOT_CFG_F_BIAS_EN   = 0x01, // slash: BIAS buffer aktif, BIASOUT terhubung ke electrode
} nocti_biopot_cfg_flags_t;

typedef struct __attribute__((packed)) {
    uint8_t  version;          // = NOCTI_PAYLOAD_BIOPOT_CFG_V1_VERSION
    uint8_t  fs_hz;            // contoh: 250 (SPS)
    uint8_t  n_ch;             // slash: 8 untuk ADS1299
    uint8_t  fmt;              // nocti_biopot_fmt_t

    uint8_t  ch_enable_mask;   // slash: bit0..bit7 channel enable
    uint8_t  bias_sensp_mask;  // slash: bit0..bit7 channel masuk BIAS_SENSP
    uint8_t  bias_sensn_mask;  // slash: bit0..bit7 channel masuk BIAS_SENSN
    uint8_t  flags;            // nocti_biopot_cfg_flags_t

    uint16_t vref_mV;          // contoh: 4500
    uint8_t  gain[8];          // slash: gain per channel (1,2,4,6,8,12,24), index 0=CH1..7=CH8
    uint8_t  _rsv0[2];         // slash: future use
} nocti_payload_biopot_cfg_v1_t;

// slash: compile time check untuk jaga kompatibilitas decoder
typedef char nocti_payload_biopot_cfg_v1_size_check[(sizeof(nocti_payload_biopot_cfg_v1_t) == 20) ? 1 : -1];


// ============================================================
// Payload: biopot frame v1 (NOCTI_REC_BIOPOT_FRAME)
// slash:
// /belajar: batching beberapa sample biopot per payload menurunkan overhead UDP dan mutex stream
// /belajar: status24 disimpan di 24-bit LSB, upper 8-bit nol
// ============================================================

#define NOCTI_PAYLOAD_BIOPOT_FRAME_V1_VERSION    (1u)
#define NOCTI_BIOPOT_FRAME_MAX_SAMPLES           (10u)

typedef enum {
    NOCTI_BIOPOT_FRAME_F_NONE     = 0x00,
    NOCTI_BIOPOT_FRAME_F_PARTIAL  = 0x01, // slash: frame di-flush sebelum penuh (n_samples < max)
    NOCTI_BIOPOT_FRAME_F_DROPPED  = 0x02, // slash: ada sample drop karena queue atau ringbuf penuh
} nocti_biopot_frame_flags_t;

typedef struct __attribute__((packed)) {
    uint8_t  version;          // = NOCTI_PAYLOAD_BIOPOT_FRAME_V1_VERSION
    uint8_t  flags;            // nocti_biopot_frame_flags_t
    uint16_t frame_id;         // slash: monotonik, wrap ok

    uint8_t  n_samples;        // 1..NOCTI_BIOPOT_FRAME_MAX_SAMPLES
    uint8_t  ch_enable_mask;   // slash: duplikasi dari cfg untuk robustness decoder
    uint16_t _rsv0;            // slash: align 32-bit

    uint32_t status24[NOCTI_BIOPOT_FRAME_MAX_SAMPLES];
    int32_t  ch[NOCTI_BIOPOT_FRAME_MAX_SAMPLES][8];   // slash: sign-extend 24-bit ke int32
} nocti_payload_biopot_frame_v1_t;

// slash: pastikan muat dalam NOCTI_STREAM_MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES (512)
typedef char nocti_payload_biopot_frame_v1_size_check[(sizeof(nocti_payload_biopot_frame_v1_t) <= 512) ? 1 : -1];

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif

// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_common\nocti_crc.c ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_common/nocti_crc.c
// ============================================================
//
// Implementasi CRC16-CCITT-FALSE
// - poly: 0x1021
// - init: 0xFFFF
// - refin: false
// - refout: false
// - xorout: 0x0000
//
// Catatan
// - Implementasi bitwise, ringan dan portable
// - Cukup cepat untuk payload BLE kita yang relatif kecil

#include "nocti_crc.h"

static uint16_t crc16_step(uint16_t crc, uint8_t data)
{
    // slash: masukkan byte ke MSB
    crc ^= (uint16_t)data << 8;

    // slash: proses 8 bit
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        if (crc & 0x8000u) {
            crc = (uint16_t)((crc << 1) ^ 0x1021u);
        } else {
            crc = (uint16_t)(crc << 1);
        }
    }
    return crc;
}

uint16_t nocti_crc16_ccitt_false(const void *data, size_t len)
{
    return nocti_crc16_ccitt_false_update((uint16_t)0xFFFFu, data, len);
}

uint16_t nocti_crc16_ccitt_false_update(uint16_t crc, const void *data, size_t len)
{
    if (!data || len == 0) {
        return crc; // slash: tidak ada data, CRC tidak berubah
    }

    const uint8_t *p = (const uint8_t *)data;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < len; i++) {
        crc = crc16_step(crc, p[i]);
    }
    return crc;
}


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_common\nocti_ringbuf.c ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_common/nocti_ringbuf.c
// ============================================================
// Revisi fokus:
// 1) wrap index tanpa modulo (lebih ringan)
// 2) tambah API drop_oldest untuk producer realtime
// 3) tambah API drop untuk buang data tanpa read
// 4) guard state lebih ketat, tapi tetap SPSC dan ringan
// ============================================================

#include "nocti_ringbuf.h"

#include <string.h>

// ============================================================
// Internal helpers
// ============================================================

static inline bool rb_ok(const nocti_ringbuf_t *rb)
{
    // slash: validasi minimal supaya operasi tidak aneh
    return (rb && rb->buf && rb->cap >= 2);
}

static inline size_t rb_advance(const nocti_ringbuf_t *rb, size_t idx, size_t n)
{
    // slash: fast path tanpa modulo untuk kasus normal (n < cap)
    if (n < rb->cap) {
        size_t v = idx + n;
        if (v >= rb->cap) v -= rb->cap;
        return v;
    }

    // slash: slow path aman untuk n besar (misalnya ada caller salah kirim len)
    // slash: modulo jarang kepakai, tapi bikin fungsi robust
    n %= rb->cap;
    size_t v = idx + n;
    if (v >= rb->cap) v -= rb->cap;
    return v;
}

static inline size_t rb_contig_from_head(const nocti_ringbuf_t *rb)
{
    // slash: berapa byte kontigu yang bisa ditulis dari head sampai akhir buffer
    return rb->cap - rb->head;
}

static inline size_t rb_contig_from_tail(const nocti_ringbuf_t *rb)
{
    // slash: berapa byte kontigu yang bisa dibaca dari tail sampai akhir buffer
    return rb->cap - rb->tail;
}

// ============================================================
// Public: init and reset
// ============================================================

bool nocti_ringbuf_init(nocti_ringbuf_t *rb, uint8_t *storage, size_t capacity)
{
    if (!rb || !storage || capacity < 2) {
        return false; // slash: minimal 2 byte agar head dan tail meaningful
    }

    rb->buf  = storage;
    rb->cap  = capacity;
    rb->head = 0;
    rb->tail = 0;
    rb->full = false;

    return true;
}

void nocti_ringbuf_reset(nocti_ringbuf_t *rb)
{
    if (!rb) return;

    rb->head = 0;
    rb->tail = 0;
    rb->full = false;
}

// ============================================================
// Public: stats
// ============================================================

size_t nocti_ringbuf_capacity(const nocti_ringbuf_t *rb)
{
    return rb_ok(rb) ? rb->cap : 0;
}

bool nocti_ringbuf_empty(const nocti_ringbuf_t *rb)
{
    if (!rb_ok(rb)) return true;
    return (!rb->full && (rb->head == rb->tail));
}

bool nocti_ringbuf_full(const nocti_ringbuf_t *rb)
{
    if (!rb_ok(rb)) return false;
    return rb->full;
}

size_t nocti_ringbuf_size(const nocti_ringbuf_t *rb)
{
    if (!rb_ok(rb)) return 0;

    if (rb->full) return rb->cap;

    if (rb->head >= rb->tail) {
        return rb->head - rb->tail;
    }

    return rb->cap - (rb->tail - rb->head);
}

size_t nocti_ringbuf_free(const nocti_ringbuf_t *rb)
{
    if (!rb_ok(rb)) return 0;
    return rb->cap - nocti_ringbuf_size(rb);
}

// ============================================================
// Internal: chunk write and read
// ============================================================

static size_t rb_write_chunk(nocti_ringbuf_t *rb, const uint8_t *p, size_t n)
{
    // slash: tulis kontigu dari head sampai end, lalu wrap jika perlu
    size_t to_end = rb_contig_from_head(rb);
    size_t first  = (n < to_end) ? n : to_end;

    memcpy(&rb->buf[rb->head], p, first);
    rb->head = rb_advance(rb, rb->head, first);

    size_t remain = n - first;
    if (remain > 0) {
        memcpy(&rb->buf[rb->head], p + first, remain);
        rb->head = rb_advance(rb, rb->head, remain);
    }

    return n;
}

static size_t rb_read_chunk(nocti_ringbuf_t *rb, uint8_t *out, size_t n)
{
    // slash: baca kontigu dari tail sampai end, lalu wrap jika perlu
    size_t to_end = rb_contig_from_tail(rb);
    size_t first  = (n < to_end) ? n : to_end;

    memcpy(out, &rb->buf[rb->tail], first);
    rb->tail = rb_advance(rb, rb->tail, first);

    size_t remain = n - first;
    if (remain > 0) {
        memcpy(out + first, &rb->buf[rb->tail], remain);
        rb->tail = rb_advance(rb, rb->tail, remain);
    }

    return n;
}

// ============================================================
// Public: IO basic
// ============================================================

size_t nocti_ringbuf_write(nocti_ringbuf_t *rb, const void *data, size_t len)
{
    if (!rb_ok(rb) || !data || len == 0) return 0;

    size_t free = nocti_ringbuf_free(rb);
    size_t n = (len < free) ? len : free;
    if (n == 0) return 0;

    rb_write_chunk(rb, (const uint8_t *)data, n);

    // slash: kalau head menyusul tail, berarti full
    if (rb->head == rb->tail) rb->full = true;

    return n;
}

bool nocti_ringbuf_write_all(nocti_ringbuf_t *rb, const void *data, size_t len)
{
    if (!rb_ok(rb) || (!data && len > 0)) return false;
    if (len == 0) return true;

    size_t free = nocti_ringbuf_free(rb);
    if (len > free) return false; // slash: tidak cukup ruang, tidak menulis sebagian

    size_t wrote = nocti_ringbuf_write(rb, data, len);
    return (wrote == len);
}


size_t nocti_ringbuf_read(nocti_ringbuf_t *rb, void *out, size_t len)
{
    if (!rb_ok(rb) || !out || len == 0) return 0;

    size_t avail = nocti_ringbuf_size(rb);
    size_t n = (len < avail) ? len : avail;
    if (n == 0) return 0;

    // slash: begitu read, tidak mungkin full lagi
    rb->full = false;

    rb_read_chunk(rb, (uint8_t *)out, n);
    return n;
}

size_t nocti_ringbuf_peek(const nocti_ringbuf_t *rb, void *out, size_t len)
{
    if (!rb_ok(rb) || !out || len == 0) return 0;

    size_t avail = nocti_ringbuf_size(rb);
    size_t n = (len < avail) ? len : avail;
    if (n == 0) return 0;

    // slash: peek tidak mengubah state, jadi pakai tail temporer
    size_t tail = rb->tail;

    size_t to_end = rb->cap - tail;
    size_t first  = (n < to_end) ? n : to_end;

    memcpy(out, &rb->buf[tail], first);
    tail += first;
    if (tail >= rb->cap) tail -= rb->cap;

    size_t remain = n - first;
    if (remain > 0) {
        memcpy((uint8_t *)out + first, &rb->buf[tail], remain);
    }

    return n;
}

// ============================================================
// Public: optional helpers
// ============================================================

size_t nocti_ringbuf_drop(nocti_ringbuf_t *rb, size_t len)
{
    if (!rb_ok(rb) || len == 0) return 0;

    size_t avail = nocti_ringbuf_size(rb);
    size_t n = (len < avail) ? len : avail;
    if (n == 0) return 0;

    // slash: drop sama seperti read tapi tanpa copy
    rb->full = false;
    // slash: drop = geser tail saja, tanpa copy
    rb->tail = rb_advance(rb, rb->tail, n);

    return n;
}

size_t nocti_ringbuf_write_drop_oldest(nocti_ringbuf_t *rb, const void *data, size_t len)
{
    if (!rb_ok(rb) || !data || len == 0) return 0;

    // slash: kalau len lebih besar dari capacity, kita hanya bisa simpan tail end-nya
    // slash: ini menjaga determinisme, daripada loop drop terlalu lama
    if (len > rb->cap) {
        const uint8_t *p = (const uint8_t *)data;
        p += (len - rb->cap);
        data = p;
        len  = rb->cap;
    }

    size_t free = nocti_ringbuf_free(rb);
    if (len > free) {
        // slash: drop data lama secukupnya agar len muat
        size_t need = len - free;
        (void)nocti_ringbuf_drop(rb, need);
    }

    // slash: setelah drop, harusnya cukup ruang
    size_t wrote = nocti_ringbuf_write(rb, data, len);

    // slash: secara teori wrote harus sama dengan len
    return wrote;
}

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_fnirs/include/nocti_fnirs.h
// ADS131M04 fNIRS front end driver (NoctiNIRS)
// ============================================================

#pragma once

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>

#include "esp_err.h"
#include "freertos/FreeRTOS.h"
#include "freertos/queue.h"

#include "nocti_types.h"     // slash: nocti_time_us_t, nocti_wavelength_t
#include "nocti_hal_spi.h"   // slash: nocti_hal_spi_t

// slash: fallback define kalau project belum define
#ifndef NOCTI_MAX_FNIRS_SOURCES
#define NOCTI_MAX_FNIRS_SOURCES 10u
#endif

#ifndef NOCTI_MAX_FNIRS_WAVELENGTHS
#define NOCTI_MAX_FNIRS_WAVELENGTHS 2u
#endif

#ifndef NOCTI_FNIRS_SLOTS_PER_FRAME
#define NOCTI_FNIRS_SLOTS_PER_FRAME (NOCTI_MAX_FNIRS_SOURCES * NOCTI_MAX_FNIRS_WAVELENGTHS)
#endif

// slash: helper konversi signed 24-bit (sign-extended) ke raw u24 (0..0xFFFFFF)
static inline uint32_t nocti_fnirs_s24_to_u24(int32_t s24)
{
    // slash: jika negatif, tambahkan 2^24 untuk balik ke representasi raw
    if (s24 < 0) return (uint32_t)(s24 + (int32_t)(1 << 24));
    return (uint32_t)s24;
}

// slash: record 1 sample hasil 1 kali baca ADS131M04 (CH0..CH3)
typedef struct {
    nocti_time_us_t timestamp_us;   // slash: timestamp saat frame dibaca (us)

    uint8_t slot_index;             // slash: 0..19, mapping led_id dan wavelength
    uint8_t led_id;                 // slash: 0..9
    uint8_t wavelength;             // slash: NOCTI_WL_735 atau NOCTI_WL_850 (cast to u8)
    uint8_t current_mA;             // slash: metadata arus LED (boleh 0 kalau dark read)

    uint32_t status24;              // slash: STATUS word 24-bit (MSB aligned status16 di bit 23..8)

    // slash: dua view sekaligus biar debug enak
    int32_t  ch[4];                 // slash: CH0..CH3 signed (24-bit sign-extended)
    uint32_t ch_u24[4];             // slash: CH0..CH3 raw u24 (0..0xFFFFFF)

    uint32_t crc24;                 // slash: CRC word 24-bit (umumnya CRC16 MSB aligned)
    uint16_t crc16_rx;              // slash: CRC16 dari device (extract dari crc24)
    uint16_t crc16_calc;            // slash: CRC16 hasil hitung lokal
    bool     crc_ok;                // slash: true jika rx == calc
} nocti_fnirs_sample_t;

// slash: frame mentah ADS131M04 (tanpa metadata LED)
// /belajar: ini dipakai untuk mode "producer" yang baca ADC terus menerus
// /belajar: scheduler cukup memilih sampel yang timestamp-nya jatuh di window LED ON
typedef struct {
    nocti_time_us_t timestamp_us;   // slash: timestamp saat frame dibaca (us)

    uint32_t status24;              // slash: STATUS word 24-bit
    int32_t  ch[4];                 // slash: CH0..CH3 signed (24-bit sign-extended)
    uint32_t ch_u24[4];             // slash: CH0..CH3 raw u24

    uint32_t crc24;                 // slash: CRC word 24-bit
    uint16_t crc16_rx;              // slash: CRC16 dari device
    uint16_t crc16_calc;            // slash: CRC16 hasil hitung lokal
    bool     crc_ok;                // slash: true jika rx == calc
} nocti_fnirs_frame_t;

// slash: config init ADS131M04
typedef struct {
    nocti_hal_spi_t *spi;       // slash: handle SPI HAL (sudah init di board)
    int gpio_drdy;              // slash: DRDY pin dari ADS131M04 ke ESP32
    int gpio_sync;              // slash: SYNC pin, boleh sama dengan gpio_reset kalau gabungan
    int gpio_reset;             // slash: RESET pin, boleh sama dengan gpio_sync kalau gabungan

    uint32_t clkin_hz;          // slash: frekuensi CLKIN, default 8192000
    uint32_t drdy_timeout_us;   // slash: timeout tunggu DRDY event
    uint32_t spi_timeout_ms;    // slash: timeout HAL SPI

    bool do_hw_reset_pulse;     // slash: toggle SYNC/RESET low cukup lama untuk hardware reset
    bool do_cmd_reset;          // slash: kirim RESET command via SPI (opsional)
    bool do_adc_register_init;  // slash: lakukan UNLOCK dan write register pilihan

    // slash: CRC check untuk buang frame korup
    bool crc_check_enable;      // slash: true = hitung CRC16 dan tandai crc_ok
    uint8_t crc_retry;          // slash: retry baca frame kalau CRC gagal (0..3)

    // slash: optional output queue
    uint16_t queue_len;         // slash: 0 = queue off, >0 buat queue untuk stream async

    // slash: register values, pakai 0 untuk skip write (biar tetap default)
    uint16_t reg_mode;          // slash: MODE addr 0x02
    uint16_t reg_clock;         // slash: CLOCK addr 0x03
    uint16_t reg_gain;          // slash: GAIN addr 0x04
} nocti_fnirs_cfg_t;

// slash: helper default config
void nocti_fnirs_cfg_set_defaults(nocti_fnirs_cfg_t *cfg);

// slash: init dan deinit
esp_err_t nocti_fnirs_init(const nocti_fnirs_cfg_t *cfg);
esp_err_t nocti_fnirs_deinit(void);

// slash: optional output queue (kalau queue_len > 0)
QueueHandle_t nocti_fnirs_get_queue(void);

// slash: baca 1 frame ADS131 (tunggu DRDY), metadata slot diberikan manual
// led_id: 0..9, wl: NOCTI_WL_735/NOCTI_WL_850, current_mA boleh 0 untuk dark read
esp_err_t nocti_fnirs_read_sample(uint8_t led_id,
                                 nocti_wavelength_t wl,
                                 uint8_t current_mA,
                                 nocti_fnirs_sample_t *out_opt);

// slash: baca 1 frame ADS131M04 (tunggu DRDY) tanpa metadata LED
// /belajar: dipakai untuk task producer ADC agar callback scheduler tetap ringan
esp_err_t nocti_fnirs_read_frame(nocti_fnirs_frame_t *out_opt);

// slash: debug helpers untuk verifikasi SPI dan DRDY
uint32_t  nocti_fnirs_dbg_drdy_count(void);
esp_err_t nocti_fnirs_dbg_read_reg_u16(uint8_t addr, uint16_t *out_u16);
esp_err_t nocti_fnirs_dbg_write_reg_u16(uint8_t addr, uint16_t val);


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_fnirs\nocti_fnirs.c ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_fnirs/nocti_fnirs.c
// ============================================================

#include "nocti_fnirs.h"

#include <string.h>

#include "driver/gpio.h"
#include "esp_attr.h"            // slash: IRAM_ATTR

#include "freertos/task.h"
#include "freertos/portmacro.h"

#include "nocti_log.h"
#include "nocti_timebase.h"      // slash: nocti_timebase_now_us, nocti_timebase_busywait_us

static const char *TAG = "nocti_fnirs";

// ============================================================
// ADS131M04 constants
// ============================================================

// slash: register address
#define ADS131_REG_MODE   0x02u
#define ADS131_REG_CLOCK  0x03u
#define ADS131_REG_GAIN   0x04u

// slash: 16-bit command word
#define ADS131_CMD_NULL    0x0000u
#define ADS131_CMD_RESET   0x0011u
#define ADS131_CMD_LOCK    0x0555u
#define ADS131_CMD_UNLOCK  0x0655u

// slash: RREG dan WREG
// bits 15..13 opcode, 12..8 addr, 7..0 count minus one
#define ADS131_CMD_RREG(addr, n_minus1) (uint16_t)((0x5u << 13) | (((addr) & 0x1Fu) << 8) | ((n_minus1) & 0xFFu))
#define ADS131_CMD_WREG(addr, n_minus1) (uint16_t)((0x3u << 13) | (((addr) & 0x1Fu) << 8) | ((n_minus1) & 0xFFu))

// slash: word size 24-bit, frame normal 6 words (STATUS, CH0..CH3, CRC)
#define ADS131_WORD_BYTES    3u
#define ADS131_FRAME_WORDS   6u
#define ADS131_FRAME_BYTES   (ADS131_WORD_BYTES * ADS131_FRAME_WORDS)

// slash: MODE bit field
#define ADS131_MODE_TIMEOUT_BIT   (1u << 4)   // slash: TIMEOUT enable
#define ADS131_MODE_WLENGTH_SHIFT 8u          // slash: WLENGTH[1:0] ada di bit 9:8

// ============================================================
// Internal context
// ============================================================

typedef struct {
    nocti_fnirs_cfg_t cfg;
    bool inited;

    // slash: DRDY sync
    TaskHandle_t wait_task;
    volatile uint32_t drdy_count;
    portMUX_TYPE mux;

    // slash: optional queue
    QueueHandle_t q;

    // slash: SPI buffers
    uint8_t tx[ADS131_FRAME_BYTES];
    uint8_t rx[ADS131_FRAME_BYTES];
} fnirs_ctx_t;

static fnirs_ctx_t s = {
    .mux = portMUX_INITIALIZER_UNLOCKED
};

// ============================================================
// Time helpers (pakai nocti_timebase)
// ============================================================

static inline uint64_t now_us64(void)
{
    // slash: satu bahasa waktu dengan scheduler dan modul lain
    return (uint64_t)nocti_timebase_now_us();
}

static inline void delay_us(uint32_t us)
{
    // slash: busywait presisi untuk pulse reset
    nocti_timebase_busywait_us(us);
}

// ============================================================
// 24-bit packing helpers
// ============================================================

static inline void pack_cmd24(uint16_t cmd16, uint8_t out3[3])
{
    // slash: cmd16 masuk ke bit 23..8, byte terakhir 0
    out3[0] = (uint8_t)((cmd16 >> 8) & 0xFFu);
    out3[1] = (uint8_t)(cmd16 & 0xFFu);
    out3[2] = 0x00u;
}

static inline uint32_t unpack_u24(const uint8_t in3[3])
{
    return ((uint32_t)in3[0] << 16) | ((uint32_t)in3[1] << 8) | (uint32_t)in3[2];
}

static inline int32_t sign_extend_24(uint32_t v24)
{
    // slash: 24-bit signed 2's complement
    if (v24 & 0x800000u) return (int32_t)(v24 | 0xFF000000u);
    return (int32_t)v24;
}

// ============================================================
// CRC16 CCITT (polynomial 0x1021, init 0xFFFF)
// ============================================================

static uint16_t crc16_ccitt_0xffff(const uint8_t *data, size_t len)
{
    uint16_t crc = 0xFFFFu;

    for (size_t i = 0; i < len; i++) {
        crc ^= (uint16_t)data[i] << 8;

        for (int b = 0; b < 8; b++) {
            if (crc & 0x8000u) crc = (uint16_t)((crc << 1) ^ 0x1021u);
            else              crc = (uint16_t)(crc << 1);
        }
    }
    return crc;
}

// ============================================================
// Slot metadata validation
// ============================================================

static inline bool is_valid_wavelength(nocti_wavelength_t wl)
{
    return (wl == NOCTI_WL_735) || (wl == NOCTI_WL_850);
}

static esp_err_t calc_slot_index(uint8_t led_id, nocti_wavelength_t wl, uint8_t *out_slot_index)
{
    if (!out_slot_index) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (led_id >= NOCTI_MAX_FNIRS_SOURCES) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!is_valid_wavelength(wl)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    uint8_t wl_idx = (wl == NOCTI_WL_735) ? 0u : 1u;
    uint8_t idx = (uint8_t)(led_id * (uint8_t)NOCTI_MAX_FNIRS_WAVELENGTHS + wl_idx);

    if (idx >= (uint8_t)NOCTI_FNIRS_SLOTS_PER_FRAME) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    *out_slot_index = idx;
    return ESP_OK;
}

// ============================================================
// SPI transfer
// ============================================================

static esp_err_t spi_frame_xfer(const uint8_t *tx, uint8_t *rx)
{
    // slash: 1 frame lengkap 6 word
    // slash: paksa 1 transaksi SPI agar CS tidak toggle di tengah frame
    return nocti_hal_spi_transfer_contig(s.cfg.spi, tx, rx, ADS131_FRAME_BYTES, s.cfg.spi_timeout_ms);
}

// ============================================================
// DRDY ISR
// ============================================================

static void IRAM_ATTR drdy_isr(void *arg)
{
    (void)arg;

    BaseType_t hp = pdFALSE;

    taskENTER_CRITICAL_ISR(&s.mux);
    s.drdy_count += 1u;
    TaskHandle_t t = s.wait_task;
    taskEXIT_CRITICAL_ISR(&s.mux);

    if (t) vTaskNotifyGiveFromISR(t, &hp);
    if (hp) portYIELD_FROM_ISR();
}

static inline void wait_task_set(TaskHandle_t t)
{
    taskENTER_CRITICAL(&s.mux);
    s.wait_task = t;
    taskEXIT_CRITICAL(&s.mux);
}

static inline void wait_task_clear(void)
{
    taskENTER_CRITICAL(&s.mux);
    s.wait_task = NULL;
    taskEXIT_CRITICAL(&s.mux);
}

static inline uint32_t drdy_count_get(void)
{
    taskENTER_CRITICAL(&s.mux);
    uint32_t c = s.drdy_count;
    taskEXIT_CRITICAL(&s.mux);
    return c;
}

// ============================================================
// Wait DRDY event
// ============================================================

static esp_err_t wait_drdy_low(uint32_t timeout_us)
{
    // slash: kalau DRDY sudah low, langsung ok
    if (gpio_get_level(s.cfg.gpio_drdy) == 0) return ESP_OK;

    // slash: gunakan ISR counter supaya aman untuk pulse atau level
    uint32_t c0 = drdy_count_get();

    // slash: short timeout polling
    if (timeout_us > 0 && timeout_us <= 2000u) {
        uint64_t t0 = now_us64();
        while (1) {
            if (gpio_get_level(s.cfg.gpio_drdy) == 0) return ESP_OK;
            if (drdy_count_get() != c0) return ESP_OK;

            if ((now_us64() - t0) >= (uint64_t)timeout_us) return ESP_ERR_TIMEOUT;
            taskYIELD();
        }
    }

    // slash: notify mode
    TaskHandle_t me = xTaskGetCurrentTaskHandle();
    (void)ulTaskNotifyTake(pdTRUE, 0);

    wait_task_set(me);

    // slash: race guard
    if (gpio_get_level(s.cfg.gpio_drdy) == 0) {
        wait_task_clear();
        return ESP_OK;
    }
    if (drdy_count_get() != c0) {
        wait_task_clear();
        return ESP_OK;
    }

    TickType_t ticks = portMAX_DELAY;
    if (timeout_us > 0) {
        uint32_t ms = (timeout_us + 999u) / 1000u;
        ticks = pdMS_TO_TICKS(ms);
        if (ticks == 0) ticks = 1;
    }

    uint32_t got = ulTaskNotifyTake(pdTRUE, ticks);
    wait_task_clear();

    if (got == 0) return ESP_ERR_TIMEOUT;
    return ESP_OK;
}

static esp_err_t wait_drdy_high(uint32_t timeout_us)
{
    // slash: simple polling tunggu DRDY balik high (deteksi frame tidak â€œngunci lowâ€)
    if (gpio_get_level(s.cfg.gpio_drdy) != 0) return ESP_OK;

    uint64_t t0 = now_us64();
    while (1) {
        if (gpio_get_level(s.cfg.gpio_drdy) != 0) return ESP_OK;
        if (timeout_us > 0 && (now_us64() - t0) >= (uint64_t)timeout_us) return ESP_ERR_TIMEOUT;
        taskYIELD();
    }
}

// ============================================================
// Register helpers
// ============================================================

static esp_err_t ads131_cmd_simple(uint16_t cmd)
{
    memset(s.tx, 0, sizeof(s.tx));
    pack_cmd24(cmd, &s.tx[0]);
    return spi_frame_xfer(s.tx, s.rx);
}

static esp_err_t ads131_write_reg_u16(uint8_t addr, uint16_t val)
{
    memset(s.tx, 0, sizeof(s.tx));

    // slash: WREG 1 reg, payload 16-bit MSB aligned di word1
    pack_cmd24(ADS131_CMD_WREG(addr, 0u), &s.tx[0]);

    s.tx[3] = (uint8_t)((val >> 8) & 0xFFu);
    s.tx[4] = (uint8_t)(val & 0xFFu);
    s.tx[5] = 0x00u;

    return spi_frame_xfer(s.tx, s.rx);
}

static esp_err_t ads131_read_reg_u16(uint8_t addr, uint16_t *out_u16)
{
    if (!out_u16) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    // slash: RREG, response muncul di frame berikutnya
    memset(s.tx, 0, sizeof(s.tx));
    pack_cmd24(ADS131_CMD_RREG(addr, 0u), &s.tx[0]);

    esp_err_t err = spi_frame_xfer(s.tx, s.rx);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    // slash: NULL untuk ambil response
    memset(s.tx, 0, sizeof(s.tx));
    pack_cmd24(ADS131_CMD_NULL, &s.tx[0]);

    err = spi_frame_xfer(s.tx, s.rx);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    uint32_t resp24 = unpack_u24(&s.rx[0]);
    *out_u16 = (uint16_t)((resp24 >> 8) & 0xFFFFu);
    return ESP_OK;
}

// ============================================================
// SYNC/RESET helpers
// ============================================================

static int pick_sync_reset_pin(void)
{
    // slash: kalau hardware 1 pin gabungan, gpio_sync dan gpio_reset sama
    if (s.cfg.gpio_reset >= 0 && s.cfg.gpio_sync >= 0 && s.cfg.gpio_reset == s.cfg.gpio_sync) {
        return s.cfg.gpio_reset;
    }
    if (s.cfg.gpio_reset >= 0) return s.cfg.gpio_reset;
    if (s.cfg.gpio_sync >= 0)  return s.cfg.gpio_sync;
    return -1;
}

static void do_hw_reset_pulse(void)
{
    int pin_sr = pick_sync_reset_pin();
    if (pin_sr < 0) return;

    uint32_t clkin = (s.cfg.clkin_hz == 0u) ? 8192000u : s.cfg.clkin_hz;
    uint32_t t_reset_us = (uint32_t)((2048u * 1000000ull) / (uint64_t)clkin);
    if (t_reset_us < 300u) t_reset_us = 300u;

    gpio_set_level(pin_sr, 0);
    delay_us(t_reset_us);
    gpio_set_level(pin_sr, 1);

    delay_us(20u);
}

// ============================================================
// Public API
// ============================================================

void nocti_fnirs_cfg_set_defaults(nocti_fnirs_cfg_t *cfg)
{
    if (!cfg) return;
    memset(cfg, 0, sizeof(*cfg));

    cfg->spi = NULL;
    cfg->gpio_drdy = -1;
    cfg->gpio_sync = -1;
    cfg->gpio_reset = -1;

    cfg->clkin_hz = 8192000u;
    cfg->drdy_timeout_us = 20000u;
    cfg->spi_timeout_ms = 20u;

    cfg->do_hw_reset_pulse = true;
    cfg->do_cmd_reset = false;
    cfg->do_adc_register_init = true;

    cfg->crc_check_enable = true;   // slash: default ON untuk buang frame korup
    cfg->crc_retry = 1;             // slash: 1x retry cukup untuk debug

    cfg->queue_len = 0;

    cfg->reg_mode = 0u;
    cfg->reg_clock = 0u;
    cfg->reg_gain = 0u;
}

esp_err_t nocti_fnirs_init(const nocti_fnirs_cfg_t *cfg)
{
    if (!cfg) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!cfg->spi) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (cfg->gpio_drdy < 0) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    memset(&s, 0, sizeof(s));
    s.mux = (portMUX_TYPE)portMUX_INITIALIZER_UNLOCKED;
    s.cfg = *cfg;

    // slash: DRDY input + negedge interrupt
    gpio_config_t io;
    memset(&io, 0, sizeof(io));
    io.pin_bit_mask = (1ULL << (uint32_t)s.cfg.gpio_drdy);
    io.mode = GPIO_MODE_INPUT;
    io.pull_up_en = GPIO_PULLUP_DISABLE;
    io.pull_down_en = GPIO_PULLDOWN_DISABLE;
    io.intr_type = GPIO_INTR_NEGEDGE;

    esp_err_t err = gpio_config(&io);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    err = gpio_isr_handler_add(s.cfg.gpio_drdy, drdy_isr, NULL);
    if (err != ESP_OK) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "gpio_isr_handler_add gagal: pastikan ISR service sudah diinstall di board");
        return err;
    }

    // slash: SYNC/RESET output idle high
    int pin_sr = pick_sync_reset_pin();
    if (pin_sr >= 0) {
        gpio_config_t r;
        memset(&r, 0, sizeof(r));
        r.pin_bit_mask = (1ULL << (uint32_t)pin_sr);
        r.mode = GPIO_MODE_OUTPUT;
        r.intr_type = GPIO_INTR_DISABLE;
        (void)gpio_config(&r);
        gpio_set_level(pin_sr, 1);
    }

    // slash: optional queue
    s.q = NULL;
    if (s.cfg.queue_len > 0) {
        s.q = xQueueCreate((UBaseType_t)s.cfg.queue_len, sizeof(nocti_fnirs_sample_t));
        if (!s.q) return ESP_ERR_NO_MEM;
    }

    // slash: hardware reset pulse
    if (s.cfg.do_hw_reset_pulse) {
        do_hw_reset_pulse();
    }

    // slash: optional SPI reset command
    if (s.cfg.do_cmd_reset) {
        (void)ads131_cmd_simple(ADS131_CMD_RESET);
        delay_us(20u);
    }

    // slash: probe register untuk verifikasi SPI hidup
    uint16_t mode0 = 0;
    uint16_t clock0 = 0;

    err = ads131_read_reg_u16(ADS131_REG_MODE, &mode0);
    if (err != ESP_OK) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "SPI probe MODE gagal: %s", esp_err_to_name(err));
        return err;
    }

    err = ads131_read_reg_u16(ADS131_REG_CLOCK, &clock0);
    if (err != ESP_OK) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "SPI probe CLOCK gagal: %s", esp_err_to_name(err));
        return err;
    }

    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "ADS131 probe ok MODE=0x%04X CLOCK=0x%04X", (unsigned)mode0, (unsigned)clock0);

    // slash: optional register init
    if (s.cfg.do_adc_register_init) {

        (void)ads131_cmd_simple(ADS131_CMD_UNLOCK);

        if (s.cfg.reg_mode != 0u) {
            (void)ads131_write_reg_u16(ADS131_REG_MODE, s.cfg.reg_mode);
        } else {
            uint16_t m = mode0;

            // slash: pastikan WLENGTH = 24-bit (datasheet: WLENGTH=01b)
            uint16_t wlen = (uint16_t)((m >> ADS131_MODE_WLENGTH_SHIFT) & 0x3u);
            if (wlen != 0x1u) {
                m &= (uint16_t)~(0x3u << ADS131_MODE_WLENGTH_SHIFT);
                m |= (uint16_t)(0x1u << ADS131_MODE_WLENGTH_SHIFT);
            }

            // slash: TIMEOUT boleh tetap ON, tapi bisa dimatikan untuk debug jika curiga frame putus
            // m &= (uint16_t)~ADS131_MODE_TIMEOUT_BIT;

            (void)ads131_write_reg_u16(ADS131_REG_MODE, m);
        }

        if (s.cfg.reg_clock != 0u) (void)ads131_write_reg_u16(ADS131_REG_CLOCK, s.cfg.reg_clock);
        if (s.cfg.reg_gain  != 0u) (void)ads131_write_reg_u16(ADS131_REG_GAIN,  s.cfg.reg_gain);

        delay_us(20u);
    }

    s.inited = true;
    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "init ok, queue=%s crc=%s retry=%u",
               s.q ? "on" : "off",
               s.cfg.crc_check_enable ? "on" : "off",
               (unsigned)s.cfg.crc_retry);
    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t nocti_fnirs_deinit(void)
{
    if (!s.inited) return ESP_OK;

    (void)gpio_isr_handler_remove(s.cfg.gpio_drdy);

    if (s.q) {
        vQueueDelete(s.q);
        s.q = NULL;
    }

    memset(&s, 0, sizeof(s));
    s.mux = (portMUX_TYPE)portMUX_INITIALIZER_UNLOCKED;
    return ESP_OK;
}

QueueHandle_t nocti_fnirs_get_queue(void)
{
    return s.q;
}

uint32_t nocti_fnirs_dbg_drdy_count(void)
{
    return drdy_count_get();
}

esp_err_t nocti_fnirs_dbg_read_reg_u16(uint8_t addr, uint16_t *out_u16)
{
    if (!s.inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
    return ads131_read_reg_u16(addr, out_u16);
}

esp_err_t nocti_fnirs_dbg_write_reg_u16(uint8_t addr, uint16_t val)
{
    if (!s.inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
    return ads131_write_reg_u16(addr, val);
}

esp_err_t nocti_fnirs_read_sample(uint8_t led_id,
                                 nocti_wavelength_t wl,
                                 uint8_t current_mA,
                                 nocti_fnirs_sample_t *out_opt)
{
    if (!s.inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

    uint8_t slot_index = 0;
    esp_err_t err = calc_slot_index(led_id, wl, &slot_index);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    nocti_fnirs_sample_t rec;
    memset(&rec, 0, sizeof(rec));

    rec.slot_index = slot_index;
    rec.led_id = led_id;
    rec.wavelength = (uint8_t)wl;
    rec.current_mA = current_mA;

    // slash: loop baca, retry kalau CRC gagal
    uint8_t attempts = (uint8_t)(1u + s.cfg.crc_retry);

    for (uint8_t k = 0; k < attempts; k++) {

        // slash: tunggu DRDY low
        err = wait_drdy_low(s.cfg.drdy_timeout_us);
        if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

        // slash: timestamp dekat pembacaan
        rec.timestamp_us = (nocti_time_us_t)now_us64();

        // slash: baca 1 frame dengan NULL command
        memset(s.tx, 0, sizeof(s.tx));
        pack_cmd24(ADS131_CMD_NULL, &s.tx[0]);

        err = spi_frame_xfer(s.tx, s.rx);
        if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

        // slash: tunggu DRDY balik high (indikator shift-out selesai)
        (void)wait_drdy_high(5000u);

        // slash: parse 6 word
        rec.status24 = unpack_u24(&s.rx[0]);

        uint32_t u0 = unpack_u24(&s.rx[3]);
        uint32_t u1 = unpack_u24(&s.rx[6]);
        uint32_t u2 = unpack_u24(&s.rx[9]);
        uint32_t u3 = unpack_u24(&s.rx[12]);

        rec.ch_u24[0] = u0;
        rec.ch_u24[1] = u1;
        rec.ch_u24[2] = u2;
        rec.ch_u24[3] = u3;

        rec.ch[0] = sign_extend_24(u0);
        rec.ch[1] = sign_extend_24(u1);
        rec.ch[2] = sign_extend_24(u2);
        rec.ch[3] = sign_extend_24(u3);

        rec.crc24 = unpack_u24(&s.rx[15]);

        // slash: CRC16 biasanya MSB aligned di word CRC
        rec.crc16_rx = (uint16_t)((rec.crc24 >> 8) & 0xFFFFu);

        // slash: hitung CRC16 dari 5 word pertama (STATUS + CH0..CH3) = 15 byte
        rec.crc16_calc = crc16_ccitt_0xffff(&s.rx[0], 15u);
        rec.crc_ok = (rec.crc16_rx == rec.crc16_calc);

        if (!s.cfg.crc_check_enable) break;
        if (rec.crc_ok) break;

        // slash: CRC gagal, retry ambil sample berikutnya
        if (k == 0) {
            NOCTI_LOGW(TAG, "CRC fail rx=0x%04X calc=0x%04X, retry",
                       (unsigned)rec.crc16_rx, (unsigned)rec.crc16_calc);
        }
    }

    if (out_opt) *out_opt = rec;

    if (s.q) (void)xQueueSend(s.q, &rec, 0);

    return ESP_OK;
}

// ============================================================
// Public API tambahan: read frame mentah (tanpa metadata LED)
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_fnirs_read_frame(nocti_fnirs_frame_t *out_opt)
{
    if (!s.inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
    if (!out_opt) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    nocti_fnirs_frame_t rec;
    memset(&rec, 0, sizeof(rec));

    // slash: loop baca, retry kalau CRC gagal
    uint8_t attempts = (uint8_t)(1u + s.cfg.crc_retry);

    for (uint8_t k = 0; k < attempts; k++) {

        // slash: tunggu DRDY low
        esp_err_t err = wait_drdy_low(s.cfg.drdy_timeout_us);
        if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

        // slash: timestamp dekat pembacaan
        rec.timestamp_us = (nocti_time_us_t)now_us64();

        // slash: baca 1 frame dengan NULL command
        memset(s.tx, 0, sizeof(s.tx));
        pack_cmd24(ADS131_CMD_NULL, &s.tx[0]);

        err = spi_frame_xfer(s.tx, s.rx);
        if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

        // slash: tunggu DRDY balik high (indikator shift-out selesai)
        (void)wait_drdy_high(5000u);

        rec.status24 = unpack_u24(&s.rx[0]);

        uint32_t u0 = unpack_u24(&s.rx[3]);
        uint32_t u1 = unpack_u24(&s.rx[6]);
        uint32_t u2 = unpack_u24(&s.rx[9]);
        uint32_t u3 = unpack_u24(&s.rx[12]);

        rec.ch_u24[0] = u0;
        rec.ch_u24[1] = u1;
        rec.ch_u24[2] = u2;
        rec.ch_u24[3] = u3;

        rec.ch[0] = sign_extend_24(u0);
        rec.ch[1] = sign_extend_24(u1);
        rec.ch[2] = sign_extend_24(u2);
        rec.ch[3] = sign_extend_24(u3);

        rec.crc24 = unpack_u24(&s.rx[15]);
        rec.crc16_rx = (uint16_t)((rec.crc24 >> 8) & 0xFFFFu);
        rec.crc16_calc = crc16_ccitt_0xffff(&s.rx[0], 15u);
        rec.crc_ok = (rec.crc16_rx == rec.crc16_calc);

        // slash: jika CRC check dimatikan, kita terima apa adanya
        if (!s.cfg.crc_check_enable) break;

        // slash: jika CRC ok, selesai
        if (rec.crc_ok) break;

        // slash: kalau CRC gagal, coba ambil frame berikutnya
    }

    *out_opt = rec;
    return ESP_OK;
}


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_hal_i2c\include\nocti_hal_i2c.h ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_hal_i2c/include/nocti_hal_i2c.h
// Rewrite fokus stabilitas untuk ESP IDF driver/i2c_master.h
// Inti perubahan:
// - Default pakai mode sinkron blocking: trans_queue_depth = 0
// - Support parameter scl_wait_us untuk antisipasi clock stretching
// - Probe pakai i2c_master_probe saat mode sinkron
// - Cache dev handle tetap ada tapi aman
// ============================================================

#pragma once

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>

#include "esp_err.h"
#include "freertos/FreeRTOS.h"
#include "freertos/semphr.h"

#include "driver/i2c_master.h"  // i2c_port_t

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

#ifndef NOCTI_HAL_I2C_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS
#define NOCTI_HAL_I2C_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS 50u
#endif

#ifndef NOCTI_HAL_I2C_MAX_DEV_CACHE
#define NOCTI_HAL_I2C_MAX_DEV_CACHE 8
#endif

#ifndef NOCTI_HAL_I2C_DEFAULT_SCL_WAIT_US
// slash: nilai aman untuk debug, bisa kamu kecilkan nanti
#define NOCTI_HAL_I2C_DEFAULT_SCL_WAIT_US 15000u
#endif

typedef struct {
    i2c_port_t i2c_port;
    int gpio_sda;
    int gpio_scl;
    uint32_t clk_speed_hz;

    bool enable_internal_pullups;
    bool delete_driver_on_deinit;

    uint32_t default_timeout_ms;     // 0 -> pakai NOCTI_HAL_I2C_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS

    // slash: 0 -> mode sinkron blocking (disarankan untuk stabilitas)
    uint32_t trans_queue_depth;

    // slash: 0 -> pakai NOCTI_HAL_I2C_DEFAULT_SCL_WAIT_US
    uint32_t scl_wait_us;
} nocti_hal_i2c_cfg_t;

typedef struct {
    uint8_t addr_7bit;
    void *dev_handle;   // i2c_master_dev_handle_t
    bool in_use;
} nocti_hal_i2c_dev_cache_t;

typedef struct {
    i2c_port_t i2c_port;
    int gpio_sda;
    int gpio_scl;
    uint32_t clk_speed_hz;

    SemaphoreHandle_t mutex;

    bool delete_driver_on_deinit;
    bool installed;

    void *bus_handle;   // i2c_master_bus_handle_t

    nocti_hal_i2c_dev_cache_t dev_cache[NOCTI_HAL_I2C_MAX_DEV_CACHE];

    uint32_t default_timeout_ms;
    uint32_t trans_queue_depth;
    uint32_t scl_wait_us;
} nocti_hal_i2c_t;

// Lifecycle
esp_err_t nocti_hal_i2c_init(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h, const nocti_hal_i2c_cfg_t *cfg);
esp_err_t nocti_hal_i2c_deinit(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h);

// Primitives
esp_err_t nocti_hal_i2c_write(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h,
                             uint8_t dev_addr_7bit,
                             const uint8_t *data, size_t len,
                             uint32_t timeout_ms);

esp_err_t nocti_hal_i2c_read(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h,
                            uint8_t dev_addr_7bit,
                            uint8_t *data, size_t len,
                            uint32_t timeout_ms);

esp_err_t nocti_hal_i2c_write_then_read(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h,
                                       uint8_t dev_addr_7bit,
                                       const uint8_t *tx, size_t tx_len,
                                       uint8_t *rx, size_t rx_len,
                                       uint32_t timeout_ms);

                                  uint8_t dev_addr_7bit,
                                  uint8_t reg,
                                  const uint8_t *data, size_t len,
                                  uint32_t timeout_ms);

esp_err_t nocti_hal_i2c_read_reg8(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h,
                                 uint8_t dev_addr_7bit,
                                 uint8_t reg,
                                 uint8_t *data, size_t len,
                                 uint32_t timeout_ms);

esp_err_t nocti_hal_i2c_write_reg16(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h,
                                   uint8_t dev_addr_7bit,
                                   uint16_t reg,
                                   const uint8_t *data, size_t len,
                                   uint32_t timeout_ms);

esp_err_t nocti_hal_i2c_read_reg16(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h,
                                  uint8_t dev_addr_7bit,
                                  uint16_t reg,
                                  uint8_t *data, size_t len,
                                  uint32_t timeout_ms);

                               uint8_t dev_addr_7bit,

                               uint32_t timeout_ms);

                                uint8_t dev_addr_7bit,
                                uint8_t reg,
                                uint8_t val,
                                uint32_t timeout_ms);

#ifdef __cplusplus

// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_hal_i2c\nocti_hal_i2c.c ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_hal_i2c/nocti_hal_i2c.c
// Rewrite fokus stabilitas
// ============================================================

#include "nocti_hal_i2c.h"

#include <string.h>

#include "driver/i2c_master.h"
#include "driver/gpio.h"
#include "freertos/FreeRTOS.h"
#include "freertos/semphr.h"

#include "nocti_log.h"

static const char *TAG = "nocti_hal_i2c";

static inline bool is_addr_7bit_valid(uint8_t a) { return (a <= 0x7Fu); }

static inline bool handle_ok(const nocti_hal_i2c_t *h)
{
    return (h && h->installed && h->mutex && h->bus_handle);
}

static inline bool port_ok(i2c_port_t port)
{
    return (port == I2C_NUM_0) || (port == I2C_NUM_1);
}

static inline uint32_t pick_timeout_ms(const nocti_hal_i2c_t *h, uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    uint32_t t = timeout_ms ? timeout_ms : h->default_timeout_ms;
    return (t == 0) ? 1u : t;
}

static void dev_cache_reset(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h)
{
    for (int i = 0; i < NOCTI_HAL_I2C_MAX_DEV_CACHE; i++) {
        h->dev_cache[i].addr_7bit = 0;
        h->dev_cache[i].dev_handle = NULL;
        h->dev_cache[i].in_use = false;
    }
}

static void dev_cache_remove_all(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h)
{
    if (!h || !h->bus_handle) return;

    for (int i = 0; i < NOCTI_HAL_I2C_MAX_DEV_CACHE; i++) {
        if (h->dev_cache[i].in_use && h->dev_cache[i].dev_handle) {
            (void)i2c_master_bus_rm_device((i2c_master_dev_handle_t)h->dev_cache[i].dev_handle);
        }
        h->dev_cache[i].addr_7bit = 0;
        h->dev_cache[i].dev_handle = NULL;
        h->dev_cache[i].in_use = false;
    }
}

static esp_err_t dev_cache_get_or_add(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h,
                                      uint8_t addr_7bit,
                                      i2c_master_dev_handle_t *out_dev)
{
    if (!handle_ok(h) || !out_dev) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!is_addr_7bit_valid(addr_7bit)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    for (int i = 0; i < NOCTI_HAL_I2C_MAX_DEV_CACHE; i++) {
        if (h->dev_cache[i].in_use && h->dev_cache[i].addr_7bit == addr_7bit) {
            *out_dev = (i2c_master_dev_handle_t)h->dev_cache[i].dev_handle;
            return ESP_OK;
        }
    }

    i2c_device_config_t dev_cfg;
    memset(&dev_cfg, 0, sizeof(dev_cfg));
    dev_cfg.dev_addr_length = I2C_ADDR_BIT_LEN_7;
    dev_cfg.device_address  = addr_7bit;
    dev_cfg.scl_speed_hz    = h->clk_speed_hz;
    dev_cfg.scl_wait_us     = h->scl_wait_us;

    i2c_master_dev_handle_t dev = NULL;
    esp_err_t err = i2c_master_bus_add_device((i2c_master_bus_handle_t)h->bus_handle, &dev_cfg, &dev);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    for (int i = 0; i < NOCTI_HAL_I2C_MAX_DEV_CACHE; i++) {
        if (!h->dev_cache[i].in_use) {
            h->dev_cache[i].addr_7bit = addr_7bit;
            h->dev_cache[i].dev_handle = (void *)dev;
            h->dev_cache[i].in_use = true;
            *out_dev = dev;
            return ESP_OK;
        }
    }

    (void)i2c_master_bus_rm_device(dev);
    return ESP_ERR_NO_MEM;
}

// slash: recovery sederhana kalau bus kebetulan nyangkut setelah reset device
static void i2c_gpio_recover(int gpio_scl, int gpio_sda)
{
    if (gpio_scl < 0 || gpio_sda < 0) return;

    gpio_config_t io = {0};
    io.mode = GPIO_MODE_INPUT_OUTPUT_OD;
    io.pin_bit_mask = (1ULL << gpio_scl) | (1ULL << gpio_sda);
    io.pull_up_en = GPIO_PULLUP_DISABLE;
    io.pull_down_en = GPIO_PULLDOWN_DISABLE;
    (void)gpio_config(&io);

    (void)gpio_set_level(gpio_scl, 1);
    (void)gpio_set_level(gpio_sda, 1);
    esp_rom_delay_us(5);

    int sda = gpio_get_level(gpio_sda);
    if (sda == 0) {
        for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
            (void)gpio_set_level(gpio_scl, 0);
            esp_rom_delay_us(5);
            (void)gpio_set_level(gpio_scl, 1);
            esp_rom_delay_us(5);
        }
        (void)gpio_set_level(gpio_sda, 0);
        esp_rom_delay_us(5);
        (void)gpio_set_level(gpio_scl, 1);
        esp_rom_delay_us(5);
        (void)gpio_set_level(gpio_sda, 1);
        esp_rom_delay_us(5);
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Init and deinit
// ------------------------------------------------------------

esp_err_t nocti_hal_i2c_init(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h, const nocti_hal_i2c_cfg_t *cfg)
{
    if (!h || !cfg) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!port_ok(cfg->i2c_port)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (cfg->clk_speed_hz == 0) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (cfg->gpio_sda < 0 || cfg->gpio_scl < 0) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    memset(h, 0, sizeof(*h));
    h->i2c_port = cfg->i2c_port;
    h->gpio_sda = cfg->gpio_sda;
    h->gpio_scl = cfg->gpio_scl;
    h->clk_speed_hz = cfg->clk_speed_hz;
    h->delete_driver_on_deinit = cfg->delete_driver_on_deinit;

    h->default_timeout_ms = cfg->default_timeout_ms ? cfg->default_timeout_ms : NOCTI_HAL_I2C_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS;

    // slash: paksa default stabil
    h->trans_queue_depth = (cfg->trans_queue_depth > 256u) ? 0u : cfg->trans_queue_depth;
    h->scl_wait_us = cfg->scl_wait_us ? cfg->scl_wait_us : NOCTI_HAL_I2C_DEFAULT_SCL_WAIT_US;

    h->mutex = xSemaphoreCreateMutex();
    if (!h->mutex) {
        memset(h, 0, sizeof(*h));
        return ESP_ERR_NO_MEM;
    }

    dev_cache_reset(h);

    // slash: optional recover sebelum bus dibuat
    i2c_gpio_recover(cfg->gpio_scl, cfg->gpio_sda);

    i2c_master_bus_config_t bus_cfg;
    memset(&bus_cfg, 0, sizeof(bus_cfg));
    bus_cfg.i2c_port = cfg->i2c_port;
    bus_cfg.sda_io_num = cfg->gpio_sda;
    bus_cfg.scl_io_num = cfg->gpio_scl;
    bus_cfg.clk_source = I2C_CLK_SRC_DEFAULT;
    bus_cfg.glitch_ignore_cnt = 7;
    bus_cfg.intr_priority = 0;

    // slash: kunci ke mode sinkron jika trans_queue_depth = 0
    bus_cfg.trans_queue_depth = h->trans_queue_depth;

    bus_cfg.flags.enable_internal_pullup = cfg->enable_internal_pullups ? 1 : 0;

    i2c_master_bus_handle_t bus = NULL;
    esp_err_t err = i2c_new_master_bus(&bus_cfg, &bus);
    if (err != ESP_OK) {
        vSemaphoreDelete(h->mutex);
        memset(h, 0, sizeof(*h));
        return err;
    }

    h->bus_handle = (void *)bus;
    h->installed = true;

    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "init ok port=%d clk=%u def_to=%u qdepth=%u scl_wait_us=%u",
               (int)h->i2c_port,
               (unsigned)h->clk_speed_hz,
               (unsigned)h->default_timeout_ms,
               (unsigned)h->trans_queue_depth,
               (unsigned)h->scl_wait_us);

    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t nocti_hal_i2c_deinit(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h)
{
    if (!h) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    if (h->installed && h->bus_handle) {
        dev_cache_remove_all(h);
        if (h->delete_driver_on_deinit) {
            (void)i2c_del_master_bus((i2c_master_bus_handle_t)h->bus_handle);
        }
    }

    if (h->mutex) {
        vSemaphoreDelete(h->mutex);
        h->mutex = NULL;
    }

    memset(h, 0, sizeof(*h));
    return ESP_OK;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Lock and unlock
// ------------------------------------------------------------

esp_err_t nocti_hal_i2c_lock(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h, uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    if (!handle_ok(h)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    uint32_t to_ms = pick_timeout_ms(h, timeout_ms);
    TickType_t ticks = pdMS_TO_TICKS(to_ms);

    if (xSemaphoreTake(h->mutex, ticks) != pdTRUE) return ESP_ERR_TIMEOUT;
    return ESP_OK;
}

void nocti_hal_i2c_unlock(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h)
{
    if (!handle_ok(h)) return;
    (void)xSemaphoreGive(h->mutex);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Probe
// ------------------------------------------------------------

static esp_err_t probe_via_tmp_dev(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h, uint8_t addr_7bit, uint32_t to_ms)
{
    i2c_device_config_t dev_cfg;
    memset(&dev_cfg, 0, sizeof(dev_cfg));
    dev_cfg.dev_addr_length = I2C_ADDR_BIT_LEN_7;
    dev_cfg.device_address  = addr_7bit;
    dev_cfg.scl_speed_hz    = h->clk_speed_hz;
    dev_cfg.scl_wait_us     = h->scl_wait_us;

    i2c_master_dev_handle_t dev = NULL;
    esp_err_t err = i2c_master_bus_add_device((i2c_master_bus_handle_t)h->bus_handle, &dev_cfg, &dev);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    uint8_t dummy = 0x00;
    err = i2c_master_transmit(dev, &dummy, 1, to_ms);
    (void)i2c_master_bus_rm_device(dev);
    return err;
}

esp_err_t nocti_hal_i2c_probe(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h, uint8_t dev_addr_7bit, uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    if (!handle_ok(h)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!is_addr_7bit_valid(dev_addr_7bit)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    esp_err_t err = nocti_hal_i2c_lock(h, timeout_ms);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    uint32_t to_ms = pick_timeout_ms(h, timeout_ms);

    // slash: kalau qdepth non nol dan driver masuk jalur async, probe API bisa bermasalah
    if (h->trans_queue_depth == 0) {
        err = i2c_master_probe((i2c_master_bus_handle_t)h->bus_handle, dev_addr_7bit, to_ms);
        if (err != ESP_OK) {
            // fallback kalau probe tidak cocok di versi tertentu
            err = probe_via_tmp_dev(h, dev_addr_7bit, to_ms);
        }
    } else {
        err = probe_via_tmp_dev(h, dev_addr_7bit, to_ms);
    }

    nocti_hal_i2c_unlock(h);
    return err;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Primitives
// ------------------------------------------------------------

esp_err_t nocti_hal_i2c_write(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h,
                             uint8_t dev_addr_7bit,
                             const uint8_t *data, size_t len,
                             uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    if (!handle_ok(h)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!is_addr_7bit_valid(dev_addr_7bit)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (len > 0 && !data) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (len == 0) return ESP_OK;

    esp_err_t err = nocti_hal_i2c_lock(h, timeout_ms);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    uint32_t to_ms = pick_timeout_ms(h, timeout_ms);

    i2c_master_dev_handle_t dev = NULL;
    err = dev_cache_get_or_add(h, dev_addr_7bit, &dev);
    if (err == ESP_OK) err = i2c_master_transmit(dev, data, len, to_ms);

    nocti_hal_i2c_unlock(h);
    return err;
}

esp_err_t nocti_hal_i2c_read(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h,
                            uint8_t dev_addr_7bit,
                            uint8_t *data, size_t len,
                            uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    if (!handle_ok(h)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!is_addr_7bit_valid(dev_addr_7bit)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (len > 0 && !data) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (len == 0) return ESP_OK;

    esp_err_t err = nocti_hal_i2c_lock(h, timeout_ms);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    uint32_t to_ms = pick_timeout_ms(h, timeout_ms);

    i2c_master_dev_handle_t dev = NULL;
    err = dev_cache_get_or_add(h, dev_addr_7bit, &dev);
    if (err == ESP_OK) err = i2c_master_receive(dev, data, len, to_ms);

    nocti_hal_i2c_unlock(h);
    return err;
}

esp_err_t nocti_hal_i2c_write_then_read(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h,
