                                       uint8_t dev_addr_7bit,
                                       const uint8_t *tx, size_t tx_len,
                                       uint8_t *rx, size_t rx_len,
                                       uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    if (!handle_ok(h)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!is_addr_7bit_valid(dev_addr_7bit)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (tx_len > 0 && !tx) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (rx_len > 0 && !rx) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (tx_len == 0 && rx_len == 0) return ESP_OK;

    esp_err_t err = nocti_hal_i2c_lock(h, timeout_ms);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    uint32_t to_ms = pick_timeout_ms(h, timeout_ms);

    i2c_master_dev_handle_t dev = NULL;
    err = dev_cache_get_or_add(h, dev_addr_7bit, &dev);
    if (err == ESP_OK) err = i2c_master_transmit_receive(dev, tx, tx_len, rx, rx_len, to_ms);

    nocti_hal_i2c_unlock(h);
    return err;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Register helpers
// ------------------------------------------------------------

esp_err_t nocti_hal_i2c_write_reg8(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h,
                                  uint8_t dev_addr_7bit,
                                  uint8_t reg,
                                  const uint8_t *data, size_t len,
                                  uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    if (!handle_ok(h)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!is_addr_7bit_valid(dev_addr_7bit)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (len > 0 && !data) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    uint8_t buf[1 + 64];
    if (len > 64) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_SIZE;

    buf[0] = reg;
    if (len) memcpy(&buf[1], data, len);

    return nocti_hal_i2c_write(h, dev_addr_7bit, buf, 1 + len, timeout_ms);
}

esp_err_t nocti_hal_i2c_read_reg8(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h,
                                 uint8_t dev_addr_7bit,
                                 uint8_t reg,
                                 uint8_t *data, size_t len,
                                 uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    if (!handle_ok(h)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!is_addr_7bit_valid(dev_addr_7bit)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (len > 0 && !data) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    return nocti_hal_i2c_write_then_read(h, dev_addr_7bit, &reg, 1, data, len, timeout_ms);
}

esp_err_t nocti_hal_i2c_write_reg16(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h,
                                   uint8_t dev_addr_7bit,
                                   uint16_t reg,
                                   const uint8_t *data, size_t len,
                                   uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    if (!handle_ok(h)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!is_addr_7bit_valid(dev_addr_7bit)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (len > 0 && !data) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    uint8_t buf[2 + 64];
    if (len > 64) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_SIZE;

    buf[0] = (uint8_t)((reg >> 8) & 0xFFu);
    buf[1] = (uint8_t)(reg & 0xFFu);
    if (len) memcpy(&buf[2], data, len);

    return nocti_hal_i2c_write(h, dev_addr_7bit, buf, 2 + len, timeout_ms);
}

esp_err_t nocti_hal_i2c_read_reg16(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h,
                                  uint8_t dev_addr_7bit,
                                  uint16_t reg,
                                  uint8_t *data, size_t len,
                                  uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    if (!handle_ok(h)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!is_addr_7bit_valid(dev_addr_7bit)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (len > 0 && !data) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    uint8_t a[2];
    a[0] = (uint8_t)((reg >> 8) & 0xFFu);
    a[1] = (uint8_t)(reg & 0xFFu);

    return nocti_hal_i2c_write_then_read(h, dev_addr_7bit, a, 2, data, len, timeout_ms);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Convenience u8
// ------------------------------------------------------------

esp_err_t nocti_hal_i2c_read_u8(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h,
                               uint8_t dev_addr_7bit,
                               uint8_t reg,
                               uint8_t *val,
                               uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    if (!val) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    return nocti_hal_i2c_read_reg8(h, dev_addr_7bit, reg, val, 1, timeout_ms);
}

esp_err_t nocti_hal_i2c_write_u8(nocti_hal_i2c_t *h,
                                uint8_t dev_addr_7bit,
                                uint8_t reg,
                                uint8_t val,
                                uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    return nocti_hal_i2c_write_reg8(h, dev_addr_7bit, reg, &val, 1, timeout_ms);
}


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_hal_spi\include\nocti_hal_spi.h ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_hal_spi/include/nocti_hal_spi.h
// ============================================================

#pragma once
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>

#include "esp_err.h"
#include "driver/spi_master.h"

#include "freertos/FreeRTOS.h"
#include "freertos/semphr.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

// ============================================================
// Konfigurasi SPI untuk satu bus + satu device handle
// Pin tidak dihardcode. Nanti nocti_board yang isi pin.
// // belajar: untuk NoctiNIRS, ADS1299 dan ADS131M04 wajib beda host
// // belajar: karena SCLK MOSI MISO-nya beda total, jadi bukan sekadar beda CS
// ============================================================

typedef struct {
    spi_host_device_t host;   // contoh SPI2_HOST atau SPI3_HOST (ESP32-S3)

    // Bus pins
    int gpio_mosi;
    int gpio_miso;
    int gpio_sclk;

    // Device pin
    int gpio_cs;              // GPIO CS, atau -1 kalau CS di-handle manual

    // SPI timing
    uint32_t clock_hz;        // contoh 2000000 sampai 4000000
    uint8_t spi_mode;         // 0..3
    uint8_t queue_size;       // default 1 atau 2

    // DMA
    bool use_dma;             // true untuk DMA, false untuk non DMA

    // Optional tuning
    int input_delay_ns;       // set -1 kalau tidak dipakai

    // Bus lifetime
    bool free_bus_on_deinit;  // true: deinit juga free bus, false: hanya remove device
} nocti_hal_spi_cfg_t;

// ============================================================
// Handle runtime
// ============================================================

typedef struct {
    spi_host_device_t host;
    spi_device_handle_t dev;

    int gpio_cs;
    bool use_dma;
    bool free_bus_on_deinit;

    // // belajar: kalau bus sudah di-init oleh module lain, kita tidak "own" bus itu
    // // belajar: artinya deinit tidak boleh spi_bus_free walaupun cfg minta free
    bool bus_owned;

    SemaphoreHandle_t mutex;  // // belajar: melindungi transaksi supaya tidak overlap
} nocti_hal_spi_t;

// ============================================================
// Init, deinit
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_hal_spi_init(nocti_hal_spi_t *h, const nocti_hal_spi_cfg_t *cfg);
esp_err_t nocti_hal_spi_deinit(nocti_hal_spi_t *h);

// ============================================================
// Lock, unlock
// timeout_ms: 0 berarti tunggu selamanya (stand-alone style)
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_hal_spi_lock(nocti_hal_spi_t *h, uint32_t timeout_ms);
void      nocti_hal_spi_unlock(nocti_hal_spi_t *h);

// ============================================================
// Transfer utama
// Full duplex dalam satu transaksi SPI (idealnya 1 CS)
// Cocok untuk device berbasis frame seperti ADS131M04.
// tx atau rx boleh NULL.
// // belajar: implementasi akan meng-chunk jika len_bytes besar.
// // belajar: kalau chunk terjadi dan CS dikelola driver, CS bisa toggle di tiap chunk.
// // belajar: untuk ADC frame sensitif CS, pakai transfer_contig dan pastikan len_bytes kecil.
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_hal_spi_transfer(nocti_hal_spi_t *h,
                                const void *tx, void *rx,
                                size_t len_bytes,
                                uint32_t timeout_ms);

// ============================================================
// Transfer kontigu (paksa 1 transaksi SPI saja)
// slash: cocok untuk ADC frame yang sensitif terhadap CS toggling.
// note: len_bytes harus <= max_transfer_sz internal.
// note: untuk read-only kontigu, siapkan tx buffer berisi nol (tx tidak boleh NULL).
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_hal_spi_transfer_contig(nocti_hal_spi_t *h,
                                       const void *tx, void *rx,
                                       size_t len_bytes,
                                       uint32_t timeout_ms);

// ============================================================
// Helper convenience
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_hal_spi_write(nocti_hal_spi_t *h,
                             const void *tx, size_t len_bytes,
                             uint32_t timeout_ms);

esp_err_t nocti_hal_spi_read(nocti_hal_spi_t *h,
                            void *rx, size_t len_bytes,
                            uint32_t timeout_ms);

// ============================================================
// Write lalu read (dua transaksi)
// Perhatian: ini akan menaikkan CS di antara transaksi tergantung driver.
// Jangan gunakan untuk device yang butuh frame kontigu dalam satu CS.
// Untuk ADS131M04, pakai nocti_hal_spi_transfer dan kirim seluruh frame sekaligus.
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_hal_spi_write_then_read(nocti_hal_spi_t *h,
                                       const void *tx, size_t tx_len,
                                       void *rx, size_t rx_len,
                                       uint32_t timeout_ms);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_hal_spi\nocti_hal_spi.c ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_hal_spi/nocti_hal_spi.c
// ============================================================

#include "nocti_hal_spi.h"

#include <string.h>

#include "nocti_log.h"

static const char *TAG = "nocti_hal_spi";

static inline TickType_t ms_to_ticks(uint32_t ms)
{
    // /fix: kontrak API kita: timeout_ms = 0 artinya tunggu selamanya.
    // FreeRTOS: portMAX_DELAY dipakai untuk block tanpa batas.
    return (ms == 0) ? portMAX_DELAY : pdMS_TO_TICKS(ms);
}

// // batas aman transfer, harus selaras dengan buscfg.max_transfer_sz
#define NOCTI_SPI_MAX_TRANSFER_SZ   (256)

// // belajar: buffer kecil untuk clock-out nol saat read-only
#define NOCTI_SPI_ZEROS_CHUNK_SZ    (32)

// ============================================================
// Init SPI bus dan tambah satu device handle
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_hal_spi_init(nocti_hal_spi_t *h, const nocti_hal_spi_cfg_t *cfg)
{
    if (!h || !cfg) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    // // validasi minimal pin bus
    if (cfg->gpio_mosi < 0 || cfg->gpio_miso < 0 || cfg->gpio_sclk < 0) {
        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    }

    memset(h, 0, sizeof(*h));
    h->host = cfg->host;
    h->gpio_cs = cfg->gpio_cs;
    h->use_dma = cfg->use_dma;
    h->free_bus_on_deinit = cfg->free_bus_on_deinit;
    h->bus_owned = false;

    h->mutex = xSemaphoreCreateMutex();
    if (!h->mutex) return ESP_ERR_NO_MEM;

    spi_bus_config_t buscfg = {
        .mosi_io_num = cfg->gpio_mosi,
        .miso_io_num = cfg->gpio_miso,
        .sclk_io_num = cfg->gpio_sclk,
        .quadwp_io_num = -1,
        .quadhd_io_num = -1,

        // // belajar: max_transfer_sz membatasi ukuran transaksi, transfer besar harus di-chunk
        .max_transfer_sz = NOCTI_SPI_MAX_TRANSFER_SZ,
    };

    int dma_chan = cfg->use_dma ? SPI_DMA_CH_AUTO : SPI_DMA_DISABLED;

    esp_err_t err = spi_bus_initialize(cfg->host, &buscfg, dma_chan);

    if (err == ESP_OK) {
        h->bus_owned = true;
    } else if (err == ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE) {
        // // bus sudah di-init pihak lain, kita tidak own
        // // belajar: pins dan max_transfer_sz harus sama dengan init pertama pada host itu
        h->bus_owned = false;
        NOCTI_LOGW(TAG,
                 "spi_bus_initialize: bus already initialized. pins and max_transfer_sz must match existing init");
    } else {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "spi_bus_initialize failed: %s", esp_err_to_name(err));
        vSemaphoreDelete(h->mutex);
        memset(h, 0, sizeof(*h));
        return err;
    }

    spi_device_interface_config_t devcfg = {
        .clock_speed_hz = (int)cfg->clock_hz,
        .mode = cfg->spi_mode,
        .spics_io_num = cfg->gpio_cs, // // -1 boleh, berarti CS manual di luar

        .queue_size = (cfg->queue_size == 0) ? 1 : cfg->queue_size,

        .command_bits = 0,
        .address_bits = 0,
        .dummy_bits = 0,

        .duty_cycle_pos = 128,
        // Tambah guard kecil sebelum dan sesudah transaksi.
        // Ini meniru setting stand-alone ADS131 bring-up dan membantu device yang
        // butuh setup/hold di sekitar CS.
        .cs_ena_pretrans = 1,
        .cs_ena_posttrans = 1,

        .input_delay_ns = (cfg->input_delay_ns < 0) ? 0 : cfg->input_delay_ns,
    };

    err = spi_bus_add_device(cfg->host, &devcfg, &h->dev);
    if (err != ESP_OK) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "spi_bus_add_device failed: %s", esp_err_to_name(err));

        if (h->bus_owned) {
            (void)spi_bus_free(cfg->host);
        }

        vSemaphoreDelete(h->mutex);
        memset(h, 0, sizeof(*h));
        return err;
    }

    return ESP_OK;
}

// ============================================================
// Deinit
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_hal_spi_deinit(nocti_hal_spi_t *h)
{
    if (!h) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!h->dev) return ESP_OK;

    esp_err_t err = spi_bus_remove_device(h->dev);
    if (err != ESP_OK) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "spi_bus_remove_device failed: %s", esp_err_to_name(err));
        return err;
    }

    h->dev = NULL;

    if (h->free_bus_on_deinit && h->bus_owned) {
        // // belajar: spi_bus_free aman kalau host itu memang hanya dipakai instance ini
        err = spi_bus_free(h->host);
        if (err != ESP_OK) {
            NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "spi_bus_free failed: %s", esp_err_to_name(err));
            return err;
        }
    }

    if (h->mutex) {
        vSemaphoreDelete(h->mutex);
        h->mutex = NULL;
    }

    memset(h, 0, sizeof(*h));
    return ESP_OK;
}

// ============================================================
// Lock, unlock
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_hal_spi_lock(nocti_hal_spi_t *h, uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    if (!h || !h->mutex) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    if (xSemaphoreTake(h->mutex, ms_to_ticks(timeout_ms)) != pdTRUE) {
        return ESP_ERR_TIMEOUT;
    }
    return ESP_OK;
}

void nocti_hal_spi_unlock(nocti_hal_spi_t *h)
{
    if (!h || !h->mutex) return;
    (void)xSemaphoreGive(h->mutex);
}

// ============================================================
// Transfer utama
// // belajar: selalu chunk supaya tidak nabrak max_transfer_sz dan lebih stabil untuk frame panjang
// ============================================================

static esp_err_t transfer_locked(nocti_hal_spi_t *h,
                                const void *tx, void *rx,
                                size_t len_bytes)
{
    if (!h || !h->dev) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
    if (len_bytes == 0) return ESP_OK;

    const uint8_t *tx8 = (const uint8_t *)tx;
    uint8_t *rx8 = (uint8_t *)rx;

    // /note: buffer nol kecil untuk kasus read-only (tx null).
    // Untuk transfer kontigu besar, minta caller kirim tx berisi nol sendiri.
    uint8_t zeros[NOCTI_SPI_ZEROS_CHUNK_SZ];
    memset(zeros, 0, sizeof(zeros));

    size_t done = 0;
    esp_err_t err = ESP_OK;

    while (done < len_bytes && err == ESP_OK) {
        size_t chunk = len_bytes - done;
        if (chunk > NOCTI_SPI_MAX_TRANSFER_SZ) chunk = NOCTI_SPI_MAX_TRANSFER_SZ;

        spi_transaction_t t;
        memset(&t, 0, sizeof(t));
        t.length = chunk * 8;

        if (tx8) {
            t.tx_buffer = tx8 + done;
        } else {
            // /note: kalau tx null, clock out nol.
            // zeros cuma 32 byte, jadi read-only akan ter-chunk.
            if (chunk > sizeof(zeros)) chunk = sizeof(zeros);
            t.length = chunk * 8;
            t.tx_buffer = zeros;
        }

        t.rx_buffer = rx8 ? (rx8 + done) : NULL;

        err = spi_device_transmit(h->dev, &t);
        done += chunk;
    }

    return err;
}

// ============================================================
// Transfer kontigu (paksa 1 transaksi SPI saja)
// /fix: versi ini tidak mengubah chunk untuk tx NULL.
// /note: untuk read-only kontigu, caller wajib menyiapkan tx buffer berisi nol.
// ============================================================

static esp_err_t transfer_contig_locked(nocti_hal_spi_t *h,
                                       const void *tx, void *rx,
                                       size_t len_bytes)
{
    if (!h || !h->dev) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
    if (len_bytes == 0) return ESP_OK;
    if (len_bytes > NOCTI_SPI_MAX_TRANSFER_SZ) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_SIZE;

    // /safe: agar benar-benar kontigu, tx wajib ada.
    // Kalau butuh read-only, kirim tx buffer nol dari caller.
    if (!tx) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    spi_transaction_t t;
    memset(&t, 0, sizeof(t));
    t.length = len_bytes * 8;
    t.tx_buffer = tx;
    t.rx_buffer = rx;

    // /jitter: polling_transmit cenderung lebih deterministik untuk frame kecil.
    return spi_device_polling_transmit(h->dev, &t);
}

static esp_err_t read_locked(nocti_hal_spi_t *h, void *rx, size_t len_bytes)
{
    if (!h || !h->dev || !rx) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (len_bytes == 0) return ESP_OK;

    // // reuse transfer_locked dengan tx null
    return transfer_locked(h, NULL, rx, len_bytes);
}

esp_err_t nocti_hal_spi_transfer(nocti_hal_spi_t *h,
                                const void *tx, void *rx,
                                size_t len_bytes,
                                uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    if (!h) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    esp_err_t err = nocti_hal_spi_lock(h, timeout_ms);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    err = transfer_locked(h, tx, rx, len_bytes);

    nocti_hal_spi_unlock(h);
    return err;
}

// ============================================================
// Transfer kontigu (paksa 1 transaksi SPI)
// /note: timeout_ms hanya dipakai untuk mutex lock.
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_hal_spi_transfer_contig(nocti_hal_spi_t *h,
                                       const void *tx, void *rx,
                                       size_t len_bytes,
                                       uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    if (!h) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    esp_err_t err = nocti_hal_spi_lock(h, timeout_ms);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    err = transfer_contig_locked(h, tx, rx, len_bytes);

    nocti_hal_spi_unlock(h);
    return err;
}

// ============================================================
// Helper write
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_hal_spi_write(nocti_hal_spi_t *h,
                             const void *tx, size_t len_bytes,
                             uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    return nocti_hal_spi_transfer(h, tx, NULL, len_bytes, timeout_ms);
}

// ============================================================
// Helper read
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_hal_spi_read(nocti_hal_spi_t *h,
                            void *rx, size_t len_bytes,
                            uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    if (!h || !rx) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    esp_err_t err = nocti_hal_spi_lock(h, timeout_ms);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    err = read_locked(h, rx, len_bytes);

    nocti_hal_spi_unlock(h);
    return err;
}

// ============================================================
// Write then read (dua transaksi)
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_hal_spi_write_then_read(nocti_hal_spi_t *h,
                                       const void *tx, size_t tx_len,
                                       void *rx, size_t rx_len,
                                       uint32_t timeout_ms)
{
    if (!h) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (tx_len == 0 && rx_len == 0) return ESP_OK;

    esp_err_t err = nocti_hal_spi_lock(h, timeout_ms);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    if (tx_len) {
        err = transfer_locked(h, tx, NULL, tx_len);
    }

    if (err == ESP_OK && rx_len) {
        err = read_locked(h, rx, rx_len);
    }

    nocti_hal_spi_unlock(h);
    return err;
}



// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_led_driver\include\nocti_led_driver.h ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_led_driver/include/nocti_led_driver.h
// ============================================================

// nocti_led_driver.h
#pragma once
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>

#include "esp_err.h"
#include "nocti_types.h"     // slash: nocti_wavelength_t dan konstanta umum
#include "nocti_hal_i2c.h"
#include "nocti_scheduler.h" // slash: nocti_slot_t untuk integrasi scheduler

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

// ============================================================
// NoctiNIRS LED Driver (PCA9956B)
//
// Konsep singkat
// - 10 sources x 2 wavelength = 20 slot
// - slot_index mengikuti urutan: led_id*2 + wl(735=0, 850=1)
// - PCA channel 10..13 gap (tidak boleh dipakai)
// ============================================================

#define NOCTI_LED_PCA_CHANNELS          (24u)
#define NOCTI_LED_SOURCES              (10u)
#define NOCTI_LED_WAVELENGTHS          (2u)
#define NOCTI_LED_SLOTS                (NOCTI_LED_SOURCES * NOCTI_LED_WAVELENGTHS)

#define NOCTI_LED_MAP_UNUSED           (0xFFu)

// // helper konsisten untuk semua modul
// // wl harus NOCTI_WL_735 atau NOCTI_WL_850
#define NOCTI_SLOT_INDEX(led_id, wl) \
    ((uint8_t)((uint8_t)(led_id) * 2u + ((wl) == NOCTI_WL_735 ? 0u : 1u)))

typedef struct {
    // I2C bus handle shared across devices
    nocti_hal_i2c_t *i2c;

    // PCA9956B 7-bit address (without bit R/W)
    uint8_t i2c_addr_7bit;

    // PCA9956B current scaling resistor (NoctiNIRS: 1000 ohm)
    uint32_t rext_ohm;

    // Optional mapping overrides
    // Any entry left as NOCTI_LED_MAP_UNUSED is ignored.
    // chan_map overrides apply after default map, before slot_to_pca overrides.
    uint8_t chan_map[NOCTI_LED_SOURCES][NOCTI_LED_WAVELENGTHS];
    uint8_t slot_to_pca[NOCTI_LED_SLOTS];

    // If true, start from default NoctiNIRS mapping described above
    bool use_nocti_default_map;

    // Default current applied at init for each wavelength (mA)
    uint16_t default_current_ma_735;
    uint16_t default_current_ma_850;

    // Safety clamp in mA, 0 means no clamp
    uint16_t max_current_ma;

    // If true, slot_begin forces OFF before ON (extra I2C but safer for bring-up)
    bool force_off_before_on;

    // If true, slot_begin also applies slot current via IREF update when needed
    // This increases I2C traffic inside the slot, so keep false for clean sampling
    bool apply_current_in_slot;

    // Jika true, driver akan melakukan cleanup defensif
    // slash: kalau scheduler miss memanggil slot_end (bug atau glitch), driver memastikan
    // slot berikutnya tidak menyalakan 2 LED sekaligus
    bool cleanup_missed_slot_end;

    // Jika true, init akan menulis register PWMALL = 0 dan GRPPWM/GRPFREQ default
    // slash: kita tidak pakai PWM sama sekali, ini hanya untuk memastikan state device bersih
    bool force_disable_pwm_regs;
} nocti_led_driver_cfg_t;

// Initialize cfg with safe defaults (mapping overrides set to NOCTI_LED_MAP_UNUSED)
void nocti_led_driver_cfg_set_defaults(nocti_led_driver_cfg_t *cfg);

// Initialize PCA9956B, set safe defaults, preload IREF, resolve mapping
esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_init(const nocti_led_driver_cfg_t *cfg);

// Turn all outputs off (LEDOUT all OFF). Does not change IREF.
esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_all_off(void);

// Set current for a PCA channel (mA). Writes IREF only if changed.
esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_set_channel_current_ma(uint8_t pca_ch, uint16_t ma);

// Set current for a logical LED (source id and wavelength). Writes IREF only if changed.
esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_set_logical_current_ma(uint8_t led_id, nocti_wavelength_t wl, uint16_t ma);

// Apply kalibrasi arus per SLOT sekali (20 nilai, urutan slot_index = led_id*2 + wl)
// slash: ini jalan tengah yang hemat, setiap LED boleh beda arus tapi write hanya 1 burst
esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_apply_slot_currents_ma(const uint16_t slot_ma[NOCTI_LED_SLOTS]);

// Turn on or off a PCA channel in "ON no PWM control" mode. Writes LEDOUT only if changed.
esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_channel_on(uint8_t pca_ch);
esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_channel_off(uint8_t pca_ch);

// Mapping helpers
esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_map_logical(uint8_t led_id, nocti_wavelength_t wl, uint8_t *out_pca_ch);
esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_map_slot(uint8_t slot_index, uint8_t *out_pca_ch);

// Debug helper: copy out resolved mapping slot 0 to 19 to caller
esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_get_resolved_slot_map(uint8_t out_slot_to_pca[NOCTI_LED_SLOTS]);

// Scheduler integration helpers
// Typical usage in scheduler callback:
// - on SLOT_BEGIN : nocti_led_driver_slot_begin(&ev->slot)
// - on SLOT_END   : nocti_led_driver_slot_end(&ev->slot)
esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_slot_begin(const nocti_slot_t *slot);
esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_slot_end(const nocti_slot_t *slot);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_led_driver\nocti_led_driver.c ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_led_driver/nocti_led_driver.c
// ============================================================

// nocti_led_driver.c
#include "nocti_led_driver.h"

#include <string.h>

#include "nocti_log.h"

// ============================================================
// PCA9956B registers used by this driver
// ============================================================
#define PCA9956_REG_MODE1     0x00
#define PCA9956_REG_MODE2     0x01

#define PCA9956_REG_LEDOUT0   0x02
#define PCA9956_REG_LEDOUT1   0x03
#define PCA9956_REG_LEDOUT2   0x04
#define PCA9956_REG_LEDOUT3   0x05
#define PCA9956_REG_LEDOUT4   0x06
#define PCA9956_REG_LEDOUT5   0x07

// slash: register PWM tidak dipakai untuk NoctiNIRS (CW), tapi kita bisa nol-kan sekali saat init
#define PCA9956_REG_GRPPWM    0x08
#define PCA9956_REG_GRPFREQ   0x09
#define PCA9956_REG_PWMALL    0x3F

#define PCA9956_REG_IREF0     0x22

// LEDOUT 2-bit field
#define PCA9956_LDR_OFF       0x0u
#define PCA9956_LDR_ON        0x1u

// MODE1 bits
#define PCA9956_MODE1_AI1     (1u << 6)
#define PCA9956_MODE1_AI0     (1u << 5)

// I2C timeout
#define PCA_I2C_TIMEOUT_MS    (50u)

static const char *TAG = "nocti_led";

// ============================================================
// Internal context
// ============================================================
typedef struct {
    nocti_led_driver_cfg_t cfg;
    bool inited;

    // Cache to reduce I2C writes
    uint8_t iref_cache[NOCTI_LED_PCA_CHANNELS];
    uint8_t ledout_cache[6];

    // Resolved mapping: slot 0..19 -> PCA channel
    uint8_t slot_map[NOCTI_LED_SLOTS];

    // Safety: track last ON channel (optional auto cleanup)
    uint8_t last_on_ch;
} led_ctx_t;

static led_ctx_t s;

// ============================================================
// Default NoctiNIRS mapping with unused gap on PCA ch 10..13
// slot  0.. 9 -> PCA  0.. 9
// slot 10..19 -> PCA 14..23
// ============================================================
static const uint8_t k_default_slot_to_pca[NOCTI_LED_SLOTS] = {
    0u, 1u, 2u, 3u, 4u, 5u, 6u, 7u, 8u, 9u,
    14u, 15u, 16u, 17u, 18u, 19u, 20u, 21u, 22u, 23u
};

// ============================================================
// Small helpers
// ============================================================

static uint16_t clamp_ma(uint16_t ma)
{
    // // max_current_ma = 0 berarti tidak ada clamp
    if (s.cfg.max_current_ma == 0) return ma;
    return (ma > s.cfg.max_current_ma) ? s.cfg.max_current_ma : ma;
}

static bool is_valid_pca_ch(uint8_t ch)
{
    return ch < NOCTI_LED_PCA_CHANNELS;
}

static bool is_gap_channel(uint8_t ch)
{
    // // PCA channels 10..13 tidak terhubung di PCB NoctiNIRS
    return (ch >= 10u) && (ch <= 13u);
}

// ============================================================
// Convert mA -> IREF (0..255)
// IO(uA) = IREF * 900000 / (4 * Rext(ohm))
// IREF   = IO(uA) * (4 * Rext) / 900000
// ============================================================
static uint8_t ma_to_iref(uint16_t ma, uint32_t rext_ohm)
{
    if (ma == 0) return 0;
    if (rext_ohm == 0) return 0;

    uint32_t io_uA = (uint32_t)ma * 1000u;

    uint64_t num = (uint64_t)io_uA * (uint64_t)(4u * rext_ohm);
    uint32_t den = 900000u;

    uint32_t iref = (uint32_t)((num + (den / 2u)) / den); // // rounded
    if (iref > 255u) iref = 255u;
    return (uint8_t)iref;
}

// ============================================================
// I2C helpers for PCA
// ============================================================

static esp_err_t pca_write_reg8(uint8_t reg, uint8_t val)
{
    return nocti_hal_i2c_write_u8(s.cfg.i2c, s.cfg.i2c_addr_7bit, reg, val, PCA_I2C_TIMEOUT_MS);
}

// Burst write helper (deterministic, no malloc)
// Control byte: AIF=1 enables auto-increment, then start register address.
// ============================================================
static esp_err_t pca_write_burst_ai(uint8_t start_reg, const uint8_t *data, uint16_t len)
{
    if (!data || len == 0) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    // // Untuk NoctiNIRS, burst maksimum yang dipakai driver ini adalah:
    // // - LEDOUT: 6 byte
    // // - IREF  : 24 byte
    // // Jadi buffer statik kecil sudah cukup.
    uint8_t buf[1 + NOCTI_LED_PCA_CHANNELS];
    if (len > NOCTI_LED_PCA_CHANNELS) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_SIZE;

    buf[0] = (uint8_t)(0x80u | (start_reg & 0x7Fu)); // // AIF=1 + start_reg
    memcpy(&buf[1], data, len);

    return nocti_hal_i2c_write(s.cfg.i2c,
                              s.cfg.i2c_addr_7bit,
                              buf,
                              (size_t)(1u + len),
                              PCA_I2C_TIMEOUT_MS);
}

// ============================================================
// LEDOUT cache update helper
// LEDOUT field per channel is 2-bit, grouped by 4 channels per register
// ============================================================
static inline void ledout_set_field(uint8_t ch, uint8_t ldr2b)
{
    uint8_t idx = (uint8_t)(ch / 4u);        // LEDOUT register index 0..5
    uint8_t pos = (uint8_t)((ch % 4u) * 2u); // bit position 0,2,4,6
    uint8_t mask = (uint8_t)(0x3u << pos);

    s.ledout_cache[idx] = (uint8_t)((s.ledout_cache[idx] & (uint8_t)(~mask)) |
                                   (uint8_t)((ldr2b & 0x3u) << pos));
}

static inline uint8_t ledout_get_field(uint8_t ch)
{
    uint8_t idx = (uint8_t)(ch / 4u);        // LEDOUT register index 0..5
    uint8_t pos = (uint8_t)((ch % 4u) * 2u); // bit position 0,2,4,6
    return (uint8_t)((s.ledout_cache[idx] >> pos) & 0x3u);
}

// ============================================================
// Resolve mapping: produce s.slot_map slot 0..19 -> PCA ch
// ============================================================
static esp_err_t resolve_slot_map_v2(void)
{
    // Start with all UNUSED
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < NOCTI_LED_SLOTS; i++) {
        s.slot_map[i] = NOCTI_LED_MAP_UNUSED;
    }

    // Base map
    if (s.cfg.use_nocti_default_map) {
        memcpy(s.slot_map, k_default_slot_to_pca, sizeof(s.slot_map));
    }

    // Override by chan_map
    for (uint8_t led = 0; led < NOCTI_LED_SOURCES; led++) {
        for (uint8_t w = 0; w < NOCTI_LED_WAVELENGTHS; w++) {
            uint8_t ch = s.cfg.chan_map[led][w];
            if (ch == NOCTI_LED_MAP_UNUSED) continue;

            if (!is_valid_pca_ch(ch) || is_gap_channel(ch)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

            uint8_t slot = (uint8_t)(led * 2u + w);
            s.slot_map[slot] = ch;
        }
    }

    // Override by slot_to_pca (highest priority)
    for (uint8_t slot = 0; slot < NOCTI_LED_SLOTS; slot++) {
        uint8_t ch = s.cfg.slot_to_pca[slot];
        if (ch == NOCTI_LED_MAP_UNUSED) continue;

        if (!is_valid_pca_ch(ch) || is_gap_channel(ch)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

        s.slot_map[slot] = ch;
    }

    // Ensure all slots resolved and valid
    for (uint8_t slot = 0; slot < NOCTI_LED_SLOTS; slot++) {
        uint8_t ch = s.slot_map[slot];
        if (ch == NOCTI_LED_MAP_UNUSED) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
        if (!is_valid_pca_ch(ch) || is_gap_channel(ch)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    }

    // Ensure no duplicates
    bool used[NOCTI_LED_PCA_CHANNELS];
    memset(used, 0, sizeof(used));

    for (uint8_t slot = 0; slot < NOCTI_LED_SLOTS; slot++) {
        uint8_t ch = s.slot_map[slot];
        if (used[ch]) {
            NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "duplicate mapping: slot %u shares PCA ch %u", (unsigned)slot, (unsigned)ch);
            return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
        }
        used[ch] = true;
    }

    return ESP_OK;
}

// Convert (slot struct) to slot_index 0..19
static esp_err_t slot_struct_to_index(const nocti_slot_t *slot, uint8_t *out_slot_index)
{
    if (!slot || !out_slot_index) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (slot->led_id >= NOCTI_LED_SOURCES) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    uint8_t wl_idx = (slot->wavelength == NOCTI_WL_735) ? 0u : 1u;
    *out_slot_index = (uint8_t)(slot->led_id * 2u + wl_idx);

    if (*out_slot_index >= NOCTI_LED_SLOTS) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    return ESP_OK;
}

// ============================================================
// Config helper
// ============================================================
void nocti_led_driver_cfg_set_defaults(nocti_led_driver_cfg_t *cfg)
{
    if (!cfg) return;

    memset(cfg, 0, sizeof(*cfg));

    // Mapping overrides disabled by default
    for (uint8_t led = 0; led < NOCTI_LED_SOURCES; led++) {
        for (uint8_t w = 0; w < NOCTI_LED_WAVELENGTHS; w++) {
            cfg->chan_map[led][w] = NOCTI_LED_MAP_UNUSED;
        }
    }
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < NOCTI_LED_SLOTS; i++) {
        cfg->slot_to_pca[i] = NOCTI_LED_MAP_UNUSED;
    }

    cfg->use_nocti_default_map = true;

    cfg->default_current_ma_735 = 0;
    cfg->default_current_ma_850 = 0;
    cfg->max_current_ma = 0;

    cfg->force_off_before_on = false;
    cfg->apply_current_in_slot = false; // // cocok untuk arus diset di awal

    cfg->cleanup_missed_slot_end = true;
    cfg->force_disable_pwm_regs = true;
}

// ============================================================
// Apply arus per slot sekali (kalibrasi)
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_apply_slot_currents_ma(const uint16_t slot_ma[NOCTI_LED_SLOTS])
{
    if (!s.inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
    if (!slot_ma) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    // slash: update cache IREF berdasarkan mapping slot -> PCA channel
    for (uint8_t slot = 0; slot < NOCTI_LED_SLOTS; slot++) {
        uint8_t ch = s.slot_map[slot];
        uint16_t ma = clamp_ma(slot_ma[slot]);
        s.iref_cache[ch] = ma_to_iref(ma, s.cfg.rext_ohm);
    }

    // slash: 1 burst write, hemat overhead start/stop I2C
    return pca_write_burst_ai(PCA9956_REG_IREF0, s.iref_cache, NOCTI_LED_PCA_CHANNELS);
}

// ============================================================
// Public API
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_init(const nocti_led_driver_cfg_t *cfg)
{
    if (!cfg) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!cfg->i2c) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (cfg->rext_ohm == 0) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    memset(&s, 0, sizeof(s));
    s.cfg = *cfg;
    s.last_on_ch = NOCTI_LED_MAP_UNUSED;

    // Resolve mapping
    esp_err_t err = resolve_slot_map_v2();
    if (err != ESP_OK) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "mapping resolve failed, err=%d", (int)err);
        return err;
    }

    // Probe device ACK before register writes
    err = nocti_hal_i2c_probe(s.cfg.i2c, s.cfg.i2c_addr_7bit, PCA_I2C_TIMEOUT_MS);
    if (err != ESP_OK) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "I2C probe failed for addr 0x%02X, err=%d",
                   (unsigned)s.cfg.i2c_addr_7bit, (int)err);
        return err;
    }

    // Prepare caches first (so we only write IREF once)
    memset(s.ledout_cache, 0, sizeof(s.ledout_cache)); // // all OFF
    memset(s.iref_cache, 0, sizeof(s.iref_cache));     // // all 0

    // Preload default currents for mapped channels
    uint16_t ma735 = clamp_ma(s.cfg.default_current_ma_735);
    uint16_t ma850 = clamp_ma(s.cfg.default_current_ma_850);

    for (uint8_t led = 0; led < NOCTI_LED_SOURCES; led++) {
        uint8_t slot_735 = (uint8_t)(led * 2u + 0u);
        uint8_t slot_850 = (uint8_t)(led * 2u + 1u);

        uint8_t ch_735 = s.slot_map[slot_735];
        uint8_t ch_850 = s.slot_map[slot_850];

        s.iref_cache[ch_735] = ma_to_iref(ma735, s.cfg.rext_ohm);
        s.iref_cache[ch_850] = ma_to_iref(ma850, s.cfg.rext_ohm);
    }

    // Configure MODE1 for auto increment
    // // konservatif: AI1=1, AI0=0
    uint8_t mode1 = (uint8_t)(PCA9956_MODE1_AI1);
    err = pca_write_reg8(PCA9956_REG_MODE1, mode1);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    // MODE2 deterministic default
    err = pca_write_reg8(PCA9956_REG_MODE2, 0x00);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    if (s.cfg.force_disable_pwm_regs) {
        // slash: GRPPWM/GRPFREQ hanya relevan kalau pakai group dimming, kita nol-kan saja
        err = pca_write_reg8(PCA9956_REG_GRPPWM, 0x00);
        if (err != ESP_OK) return err;
        err = pca_write_reg8(PCA9956_REG_GRPFREQ, 0x00);
        if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

        // slash: PWMALL mengatur semua PWMx sekaligus, nol-kan untuk memastikan tidak ada residu setting
        err = pca_write_reg8(PCA9956_REG_PWMALL, 0x00);
        if (err != ESP_OK) return err;
    }

    // Push safe state to device: LEDOUT then IREF (single write each)
    err = pca_write_burst_ai(PCA9956_REG_LEDOUT0, s.ledout_cache, 6);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    err = pca_write_burst_ai(PCA9956_REG_IREF0, s.iref_cache, NOCTI_LED_PCA_CHANNELS);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    s.inited = true;
    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "init ok addr=0x%02X", (unsigned)s.cfg.i2c_addr_7bit);
    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_all_off(void)
{
    if (!s.inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

    bool changed = false;
    for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
        if (s.ledout_cache[i] != 0x00) {
            s.ledout_cache[i] = 0x00;
            changed = true;
        }
    }

    // // safety: reset internal state
    s.last_on_ch = NOCTI_LED_MAP_UNUSED;

    if (!changed) return ESP_OK;
    return pca_write_burst_ai(PCA9956_REG_LEDOUT0, s.ledout_cache, 6);
}

esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_set_channel_current_ma(uint8_t pca_ch, uint16_t ma)
{
    if (!s.inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

    // Do not allow gap channels
    if (!is_valid_pca_ch(pca_ch) || is_gap_channel(pca_ch)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    ma = clamp_ma(ma);
    uint8_t iref = ma_to_iref(ma, s.cfg.rext_ohm);

    // Skip I2C if unchanged
    if (s.iref_cache[pca_ch] == iref) return ESP_OK;

    s.iref_cache[pca_ch] = iref;
    return pca_write_reg8((uint8_t)(PCA9956_REG_IREF0 + pca_ch), iref);
}

esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_set_logical_current_ma(uint8_t led_id, nocti_wavelength_t wl, uint16_t ma)
{
    uint8_t ch = 0;
    esp_err_t err = nocti_led_driver_map_logical(led_id, wl, &ch);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    return nocti_led_driver_set_channel_current_ma(ch, ma);
}

esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_channel_on(uint8_t pca_ch)
{
    if (!s.inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
    if (!is_valid_pca_ch(pca_ch) || is_gap_channel(pca_ch)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    uint8_t idx = (uint8_t)(pca_ch / 4u);
    uint8_t prev = s.ledout_cache[idx];

    ledout_set_field(pca_ch, PCA9956_LDR_ON);

    // Skip I2C if unchanged
    if (s.ledout_cache[idx] == prev) return ESP_OK;

    return pca_write_reg8((uint8_t)(PCA9956_REG_LEDOUT0 + idx), s.ledout_cache[idx]);
}

esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_channel_off(uint8_t pca_ch)
{
    if (!s.inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
    if (!is_valid_pca_ch(pca_ch) || is_gap_channel(pca_ch)) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    uint8_t idx = (uint8_t)(pca_ch / 4u);
    uint8_t prev = s.ledout_cache[idx];

    ledout_set_field(pca_ch, PCA9956_LDR_OFF);

    // Skip I2C if unchanged
    if (s.ledout_cache[idx] == prev) return ESP_OK;

    return pca_write_reg8((uint8_t)(PCA9956_REG_LEDOUT0 + idx), s.ledout_cache[idx]);
}

esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_map_logical(uint8_t led_id, nocti_wavelength_t wl, uint8_t *out_pca_ch)
{
    if (!out_pca_ch) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!s.inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
    if (led_id >= NOCTI_LED_SOURCES) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    uint8_t wl_idx = (wl == NOCTI_WL_735) ? 0u : 1u;
    uint8_t slot_index = (uint8_t)(led_id * 2u + wl_idx);

    *out_pca_ch = s.slot_map[slot_index];
    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_map_slot(uint8_t slot_index, uint8_t *out_pca_ch)
{
    if (!out_pca_ch) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!s.inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;
    if (slot_index >= NOCTI_LED_SLOTS) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    *out_pca_ch = s.slot_map[slot_index];
    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_get_resolved_slot_map(uint8_t out_slot_to_pca[NOCTI_LED_SLOTS])
{
    if (!out_slot_to_pca) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!s.inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

    memcpy(out_slot_to_pca, s.slot_map, NOCTI_LED_SLOTS);
    return ESP_OK;
}

// ============================================================
// Scheduler integration helpers
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_slot_begin(const nocti_slot_t *slot)
{
    if (!s.inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

    uint8_t slot_index = 0;
    esp_err_t err = slot_struct_to_index(slot, &slot_index);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    uint8_t ch = s.slot_map[slot_index];

    // slash: defensive cleanup kalau slot_end sebelumnya terlewat
    // hanya aktif pada kasus abnormal, jadi tidak menambah jitter di jalur normal
    if (s.cfg.cleanup_missed_slot_end && (s.last_on_ch != NOCTI_LED_MAP_UNUSED) && (s.last_on_ch != ch)) {
        // slash: cek cache dulu, kalau memang masih ON baru kita kirim I2C OFF
        if (ledout_get_field(s.last_on_ch) == PCA9956_LDR_ON) {
            // ignore return, karena ini best effort
            (void)nocti_led_driver_channel_off(s.last_on_ch);
        }
        s.last_on_ch = NOCTI_LED_MAP_UNUSED;
    }

    // // defaultnya: tidak update IREF di slot (arus diset di awal)
    if (s.cfg.apply_current_in_slot) {
        (void)nocti_led_driver_set_channel_current_ma(ch, slot->current_mA);
    }

    // // optional: OFF sebelum ON untuk bringup
    if (s.cfg.force_off_before_on) {
        (void)nocti_led_driver_channel_off(ch);
    }

    err = nocti_led_driver_channel_on(ch);
    if (err == ESP_OK) {
        s.last_on_ch = ch;
    }
    return err;
}

esp_err_t nocti_led_driver_slot_end(const nocti_slot_t *slot)
{
    if (!s.inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

    uint8_t slot_index = 0;
    esp_err_t err = slot_struct_to_index(slot, &slot_index);
    if (err != ESP_OK) return err;

    uint8_t ch = s.slot_map[slot_index];

    esp_err_t e2 = nocti_led_driver_channel_off(ch);
    if (s.last_on_ch == ch) s.last_on_ch = NOCTI_LED_MAP_UNUSED;

    return e2;
}


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_scheduler\include\nocti_scheduler.h ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_scheduler/include/nocti_scheduler.h
// ============================================================
// nocti_scheduler.h
#pragma once
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>

#include "nocti_types.h" // slash: nocti_time_us_t, nocti_wavelength_t, NOCTI_MAX_FNIRS_SOURCES

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

// ============================================================
// NoctiNIRS Scheduler
//
// Konsep dasar:
// - Timebase memanggil ISR setiap tick_period_us (misal 1000 us).
// - Scheduler task dibangunkan oleh ISR, lalu mengeksekusi 1 "tick program".
//
// Dalam 1 tick ada 2 kemungkinan:
// 1) DARK_TICK
//    - Tidak ada LED yang dinyalakan.
//    - Dipakai untuk "dark time budget" di dalam frame.
//
// 2) SLOT tick
//    - BEGIN: biasanya LED ON (LEDOUT write)
//    - settle_us: menunggu optik/analog settle
//    - SAMPLE: waktu ideal untuk ADC read
//    - sample_window_us: waktu window sampling/averaging
//    - END: biasanya LED OFF
//    - on_time_us/off_gap_us: memastikan total timing sesuai slot definition
//
// Timestamp policy:
// - ev.t_event_us adalah "tick aligned timestamp" dari timebase ISR
// - Untuk event SAMPLE dan END, scheduler menambahkan offset used_us
//   supaya event time merepresentasikan posisi di dalam tick
// ============================================================

typedef enum {
    NOCTI_DARK_BLOCK = 0,
    NOCTI_DARK_INTERLEAVED = 1,
} nocti_dark_mode_t;

typedef enum {
    NOCTI_EV_DARK_TICK = 0,
    NOCTI_EV_SLOT_BEGIN = 1,
    NOCTI_EV_SLOT_SAMPLE = 2,
    NOCTI_EV_SLOT_END = 3,
} nocti_sched_phase_t;

typedef struct {
    uint8_t led_id;                 // 0..9
    nocti_wavelength_t wavelength;  // slash: pakai dari nocti_types.h (735 atau 850)

    // Slot timing in microseconds (inside one tick)
    uint16_t on_time_us;            // total LED on duration in this tick
    uint16_t settle_us;             // wait after LED on before sampling
    uint16_t sample_window_us;      // sampling window duration
    uint16_t off_gap_us;            // gap after LED off

    // Requested current for this slot (optional use by LED driver)
    uint16_t current_mA;
} nocti_slot_t;

typedef struct {
    const nocti_slot_t *slots;
    uint16_t slot_count;

    uint32_t tick_period_us;        // typically 1000
    uint32_t frame_period_us;       // 25000, 50000, 100000

    uint32_t dark_us_total;         // total dark time inside a frame
    nocti_dark_mode_t dark_mode;

    uint8_t priority;
    uint16_t stack_words;
} nocti_scheduler_cfg_t;

typedef struct {
    nocti_time_us_t t_event_us;     // tick aligned timestamp (microseconds)
    uint32_t frame_index;           // increments each time frame rolls over
    uint32_t slot_index;            // index of current slot within cfg.slots
    nocti_sched_phase_t phase;

    // valid only for slot phases
    nocti_slot_t slot;
} nocti_sched_event_t;

typedef void (*nocti_sched_cb_t)(const nocti_sched_event_t *ev, void *user);

// Public API (tetap sama, non-API break)
bool nocti_scheduler_init(const nocti_scheduler_cfg_t *cfg, nocti_sched_cb_t cb, void *user);
bool nocti_scheduler_start(void);
void nocti_scheduler_stop(void);
bool nocti_scheduler_is_running(void);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_scheduler\nocti_scheduler.c ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_scheduler/nocti_scheduler.c
// ============================================================
// nocti_scheduler.c
#include "nocti_scheduler.h"

#include <string.h>

#include "freertos/FreeRTOS.h"
#include "freertos/task.h"

#include "esp_attr.h"

#include "nocti_timebase.h"
#include "nocti_log.h"

// ============================================================
// Revisi utama
// - Hindari busywait sepanjang tick, supaya task lain (BLE, log) tetap hidup.
// - Gunakan deadline absolut berbasis t_tick: BEGIN, SAMPLE, END, TICK_END.
// - Sleep kasar pakai sleep_until, lalu finishing pakai busywait tipis.
// - Selaraskan tipe waktu ke nocti_time_us_t (common).
// - Logging pakai NOCTI_LOGW (common) bukan ESP_LOGW.
// ============================================================

static const char *TAG = "nocti_sched";

// Busywait hanya untuk ekor kecil supaya presisi, tidak mengunci CPU lama
#define NOCTI_SCHED_SPIN_TAIL_US   (150)   // // tunable: 100 sampai 200 us biasanya oke

// slash: mode debug timing callback
// - 0: scheduler super ringan (default)
// - 1: ukur durasi cb dan log jika melewati ambang
#ifndef NOCTI_SCHED_ENABLE_CB_TIMING
#define NOCTI_SCHED_ENABLE_CB_TIMING   (0)
#endif

// slash: ambang warning durasi callback (dipakai hanya jika NOCTI_SCHED_ENABLE_CB_TIMING = 1)
#define NOCTI_SCHED_CB_WARN_US     (1200)

// ============================================================
// Internal context
// ============================================================

typedef struct {
    nocti_scheduler_cfg_t cfg;
    nocti_sched_cb_t cb;
    void *user;

    TaskHandle_t task;
    volatile bool running;

    uint32_t frame_index;
    uint32_t slot_index;

    uint32_t frame_elapsed_us;
    uint32_t dark_used_us;

    // ISR -> task timestamp handoff (anti torn read 64-bit)
    volatile uint32_t tick_seq;
    volatile uint32_t tick_lo;
    volatile uint32_t tick_hi;

    // Debug: callback timing stats
    uint32_t cb_overrun_cnt;
    uint32_t cb_max_us;
} sched_ctx_t;

static sched_ctx_t s;

// ============================================================
// Atomic tick read helper
// ============================================================

static inline nocti_time_us_t read_last_tick_us_atomic(void)
{
    while (1) {
        uint32_t s1 = s.tick_seq;
        uint32_t hi = s.tick_hi;
        uint32_t lo = s.tick_lo;
        uint32_t s2 = s.tick_seq;

        if (s1 == s2 && ((s1 & 1u) == 0u)) {
            uint64_t v = ((uint64_t)hi << 32) | (uint64_t)lo;
            return (nocti_time_us_t)v;
        }
        // // retry sampai stabil
    }
}

// ============================================================
// Event emitter + callback timing guard
// ============================================================

static void emit_event(nocti_sched_phase_t phase, const nocti_slot_t *slot_opt, nocti_time_us_t t_us)
{
    if (!s.cb) return;

    // slash: isi struct event tanpa memset besar, biar cb path lebih ringan
    nocti_sched_event_t ev;
    ev.t_event_us  = t_us;
    ev.frame_index = s.frame_index;
    ev.slot_index  = s.slot_index;
    ev.phase       = phase;

    if (slot_opt) ev.slot = *slot_opt;
    else memset(&ev.slot, 0, sizeof(ev.slot));

#if NOCTI_SCHED_ENABLE_CB_TIMING
    // slash: ukur durasi callback untuk debug jitter
    nocti_time_us_t t0 = nocti_timebase_now_us();
    s.cb(&ev, s.user);
    nocti_time_us_t dt = nocti_timebase_now_us() - t0;

    if (dt > (nocti_time_us_t)NOCTI_SCHED_CB_WARN_US) {
        s.cb_overrun_cnt++;
        if ((uint32_t)dt > s.cb_max_us) s.cb_max_us = (uint32_t)dt;

        NOCTI_LOGW(TAG, "cb slow: %lld us, overrun_cnt=%u, max=%u us",
                   (long long)dt, (unsigned)s.cb_overrun_cnt, (unsigned)s.cb_max_us);
    }
#else
    // slash: mode cepat, tidak ada pengukuran waktu
    s.cb(&ev, s.user);
#endif
}

// ============================================================
// Dark policy decision
// ============================================================

static bool should_insert_dark_this_tick(void)
{
    if (s.cfg.dark_us_total == 0) return false;
    if (s.dark_used_us >= s.cfg.dark_us_total) return false;

    uint32_t remaining_dark = s.cfg.dark_us_total - s.dark_used_us;

    if (s.cfg.dark_mode == NOCTI_DARK_BLOCK) {
        uint32_t remaining_in_frame = s.cfg.frame_period_us - s.frame_elapsed_us;
        return (remaining_in_frame <= remaining_dark);
    }

    // // interleaved: target dark proporsional terhadap progress frame
    uint64_t dark_target =
        ((uint64_t)s.cfg.dark_us_total * (uint64_t)s.frame_elapsed_us) /
        (uint64_t)s.cfg.frame_period_us;

    return ((uint64_t)s.dark_used_us < dark_target);
}

// ============================================================
// Wait helper
// - Sleep kasar sampai mendekati deadline
// - Busywait ekor tipis untuk presisi
// ============================================================

static inline void wait_until_deadline_us(nocti_time_us_t t_deadline_us)
{
    while (1) {
        nocti_time_us_t now = nocti_timebase_now_us();
        nocti_time_us_t diff = t_deadline_us - now;

        if (diff <= 0) return;

        if (diff > (nocti_time_us_t)NOCTI_SCHED_SPIN_TAIL_US) {
            // // tidur sampai mendekati deadline, sisakan ekor untuk busywait
            nocti_timebase_sleep_until_us(t_deadline_us - (nocti_time_us_t)NOCTI_SCHED_SPIN_TAIL_US);
        } else {
            // // ekor tipis untuk presisi
            nocti_timebase_busywait_us((uint32_t)diff);
            return;
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================
// ISR tick callback from timebase
// ============================================================

static bool IRAM_ATTR timebase_tick_isr(nocti_time_us_t now_us, void *user)
{
    TaskHandle_t h = (TaskHandle_t)user;

    // // publish timestamp atomik (seq lock)
    s.tick_seq++;
    uint64_t u = (uint64_t)now_us;
    s.tick_hi = (uint32_t)(u >> 32);
    s.tick_lo = (uint32_t)(u & 0xFFFFFFFFu);
    s.tick_seq++;

    BaseType_t hp_woken = pdFALSE;
    vTaskNotifyGiveFromISR(h, &hp_woken);
    return (hp_woken == pdTRUE);
}

// ============================================================
// Scheduler task
// ============================================================

static void scheduler_task(void *arg)
{
    (void)arg;

    while (1) {
        ulTaskNotifyTake(pdTRUE, portMAX_DELAY);

        if (!s.running) {
            // // stop cepat: abaikan tick jika running false
            continue;
        }

        // // anti backlog: buang notifikasi lama, pakai tick terbaru
        while (ulTaskNotifyTake(pdTRUE, 0) > 0) {
            // drain
        }

        nocti_time_us_t t_tick = read_last_tick_us_atomic();
        nocti_time_us_t t_tick_end = t_tick + (nocti_time_us_t)s.cfg.tick_period_us;

        bool do_dark = should_insert_dark_this_tick();

        if (do_dark) {
            emit_event(NOCTI_EV_DARK_TICK, NULL, t_tick);

            s.dark_used_us += s.cfg.tick_period_us;
            s.frame_elapsed_us += s.cfg.tick_period_us;

            // // tunggu sampai akhir tick tanpa mengunci CPU lama
            wait_until_deadline_us(t_tick_end);
        } else {
            const nocti_slot_t *slot = &s.cfg.slots[s.slot_index];

            // Deadlines absolut di dalam tick
            nocti_time_us_t t_sample = t_tick + (nocti_time_us_t)slot->settle_us;
            nocti_time_us_t t_end_on = t_tick + (nocti_time_us_t)slot->on_time_us;

            // // sample_window dimaknai sebagai durasi setelah SAMPLE sebelum END event
            nocti_time_us_t t_end_event = t_sample + (nocti_time_us_t)slot->sample_window_us;
            if (t_end_event > t_end_on) {
                // // safety: END event tidak boleh lewat on_time
                t_end_event = t_end_on;
            }

            // BEGIN
            emit_event(NOCTI_EV_SLOT_BEGIN, slot, t_tick);

            // SAMPLE
            wait_until_deadline_us(t_sample);
            emit_event(NOCTI_EV_SLOT_SAMPLE, slot, t_sample);

            // END (LED OFF idealnya di callback ini)
            wait_until_deadline_us(t_end_event);
            emit_event(NOCTI_EV_SLOT_END, slot, t_end_event);

            // // Pastikan LED ON window selesai (hingga on_time_us), lalu off_gap
            // // Ini menjaga spacing slot sesuai definisi
            wait_until_deadline_us(t_end_on);

            // // Off gap menambah jarak setelah LED OFF, tapi tetap di dalam tick budget
            nocti_time_us_t t_after_gap = t_end_on + (nocti_time_us_t)slot->off_gap_us;
            if (t_after_gap > t_tick_end) {
                // // jika user set off_gap terlalu besar, kita clamp ke tick_end
                t_after_gap = t_tick_end;
            }
            wait_until_deadline_us(t_after_gap);

            // // Sisa tick sampai t_tick_end
            wait_until_deadline_us(t_tick_end);

            // Advance slot index
            s.slot_index++;
            if (s.slot_index >= s.cfg.slot_count) s.slot_index = 0;

            s.frame_elapsed_us += s.cfg.tick_period_us;
        }

        // Frame rollover
        if (s.frame_elapsed_us >= s.cfg.frame_period_us) {
            s.frame_index++;
            s.frame_elapsed_us = 0;
            s.dark_used_us = 0;
            s.slot_index = 0;
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================
// Public API
// ============================================================

bool nocti_scheduler_init(const nocti_scheduler_cfg_t *cfg, nocti_sched_cb_t cb, void *user)
{
    if (!cfg) return false;
    if (!cfg->slots || cfg->slot_count == 0) return false;
    if (cfg->tick_period_us == 0) return false;
    if (cfg->frame_period_us == 0) return false;
    if (cfg->dark_us_total > cfg->frame_period_us) return false;

    memset(&s, 0, sizeof(s));
    s.cfg = *cfg;
    s.cb = cb;
    s.user = user;

    // Validate each slot timing
    for (uint16_t i = 0; i < s.cfg.slot_count; i++) {
        const nocti_slot_t *sl = &s.cfg.slots[i];

        uint32_t settle_plus_window =
            (uint32_t)sl->settle_us + (uint32_t)sl->sample_window_us;

        if (settle_plus_window > (uint32_t)sl->on_time_us) return false;

        uint32_t total_tick = (uint32_t)sl->on_time_us + (uint32_t)sl->off_gap_us;
        if (total_tick > s.cfg.tick_period_us) return false;

        if (sl->wavelength != NOCTI_WL_735 && sl->wavelength != NOCTI_WL_850) return false;
        if (sl->led_id >= NOCTI_MAX_FNIRS_SOURCES) return false;
    }

    // Default stack and priority
    if (s.cfg.stack_words == 0) {
        s.cfg.stack_words = 4096 / sizeof(StackType_t);
    }
    if (s.cfg.priority == 0) {
        s.cfg.priority = 10;
    }

    BaseType_t ok = xTaskCreate(
        scheduler_task,
        "nocti_sched",
        s.cfg.stack_words,
        NULL,
        s.cfg.priority,
        &s.task
    );

    return (ok == pdPASS);
}

bool nocti_scheduler_start(void)
{
    if (!s.task) return false;
    if (s.running) return false;

    s.frame_index = 0;
    s.slot_index = 0;
    s.frame_elapsed_us = 0;
    s.dark_used_us = 0;

    s.cb_overrun_cnt = 0;
    s.cb_max_us = 0;

    s.running = true;

    nocti_timebase_tick_cfg_t tcfg = {
        .tick_period_us = s.cfg.tick_period_us,
        .isr_cb = timebase_tick_isr,
        .user = (void *)s.task,
    };

    return nocti_timebase_start_tick(&tcfg);
}

void nocti_scheduler_stop(void)
{
    if (!s.running) return;

    s.running = false;
    nocti_timebase_stop_tick();
}

bool nocti_scheduler_is_running(void)
{
    return s.running;
}


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_storage\include\nocti_storage.h ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_storage/include/nocti_storage.h
// ============================================================

#pragma once
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>

#include "esp_err.h"
#include "freertos/FreeRTOS.h"

#include "nocti_types.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

// ============================================================
// NoctiNIRS Storage v0 (Recording Manager)
// slash: di versi ini, storage tidak menulis ke media fisik
// slash: fungsi utamanya mengelola sesi recording dan menerbitkan STATUS, EVENT ke nocti_stream
// slash: nanti bisa ditambah backend flash spool tanpa ubah producer dan BLE
// ============================================================

typedef struct nocti_stream_t nocti_stream_t;

// Event codes sederhana untuk logging
typedef enum {
    NOCTI_EVT_REC_START       = 1, // slash: mulai sesi
    NOCTI_EVT_REC_STOP        = 2, // slash: stop sesi
    NOCTI_EVT_LINK_UP         = 3, // slash: koneksi uplink aktif (BLE connected dan notify enabled)
    NOCTI_EVT_LINK_DOWN       = 4, // slash: uplink putus
    NOCTI_EVT_DROP_REPORT     = 5, // slash: laporan drop counter
} nocti_evt_code_t;

// Payload STATUS untuk monitoring dari sisi PC
// slash: dibuat fixed size agar gampang diparse
typedef struct __attribute__((packed)) {
    uint32_t session_id;         // slash: id sesi aktif
    uint32_t t_rec_ms;           // slash: waktu sejak start recording dalam ms (wrap ok)
    uint32_t drop_ctrl;          // slash: drop dari stream ctrl
    uint32_t drop_fnirs;         // slash: drop dari stream fnirs
    uint32_t drop_biopot;        // slash: drop dari stream biopot
    uint8_t  rec_on;             // slash: 1 kalau recording aktif
    uint8_t  link_on;            // slash: 1 kalau uplink aktif
    uint16_t reserved;           // slash: align dan ruang future
} nocti_status_v0_t;

// Config
typedef struct {
    nocti_stream_t *stream;         // slash: target stream untuk push record
    uint32_t status_period_ms;      // slash: periode STATUS, 0 untuk disable
    uint32_t drop_report_period_ms; // slash: periode event drop report, 0 untuk disable
} nocti_storage_cfg_t;

// Runtime handle
typedef struct {
    bool inited;

    nocti_storage_cfg_t cfg;

    uint32_t session_id;            // slash: naik tiap START
    bool rec_on;                    // slash: state recording
    bool link_on;                   // slash: state uplink

    nocti_time_us_t t0_boot_us;     // slash: base time saat init
    nocti_time_us_t t0_rec_us;      // slash: base time saat start recording

    uint32_t next_status_due_ms;    // slash: scheduler periodik
    uint32_t next_drop_due_ms;      // slash: scheduler periodik

    // cache drop terakhir, agar laporan tidak spam kalau tidak berubah
    uint32_t last_drop_ctrl;
    uint32_t last_drop_fnirs;
    uint32_t last_drop_biopot;
} nocti_storage_t;

// ============================================================
// API
// ============================================================

// Init recording manager
esp_err_t nocti_storage_init(nocti_storage_t *st, const nocti_storage_cfg_t *cfg, nocti_time_us_t now_us);
esp_err_t nocti_storage_deinit(nocti_storage_t *st);

// Convenience init (now_us dari timebase boleh dilakukan di app, bukan di sini)
esp_err_t nocti_storage_init_now(nocti_storage_t *st, const nocti_storage_cfg_t *cfg);

// Start dan stop sesi recording
esp_err_t nocti_storage_start(nocti_storage_t *st, nocti_time_us_t now_us);
esp_err_t nocti_storage_stop(nocti_storage_t *st, nocti_time_us_t now_us);

// Update state uplink dari BLE layer
// slash: panggil saat connect atau subscribe notify berubah
esp_err_t nocti_storage_set_link(nocti_storage_t *st, bool link_on, nocti_time_us_t now_us);

// Periodic service, dipanggil dari loop main atau task ringan
// slash: tugasnya push STATUS dan event drop report sesuai period
esp_err_t nocti_storage_service(nocti_storage_t *st, nocti_time_us_t now_us);

// Getter sederhana
uint32_t nocti_storage_session_id(const nocti_storage_t *st);
bool     nocti_storage_is_recording(const nocti_storage_t *st);
bool     nocti_storage_is_link_on(const nocti_storage_t *st);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_storage\nocti_storage.c ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_storage/nocti_storage.c
// ============================================================

#include "nocti_storage.h"

#include <string.h>

#include "nocti_log.h"
#include "nocti_stream.h"
#include "nocti_timebase.h" // slash: untuk init_now dan now_us helper

static const char *TAG = "nocti_storage";

static inline uint32_t us_to_ms_u32(nocti_time_us_t us)
{
    if (us <= 0) return 0;
    return (uint32_t)(us / 1000); // slash: wrap natural setelah 2^32 ms
}

static inline bool time_after_eq_u32(uint32_t a, uint32_t b)
{
    // slash: wrap safe compare untuk ms counter
    return (int32_t)(a - b) >= 0;
}

static esp_err_t push_event(nocti_storage_t *st, nocti_evt_code_t code, nocti_time_us_t now_us, uint32_t v0, uint32_t v1)
{
    // slash: payload event kecil, fixed 12 byte, mudah diparse di PC
    typedef struct __attribute__((packed)) {
        uint8_t  code;       // slash: nocti_evt_code_t
        uint8_t  rsv0;
        uint16_t rsv1;
        uint32_t v0;         // slash: nilai bebas
        uint32_t v1;         // slash: nilai bebas
    } evt_v0_t;

    if (!st || !st->cfg.stream) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

    evt_v0_t e;
    memset(&e, 0, sizeof(e));
    e.code = (uint8_t)code;
    e.v0 = v0;
    e.v1 = v1;

    return nocti_stream_push(st->cfg.stream, NOCTI_REC_EVENT, now_us, &e, sizeof(e));
}

static esp_err_t push_status(nocti_storage_t *st, nocti_time_us_t now_us)
{
    if (!st || !st->cfg.stream) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

    nocti_status_v0_t s;
    memset(&s, 0, sizeof(s));

    s.session_id = st->session_id;

    nocti_time_us_t t_rec_us = now_us - st->t0_rec_us;
    if (!st->rec_on) t_rec_us = 0; // slash: jika tidak recording, t_rec_ms dibuat 0 agar sederhana
    if (t_rec_us < 0) t_rec_us = 0;

    s.t_rec_ms = us_to_ms_u32(t_rec_us);

    // slash: drop counter diambil dari nocti_stream
    s.drop_ctrl   = nocti_stream_drop_ctrl(st->cfg.stream);
    s.drop_fnirs  = nocti_stream_drop_fnirs(st->cfg.stream);
    s.drop_biopot = nocti_stream_drop_biopot(st->cfg.stream);

    s.rec_on  = (uint8_t)(st->rec_on ? 1 : 0);
    s.link_on = (uint8_t)(st->link_on ? 1 : 0);

    return nocti_stream_push(st->cfg.stream, NOCTI_REC_STATUS, now_us, &s, sizeof(s));
}

esp_err_t nocti_storage_init(nocti_storage_t *st, const nocti_storage_cfg_t *cfg, nocti_time_us_t now_us)
{
    if (!st || !cfg) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!cfg->stream) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;

    memset(st, 0, sizeof(*st));
    st->cfg = *cfg;

    st->t0_boot_us = now_us;
    st->t0_rec_us = now_us;

    st->session_id = 0;
    st->rec_on = false;
    st->link_on = false;

    st->next_status_due_ms = 0;
    st->next_drop_due_ms = 0;

    st->last_drop_ctrl = 0;
    st->last_drop_fnirs = 0;
    st->last_drop_biopot = 0;

    st->inited = true;

    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "storage manager inited");
    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t nocti_storage_init_now(nocti_storage_t *st, const nocti_storage_cfg_t *cfg)
{
    // slash: convenience init
    return nocti_storage_init(st, cfg, (nocti_time_us_t)nocti_timebase_now_us());
}

esp_err_t nocti_storage_deinit(nocti_storage_t *st)
{
    if (!st) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    memset(st, 0, sizeof(*st));
    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t nocti_storage_start(nocti_storage_t *st, nocti_time_us_t now_us)
{
    if (!st || !st->inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

    st->session_id += 1;
    st->rec_on = true;
    st->t0_rec_us = now_us;

    // schedule status period
    uint32_t now_ms = us_to_ms_u32(now_us - st->t0_boot_us);
    if (st->cfg.status_period_ms > 0) {
        st->next_status_due_ms = now_ms;
    }
    if (st->cfg.drop_report_period_ms > 0) {
        st->next_drop_due_ms = now_ms;
    }

    (void)push_event(st, NOCTI_EVT_REC_START, now_us, st->session_id, 0);
    (void)nocti_stream_push_time64(st->cfg.stream, now_us); // slash: anchor absolut saat start

    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "rec start, session=%u", (unsigned)st->session_id);
    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t nocti_storage_stop(nocti_storage_t *st, nocti_time_us_t now_us)
{
    if (!st || !st->inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

    st->rec_on = false;

    (void)push_event(st, NOCTI_EVT_REC_STOP, now_us, st->session_id, 0);

    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "rec stop, session=%u", (unsigned)st->session_id);
    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t nocti_storage_set_link(nocti_storage_t *st, bool link_on, nocti_time_us_t now_us)
{
    if (!st || !st->inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

    if (st->link_on == link_on) {
        return ESP_OK;
    }

    st->link_on = link_on;

    if (link_on) {
        (void)push_event(st, NOCTI_EVT_LINK_UP, now_us, 0, 0);
    } else {
        (void)push_event(st, NOCTI_EVT_LINK_DOWN, now_us, 0, 0);
    }

    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t nocti_storage_service(nocti_storage_t *st, nocti_time_us_t now_us)
{
    if (!st || !st->inited) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

    // slash: schedule pakai ms relatif boot agar wrap safe
    uint32_t now_ms = us_to_ms_u32(now_us - st->t0_boot_us);

    // push STATUS periodik
    if (st->cfg.status_period_ms > 0) {
        if (time_after_eq_u32(now_ms, st->next_status_due_ms)) {
            st->next_status_due_ms = now_ms + st->cfg.status_period_ms;
            (void)push_status(st, now_us);
        }
    }

    // drop report periodik, hanya kalau drop berubah atau memang interval lewat
    if (st->cfg.drop_report_period_ms > 0) {
        if (time_after_eq_u32(now_ms, st->next_drop_due_ms)) {
            st->next_drop_due_ms = now_ms + st->cfg.drop_report_period_ms;

            uint32_t dc = nocti_stream_drop_ctrl(st->cfg.stream);
            uint32_t df = nocti_stream_drop_fnirs(st->cfg.stream);
            uint32_t db = nocti_stream_drop_biopot(st->cfg.stream);

            bool changed = (dc != st->last_drop_ctrl) || (df != st->last_drop_fnirs) || (db != st->last_drop_biopot);

            st->last_drop_ctrl = dc;
            st->last_drop_fnirs = df;
            st->last_drop_biopot = db;

            if (changed) {
                // slash: laporkan ringkas, v0 dan v1 dipakai packing
                // slash: v0 = drop_ctrl, v1 high16 drop_fnirs low16 drop_biopot
                uint32_t v0 = dc;
                uint32_t v1 = ((df & 0xFFFFu) << 16) | (db & 0xFFFFu);
                (void)push_event(st, NOCTI_EVT_DROP_REPORT, now_us, v0, v1);
            }
        }
    }

    // slash: TIME64 periodik tetap dipanggil dari tempat lain juga boleh
    (void)nocti_stream_maybe_push_time64(st->cfg.stream, now_us);

    return ESP_OK;
}

uint32_t nocti_storage_session_id(const nocti_storage_t *st)
{
    return st ? st->session_id : 0;
}

bool nocti_storage_is_recording(const nocti_storage_t *st)
{
    return st ? st->rec_on : false;
}

bool nocti_storage_is_link_on(const nocti_storage_t *st)
{
    return st ? st->link_on : false;
}


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_stream\include\nocti_stream.h ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_stream/include/nocti_stream.h
// ============================================================

#pragma once
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>

#include "esp_err.h"

#include "freertos/FreeRTOS.h"
#include "freertos/semphr.h"
#include "freertos/ringbuf.h"

#include "nocti_types.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

// ============================================================
// NoctiNIRS Stream v1
// slash: stream membungkus payload menjadi frame konsisten untuk WiFi, BLE, dan storage
// slash: stream bukan transport, dia hanya buffer + framing + drop policy
// ============================================================

#define NOCTI_STREAM_MAGIC                 (0xA5u)
#define NOCTI_STREAM_VERSION               (0x01u)

#define NOCTI_STREAM_MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES     (512u)
#define NOCTI_STREAM_HDR_BYTES             (12u)
#define NOCTI_STREAM_CRC_BYTES             (2u)
#define NOCTI_STREAM_MAX_FRAME_BYTES       (NOCTI_STREAM_HDR_BYTES + NOCTI_STREAM_MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES + NOCTI_STREAM_CRC_BYTES)

// ============================================================
// RAM guard policy (optional override)
// slash: boleh di-override dari build system dengan -DNOCTI_STREAM_RB_*
// ============================================================

#ifndef NOCTI_STREAM_RB_MIN_BYTES
#define NOCTI_STREAM_RB_MIN_BYTES        ((size_t)(NOCTI_STREAM_MAX_FRAME_BYTES * 4u)) // slash: min 4 frame
#endif

#ifndef NOCTI_STREAM_RB_MAX_BYTES
#define NOCTI_STREAM_RB_MAX_BYTES        ((size_t)(192u * 1024u)) // slash: limit per ringbuffer
#endif

#ifndef NOCTI_STREAM_RB_TOTAL_MAX_BYTES
#define NOCTI_STREAM_RB_TOTAL_MAX_BYTES  ((size_t)(256u * 1024u)) // slash: limit total 3 ringbuffer
#endif

// slash: TIME64 sync dipush saat start dan setiap 30 detik
#define NOCTI_STREAM_TIME64_PERIOD_US      (30000000u)

// ============================================================
// Record types
// ============================================================

typedef enum {
    NOCTI_REC_INVALID = 0,
    NOCTI_REC_STATUS  = 1,
    NOCTI_REC_EVENT   = 2,
    NOCTI_REC_TIME64  = 3,
    NOCTI_REC_FNIRS   = 4,
    NOCTI_REC_BIOPOT  = 5,

    NOCTI_REC_FNIRS_CFG   = 6,  // slash: config sekali
    NOCTI_REC_FNIRS_FRAME = 7,  // slash: data per frame

    NOCTI_REC_SESSION     = 8,  // slash: meta sesi, dikirim pada START

    // slash: biopot v1 (ADS1299)
    // /belajar: cfg dikirim sekali saat START agar decoder tahu fs, gain, dan mask
    // /belajar: frame adalah batching beberapa sample agar overhead transport turun
    NOCTI_REC_BIOPOT_CFG   = 9,
    NOCTI_REC_BIOPOT_FRAME = 10,
} nocti_rec_type_t;

// ============================================================
// Flags (header)
// ============================================================

typedef enum {
    NOCTI_STREAM_F_NONE       = 0x00,
    NOCTI_STREAM_F_HAS_CRC16  = 0x01, // slash: v1 selalu pakai CRC16
} nocti_stream_flags_t;

// ============================================================
// Frame header (packed)
// slash: t_rel_us adalah waktu relatif sejak stream start, 32-bit (wrap sekitar 71.6 menit)
// slash: TIME64 record dipakai untuk re-anchor sesi panjang
// ============================================================

typedef struct __attribute__((packed)) {
    uint8_t  magic;        // 0xA5
    uint8_t  version;      // 0x01
    uint8_t  type;         // nocti_rec_type_t
    uint8_t  flags;        // nocti_stream_flags_t
    uint16_t seq;          // rolling sequence
    uint32_t t_rel_us;     // relative timestamp us (wrap ok)
    uint16_t payload_len;  // bytes
} nocti_stream_hdr_t;

// ============================================================
// Payload: session v1 (NOCTI_REC_SESSION)
// slash: dipush saat START untuk memberi boundary sesi dan bantu decoder
// ============================================================

#define NOCTI_PAYLOAD_SESSION_V1_VERSION   (1u)

typedef struct __attribute__((packed)) {
    uint8_t  version;      // = NOCTI_PAYLOAD_SESSION_V1_VERSION
    uint8_t  _rsv0[3];
    uint32_t session_id;   // slash: sama dengan session_id UDP jika dipakai
    uint64_t t0_us;        // slash: absolute base time at session start
} nocti_payload_session_v1_t;

// ============================================================
// Config
// slash: ukuran ring buffer dalam bytes, sesuaikan dengan PSRAM
// ============================================================

typedef struct {
    size_t rb_ctrl_bytes;   // status, event, time64, session, cfg
    size_t rb_fnirs_bytes;  // fnirs records
    size_t rb_biopot_bytes; // biopot records
} nocti_stream_cfg_t;

// ============================================================
// Runtime handle
// ============================================================

typedef struct nocti_stream_t{
    bool inited;

    RingbufHandle_t rb_ctrl;
    RingbufHandle_t rb_fnirs;
    RingbufHandle_t rb_biopot;

    SemaphoreHandle_t mutex;

    uint16_t seq;
    nocti_time_us_t t0_us;         // absolute base time (us) at stream start
    uint32_t next_time64_due_us;   // relative us time for next TIME64 record

    uint32_t drop_ctrl;
    uint32_t drop_fnirs;
    uint32_t drop_biopot;
} nocti_stream_t;

// ============================================================
// API
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_stream_init(nocti_stream_t *s, const nocti_stream_cfg_t *cfg, nocti_time_us_t now_us);
esp_err_t nocti_stream_deinit(nocti_stream_t *s);

// Convenience init (now_us dari timebase)
// slash: memudahkan pemanggil, tapi dependency jadi ke nocti_timebase
esp_err_t nocti_stream_init_now(nocti_stream_t *s, const nocti_stream_cfg_t *cfg);

// ============================================================
// Session control
// ============================================================

// slash: reset boundary sesi, clear ringbuffer, reset seq, push SESSION + TIME64
esp_err_t nocti_stream_start_session(nocti_stream_t *s, nocti_time_us_t now_us, uint32_t session_id);

// slash: convenience start session (now_us dari timebase)
esp_err_t nocti_stream_start_session_now(nocti_stream_t *s, uint32_t session_id);

// slash: clear semua ringbuffer tanpa copy
esp_err_t nocti_stream_clear(nocti_stream_t *s);

// ============================================================
// Push generic payload
// slash: panggil dari producer (fnirs, biopot, status)
// slash: now_us sebaiknya dari nocti_timebase_now_us
// ============================================================

esp_err_t nocti_stream_push(nocti_stream_t *s,
                            nocti_rec_type_t type,
                            nocti_time_us_t now_us,
