// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_timebase/include/nocti_timebase.h
// ============================================================

#pragma once
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>

#include "nocti_types.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

// slash: callback ISR untuk tick periodik
// slash: return true jika perlu yield (high priority task woken)
typedef bool (*nocti_timebase_isr_cb_t)(nocti_time_us_t now_us, void *user);

typedef struct {
    uint32_t tick_period_us;         // slash: contoh 1000 us untuk 1 kHz tick scheduler
    nocti_timebase_isr_cb_t isr_cb;   // slash: boleh NULL jika hanya ingin tick tanpa callback
    void *user;
} nocti_timebase_tick_cfg_t;

// slash: init base timer GPTimer 1 MHz (free running) untuk sumber now_us
// slash: harus dipanggil sebelum scheduler start
bool nocti_timebase_init(void);

// slash: monotonic microseconds berbasis GPTimer base (1 tick = 1 us)
nocti_time_us_t nocti_timebase_now_us(void);

// slash: start tick periodik (pakai GPTimer terpisah), dipakai scheduler
bool nocti_timebase_start_tick(const nocti_timebase_tick_cfg_t *cfg);

// slash: stop tick periodik, base timer tetap jalan
void nocti_timebase_stop_tick(void);

// slash: busywait presisi, cocok untuk tail kecil (puluhan sampai ratusan us)
void nocti_timebase_busywait_us(uint32_t us);

// slash: tidur sampai target timestamp, hybrid coarse sleep + busywait tail
void nocti_timebase_sleep_until_us(nocti_time_us_t t_us);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_timebase\nocti_timebase.c ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_timebase/nocti_timebase.c
// ============================================================

#include "nocti_timebase.h"

#include <string.h>

#include "driver/gptimer.h"
#include "esp_err.h"
#include "nocti_log.h"

#include "freertos/FreeRTOS.h"
#include "freertos/task.h"

static const char *TAG = "nocti_timebase";

// ============================================================
// /learn Desain
// 1) s_base_timer: GPTimer free running 1 MHz, sumber now_us
// 2) s_tick_timer: GPTimer periodik untuk ISR tick scheduler
// /learn Kenapa 2 timer
// - base timer tidak pernah reload, jadi now_us selalu monotonic
// - tick timer boleh auto reload, tapi tidak dipakai untuk now_us
// ============================================================

static gptimer_handle_t s_base_timer = NULL;
static gptimer_handle_t s_tick_timer = NULL;

static nocti_timebase_tick_cfg_t s_tick_cfg;
static volatile bool s_tick_running = false;
static volatile bool s_inited = false;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// /learn helper: read base timer count in us
// ------------------------------------------------------------
static inline bool base_read_us(uint64_t *out_us)
{
    if (!out_us) return false;
    if (s_base_timer == NULL) return false;

    uint64_t v = 0;
    esp_err_t e = gptimer_get_raw_count(s_base_timer, &v);
    if (e != ESP_OK) return false;

    *out_us = v; // slash: resolution 1 MHz jadi 1 count = 1 us
    return true;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Tick ISR callback
// slash: timestamp diambil dari base timer agar konsisten
// ------------------------------------------------------------
static bool on_tick_alarm(gptimer_handle_t t,
                          const gptimer_alarm_event_data_t *edata,
                          void *user_ctx)
{
    (void)t;
    (void)edata;
    (void)user_ctx;

    if (!s_tick_running) return false;

    uint64_t now64 = 0;
    if (!base_read_us(&now64)) {
        // slash: kalau gagal baca base timer, jangan crash
        // slash: scheduler akan tetap dapat notify, tapi timestamp bisa 0
        now64 = 0;
    }

    if (s_tick_cfg.isr_cb) {
        return s_tick_cfg.isr_cb((nocti_time_us_t)now64, s_tick_cfg.user);
    }
    return false;
}

bool nocti_timebase_init(void)
{
    if (s_inited) return true;

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // /learn base GPTimer 1 MHz free running
    // --------------------------------------------------------
    gptimer_config_t cfg = {
        .clk_src = GPTIMER_CLK_SRC_DEFAULT,
        .direction = GPTIMER_COUNT_UP,
        .resolution_hz = 1000000u, // slash: 1 MHz
    };

    esp_err_t e = gptimer_new_timer(&cfg, &s_base_timer);
    if (e != ESP_OK || s_base_timer == NULL) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "base gptimer_new_timer fail: %s", esp_err_to_name(e));
        s_base_timer = NULL;
        return false;
    }

    // slash: base timer tidak pakai alarm, hanya counter
    e = gptimer_enable(s_base_timer);
    if (e != ESP_OK) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "base gptimer_enable fail: %s", esp_err_to_name(e));
        (void)gptimer_del_timer(s_base_timer);
        s_base_timer = NULL;
        return false;
    }

    e = gptimer_set_raw_count(s_base_timer, 0);
    if (e != ESP_OK) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "base set_raw_count fail: %s", esp_err_to_name(e));
        (void)gptimer_disable(s_base_timer);
        (void)gptimer_del_timer(s_base_timer);
        s_base_timer = NULL;
        return false;
    }

    e = gptimer_start(s_base_timer);
    if (e != ESP_OK) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "base gptimer_start fail: %s", esp_err_to_name(e));
        (void)gptimer_disable(s_base_timer);
        (void)gptimer_del_timer(s_base_timer);
        s_base_timer = NULL;
        return false;
    }

    memset(&s_tick_cfg, 0, sizeof(s_tick_cfg));
    s_tick_timer = NULL;
    s_tick_running = false;
    s_inited = true;

    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "init ok: base GPTimer 1MHz started");
    return true;
}

nocti_time_us_t nocti_timebase_now_us(void)
{
    uint64_t now64 = 0;
    if (!base_read_us(&now64)) return 0;
    return (nocti_time_us_t)now64;
}

bool nocti_timebase_start_tick(const nocti_timebase_tick_cfg_t *cfg)
{
    if (!cfg) return false;
    if (cfg->tick_period_us == 0) return false;
    if (!s_inited) {
        if (!nocti_timebase_init()) return false;
    }
    if (s_tick_running) return false;
    if (s_tick_timer != NULL) return false;

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // /learn tick GPTimer 1 MHz + alarm auto reload
    // --------------------------------------------------------
    gptimer_config_t tcfg = {
        .clk_src = GPTIMER_CLK_SRC_DEFAULT,
        .direction = GPTIMER_COUNT_UP,
        .resolution_hz = 1000000u,
    };

    esp_err_t e = gptimer_new_timer(&tcfg, &s_tick_timer);
    if (e != ESP_OK || s_tick_timer == NULL) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "tick gptimer_new_timer fail: %s", esp_err_to_name(e));
        s_tick_timer = NULL;
        return false;
    }

    gptimer_event_callbacks_t cbs = {
        .on_alarm = on_tick_alarm,
    };

    e = gptimer_register_event_callbacks(s_tick_timer, &cbs, NULL);
    if (e != ESP_OK) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "tick register_callbacks fail: %s", esp_err_to_name(e));
        (void)gptimer_del_timer(s_tick_timer);
        s_tick_timer = NULL;
        return false;
    }

    e = gptimer_enable(s_tick_timer);
    if (e != ESP_OK) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "tick enable fail: %s", esp_err_to_name(e));
        (void)gptimer_del_timer(s_tick_timer);
        s_tick_timer = NULL;
        return false;
    }

    e = gptimer_set_raw_count(s_tick_timer, 0);
    if (e != ESP_OK) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "tick set_raw_count fail: %s", esp_err_to_name(e));
        (void)gptimer_disable(s_tick_timer);
        (void)gptimer_del_timer(s_tick_timer);
        s_tick_timer = NULL;
        return false;
    }

    gptimer_alarm_config_t alarm = {
        .alarm_count = (uint64_t)cfg->tick_period_us,
        .reload_count = 0,
        .flags.auto_reload_on_alarm = true,
    };

    e = gptimer_set_alarm_action(s_tick_timer, &alarm);
    if (e != ESP_OK) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "tick set_alarm_action fail: %s", esp_err_to_name(e));
        (void)gptimer_disable(s_tick_timer);
        (void)gptimer_del_timer(s_tick_timer);
        s_tick_timer = NULL;
        return false;
    }

    // slash: copy cfg terakhir, dipakai ISR
    s_tick_cfg = *cfg;
    s_tick_running = true;

    e = gptimer_start(s_tick_timer);
    if (e != ESP_OK) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "tick start fail: %s", esp_err_to_name(e));
        s_tick_running = false;
        memset(&s_tick_cfg, 0, sizeof(s_tick_cfg));
        (void)gptimer_disable(s_tick_timer);
        (void)gptimer_del_timer(s_tick_timer);
        s_tick_timer = NULL;
        return false;
    }

    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "tick started: period_us=%u", (unsigned)cfg->tick_period_us);
    return true;
}

void nocti_timebase_stop_tick(void)
{
    if (!s_tick_running && s_tick_timer == NULL) return;

    s_tick_running = false;
    memset(&s_tick_cfg, 0, sizeof(s_tick_cfg));

    if (s_tick_timer) {
        (void)gptimer_stop(s_tick_timer);
        (void)gptimer_disable(s_tick_timer);
        (void)gptimer_del_timer(s_tick_timer);
        s_tick_timer = NULL;
    }

    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "tick stopped");
}

void nocti_timebase_busywait_us(uint32_t us)
{
    // slash: busywait pakai base timer, presisi dan tidak pakai vTaskDelay
    const nocti_time_us_t t0 = nocti_timebase_now_us();
    const nocti_time_us_t t_end = t0 + (nocti_time_us_t)us;

    while (nocti_timebase_now_us() < t_end) {
        __asm__ __volatile__("nop");
    }
}

void nocti_timebase_sleep_until_us(nocti_time_us_t t_us)
{
    // /learn strategi anti jitter
    // 1) kalau masih jauh, tidur kasar pakai vTaskDelay supaya CPU tidak terkunci
    // 2) mendekati deadline, masuk busywait tail supaya presisi
    const nocti_time_us_t spin_tail_us = 1500; // slash: tail 1.5 ms, bisa kamu tuning

    while (1) {
        nocti_time_us_t now = nocti_timebase_now_us();
        nocti_time_us_t diff = t_us - now;

        if (diff <= 0) return;

        if (diff > spin_tail_us) {
            // slash: tidur sampai mendekati deadline
            nocti_time_us_t sleep_us = diff - spin_tail_us;

            // slash: vTaskDelay resolusinya ms, jadi konversi dan sisakan tail
            uint32_t sleep_ms = (uint32_t)(sleep_us / 1000);
            if (sleep_ms > 0) {
                vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(sleep_ms));
            } else {
                taskYIELD();
            }
        } else {
            // slash: tail presisi
            nocti_timebase_busywait_us((uint32_t)diff);
            return;
        }
    }
}


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_wifi\include\nocti_wifi.h ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_wifi/include/nocti_wifi.h
// ============================================================
//
// NoctiNIRS WiFi SoftAP + UDP transport
//
// Fitur:
// - SoftAP + DHCP
// - UDP CTRL RX port 3333 (PING, HELLO, START, STOP, INFO)
// - UDP STREAM TX port 3334 (baca dari nocti_stream lalu dibungkus UDP header)
//
// slash:
// /belajar: START akan mengunci target IP pengirim dan mengaktifkan streaming
// /belajar: STOP mematikan streaming dan tidak mengirim data via UDP
// /belajar: UDP stream packet punya seq 32-bit untuk deteksi loss dan session_id untuk pemisah sesi
// ============================================================

#pragma once
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>

#include "esp_err.h"
#include "freertos/FreeRTOS.h"

typedef struct nocti_stream_t nocti_stream_t;

// ============================================================
// UDP stream packet v1
//
// Tujuan
// - Membuat payload stream (nocti_stream frame) lebih robust di UDP
// - Menyediakan seq 32-bit, session_id, dan timestamp
//
// Konvensi
// - payload = 1 buah nocti_stream frame utuh (hdr 12 + payload + crc16)
// - crc16 = CRC16-CCITT-FALSE dari header tanpa field crc16 + payload
// ============================================================

#define NOCTI_UDP_MAGIC      (0x4E4F4354u)  // slash: 'NOCT'
#define NOCTI_UDP_VER_V1     (1u)

typedef enum {
    NOCTI_UDP_TYPE_INVALID = 0,
    NOCTI_UDP_TYPE_STREAM  = 1,            // slash: payload berisi nocti_stream frame
    NOCTI_UDP_TYPE_STATUS  = 2,            // slash: optional, payload status ringkas
} nocti_udp_type_t;

#pragma pack(push, 1)
typedef struct {
    uint32_t magic;         // slash: NOCTI_UDP_MAGIC
    uint8_t  ver;           // slash: NOCTI_UDP_VER_V1
    uint8_t  type;          // slash: nocti_udp_type_t
    uint16_t flags;         // slash: reserved, set 0

    uint32_t session_id;    // slash: naik tiap START
    uint32_t seq;           // slash: naik tiap paket stream
    uint64_t timestamp_us;  // slash: esp_timer_get_time monotonic

    uint16_t payload_len;   // slash: bytes payload
    uint16_t crc16;         // slash: CRC16-CCITT-FALSE
} nocti_udp_hdr_v1_t;
#pragma pack(pop)

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

typedef struct {
    uint32_t ip4;   // slash: host order
    uint16_t port;  // slash: host order
} nocti_wifi_peer_t;

typedef struct nocti_wifi_t nocti_wifi_t;

typedef void (*nocti_wifi_cmd_cb_t)(nocti_wifi_t *self,
                                   const uint8_t *data,
                                   size_t len,
                                   const nocti_wifi_peer_t *from,
                                   void *user_ctx);

typedef struct {
    // SoftAP identity
    const char *ap_ssid;
    const char *ap_pass;
    uint8_t ap_channel;
    uint8_t ap_max_conn;

    // Optional custom subnet
    bool use_custom_ip;
    uint32_t ap_ip;       // slash: host order
    uint32_t ap_gw;       // slash: host order
    uint32_t ap_netmask;  // slash: host order

    // UDP ports
    uint16_t udp_ctrl_port;    // slash: default 3333
    uint16_t udp_stream_port;  // slash: default 3334

    // Built in cmd parser
    bool enable_builtin_cmd;

    // Advert broadcast optional
    bool enable_advert;
    uint32_t advert_period_ms;

    // Optional user cmd callback
    nocti_wifi_cmd_cb_t cmd_cb;
    void *cmd_cb_ctx;

    // Stream integration
    nocti_stream_t *stream;
    bool enable_stream_tx;
    uint32_t stream_read_timeout_ms;

    // UDP packet behavior
    bool use_udp_header;          // slash: true untuk membungkus dengan nocti_udp_hdr_v1
    bool reset_session_on_start;  // slash: true untuk session_id++ pada START

    // Task configs (stack depth dalam words)
    uint16_t ctrl_task_stack_words;
    UBaseType_t ctrl_task_prio;

    uint16_t adv_task_stack_words;
    UBaseType_t adv_task_prio;

    uint16_t tx_task_stack_words;
    UBaseType_t tx_task_prio;

} nocti_wifi_cfg_t;

struct nocti_wifi_t {
    bool inited;

    bool ap_started;
    bool sta_connected;

    bool has_target;
    nocti_wifi_peer_t target;

    bool streaming_enabled;

    // UDP stream sequencing
    uint32_t session_id;
    uint32_t stream_seq;

    nocti_wifi_cfg_t cfg;

    void *ap_netif;   // slash: esp_netif_t
    void *ev;         // slash: EventGroupHandle_t
    void *mutex;      // slash: SemaphoreHandle_t

    int sock_ctrl;
    int sock_tx;

    void *ctrl_task;
    void *adv_task;
    void *tx_task;

    bool stop_req;
};

void nocti_wifi_cfg_set_defaults(nocti_wifi_cfg_t *cfg);

esp_err_t nocti_wifi_init(nocti_wifi_t *w, const nocti_wifi_cfg_t *cfg);
esp_err_t nocti_wifi_stop(nocti_wifi_t *w);

bool nocti_wifi_is_sta_connected(const nocti_wifi_t *w);
bool nocti_wifi_has_target(const nocti_wifi_t *w);
bool nocti_wifi_is_streaming(const nocti_wifi_t *w);

uint32_t nocti_wifi_session_id(const nocti_wifi_t *w);
uint32_t nocti_wifi_stream_seq(const nocti_wifi_t *w);

esp_err_t nocti_wifi_send_ctrl_to(nocti_wifi_t *w,
                                 const nocti_wifi_peer_t *to,
                                 const void *data,
                                 size_t len);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\components\nocti_wifi\nocti_wifi.c ==========

// ============================================================
// File: components/nocti_wifi/nocti_wifi.c
// ============================================================
//
// Implementasi SoftAP + UDP control + UDP stream
//
// slash:
// /belajar: ctrl socket bind port 3333 untuk recvfrom
// /belajar: tx socket dipakai untuk sendto stream dan broadcast advert
// /belajar: streaming_enabled dipicu oleh START dan STOP
// ============================================================

#include "nocti_wifi.h"

#include <string.h>
#include <unistd.h>
// slash: sendmsg butuh iovec
#include <sys/uio.h>

#include "freertos/task.h"
#include "freertos/event_groups.h"
#include "freertos/semphr.h"

#include "nvs_flash.h"

#include "esp_netif.h"
#include "esp_event.h"
#include "esp_wifi.h"
#include "esp_timer.h"
#include "lwip/sockets.h"
#include "lwip/inet.h"

#include "nocti_log.h"
#include "nocti_crc.h"
#include "nocti_stream.h"

static const char *TAG = "nocti_wifi";

#define EV_AP_STARTED     (1u << 0)
#define EV_STA_CONNECTED  (1u << 1)

static inline uint32_t ip4_host_from_inaddr(uint32_t inaddr_net_order)
{
    return ntohl(inaddr_net_order);
}

static inline uint32_t ip4_net_from_host(uint32_t ip4_host)
{
    return htonl(ip4_host);
}

static inline bool starts_with(const uint8_t *p, size_t n, const char *s)
{
    size_t m = strlen(s);
    if (n < m) return false;
    return (memcmp(p, s, m) == 0);
}

static void wifi_event_handler(void *arg, esp_event_base_t base, int32_t id, void *data);

static void ctrl_task_entry(void *arg);
static void adv_task_entry(void *arg);
static void tx_task_entry(void *arg);

void nocti_wifi_cfg_set_defaults(nocti_wifi_cfg_t *cfg)
{
    if (!cfg) return;
    memset(cfg, 0, sizeof(*cfg));

    cfg->ap_ssid = "REFERENCE_WIFI_SSID";
    cfg->ap_pass = "REFERENCE_WIFI_PASSWORD";
    cfg->ap_channel = 6;
    cfg->ap_max_conn = 2;

    cfg->use_custom_ip = false;
    cfg->ap_ip = ip4_host_from_inaddr(inet_addr("192.168.4.1"));
    cfg->ap_gw = ip4_host_from_inaddr(inet_addr("192.168.4.1"));
    cfg->ap_netmask = ip4_host_from_inaddr(inet_addr("255.255.255.0"));

    cfg->udp_ctrl_port = 3333;
    cfg->udp_stream_port = 3334;

    cfg->enable_builtin_cmd = true;

    cfg->enable_advert = true;
    cfg->advert_period_ms = 1000;

    cfg->stream = NULL;
    cfg->enable_stream_tx = false;
    cfg->stream_read_timeout_ms = 10;

    cfg->use_udp_header = true;
    cfg->reset_session_on_start = true;

    cfg->ctrl_task_stack_words = 4096;
    cfg->ctrl_task_prio = 6;

    cfg->adv_task_stack_words = 2048;
    cfg->adv_task_prio = 4;

    cfg->tx_task_stack_words = 4096;
    cfg->tx_task_prio = 7;
}

static esp_err_t set_softap_ip_if_needed(nocti_wifi_t *w, esp_netif_t *netif)
{
    if (!w->cfg.use_custom_ip) return ESP_OK;

    ESP_ERROR_CHECK(esp_netif_dhcps_stop(netif));

    esp_netif_ip_info_t ip = {0};
    ip.ip.addr = ip4_net_from_host(w->cfg.ap_ip);
    ip.gw.addr = ip4_net_from_host(w->cfg.ap_gw);
    ip.netmask.addr = ip4_net_from_host(w->cfg.ap_netmask);

    ESP_ERROR_CHECK(esp_netif_set_ip_info(netif, &ip));
    ESP_ERROR_CHECK(esp_netif_dhcps_start(netif));
    return ESP_OK;
}

static void compute_broadcast_ip(esp_netif_t *netif, uint32_t *out_bcast_host)
{
    if (!out_bcast_host) return;

    esp_netif_ip_info_t ip = {0};
    if (esp_netif_get_ip_info(netif, &ip) != ESP_OK) {
        *out_bcast_host = ip4_host_from_inaddr(inet_addr("192.168.4.255"));
        return;
    }

    uint32_t ip_host = ntohl(ip.ip.addr);
    uint32_t nm_host = ntohl(ip.netmask.addr);

    uint32_t bcast_host = (ip_host & nm_host) | (~nm_host);
    *out_bcast_host = bcast_host;
}

static esp_err_t wifi_softap_start(nocti_wifi_t *w)
{
    // slash: NVS diperlukan oleh WiFi internal
    esp_err_t ret = nvs_flash_init();
    if (ret == ESP_ERR_NVS_NO_FREE_PAGES || ret == ESP_ERR_NVS_NEW_VERSION_FOUND) {
        ESP_ERROR_CHECK(nvs_flash_erase());
        ret = nvs_flash_init();
    }
    if (ret != ESP_OK) return ret;

    ret = esp_netif_init();
    if (ret != ESP_OK && ret != ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE) return ret;

    ret = esp_event_loop_create_default();
    if (ret != ESP_OK && ret != ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE) return ret;

    esp_netif_t *ap_netif = esp_netif_create_default_wifi_ap();
    if (!ap_netif) return ESP_FAIL;
    w->ap_netif = ap_netif;

    ret = set_softap_ip_if_needed(w, ap_netif);
    if (ret != ESP_OK) return ret;

    wifi_init_config_t icfg = WIFI_INIT_CONFIG_DEFAULT();
    ret = esp_wifi_init(&icfg);
    if (ret != ESP_OK && ret != ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE) return ret;

    ESP_ERROR_CHECK(esp_wifi_set_storage(WIFI_STORAGE_RAM));

    ESP_ERROR_CHECK(esp_event_handler_register(WIFI_EVENT, ESP_EVENT_ANY_ID, &wifi_event_handler, w));
    ESP_ERROR_CHECK(esp_event_handler_register(IP_EVENT, IP_EVENT_AP_STAIPASSIGNED, &wifi_event_handler, w));

    wifi_config_t cfg = {0};
    strncpy((char *)cfg.ap.ssid, w->cfg.ap_ssid, sizeof(cfg.ap.ssid));
    cfg.ap.ssid_len = (uint8_t)strlen(w->cfg.ap_ssid);

    if (w->cfg.ap_pass && strlen(w->cfg.ap_pass) > 0) {
        strncpy((char *)cfg.ap.password, w->cfg.ap_pass, sizeof(cfg.ap.password));
        cfg.ap.authmode = WIFI_AUTH_WPA2_PSK;
    } else {
        cfg.ap.password[0] = 0;
        cfg.ap.authmode = WIFI_AUTH_OPEN;
    }

    cfg.ap.channel = w->cfg.ap_channel;
    cfg.ap.max_connection = w->cfg.ap_max_conn;

    ESP_ERROR_CHECK(esp_wifi_set_mode(WIFI_MODE_AP));
    ESP_ERROR_CHECK(esp_wifi_set_config(WIFI_IF_AP, &cfg));
    ESP_ERROR_CHECK(esp_wifi_start());

    esp_netif_ip_info_t ip = {0};
    ESP_ERROR_CHECK(esp_netif_get_ip_info(ap_netif, &ip));

    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "SoftAP SSID: %s", w->cfg.ap_ssid);
    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "SoftAP PASS: %s", (w->cfg.ap_pass && strlen(w->cfg.ap_pass)) ? w->cfg.ap_pass : "(open)");
    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "SoftAP channel: %u", (unsigned)w->cfg.ap_channel);
    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "AP IP: " IPSTR, IP2STR(&ip.ip));
    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "AP GW: " IPSTR, IP2STR(&ip.gw));
    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "AP NM: " IPSTR, IP2STR(&ip.netmask));

    return ESP_OK;
}

static void wifi_event_handler(void *arg, esp_event_base_t base, int32_t id, void *data)
{
    nocti_wifi_t *w = (nocti_wifi_t *)arg;
    if (!w) return;

    EventGroupHandle_t ev = (EventGroupHandle_t)w->ev;

    if (base == WIFI_EVENT) {

        if (id == WIFI_EVENT_AP_START) {
            w->ap_started = true;
            xEventGroupSetBits(ev, EV_AP_STARTED);
            NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "SoftAP started");
        }

        if (id == WIFI_EVENT_AP_STACONNECTED) {
            wifi_event_ap_staconnected_t *e = (wifi_event_ap_staconnected_t *)data;
            w->sta_connected = true;
            xEventGroupSetBits(ev, EV_STA_CONNECTED);
            NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "STA connected aid=%d", (int)e->aid);
        }

        if (id == WIFI_EVENT_AP_STADISCONNECTED) {
            wifi_event_ap_stadisconnected_t *e = (wifi_event_ap_stadisconnected_t *)data;
            (void)e;
            w->sta_connected = false;
            xEventGroupClearBits(ev, EV_STA_CONNECTED);
            NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "STA disconnected");
        }
    }

    if (base == IP_EVENT && id == IP_EVENT_AP_STAIPASSIGNED) {
        ip_event_ap_staipassigned_t *e = (ip_event_ap_staipassigned_t *)data;
        NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "STA got IP: " IPSTR, IP2STR(&e->ip));
    }
}

static esp_err_t set_target_locked(nocti_wifi_t *w, const nocti_wifi_peer_t *peer)
{
    w->target = *peer;
    w->has_target = true;

    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "Target set to %u.%u.%u.%u",
               (unsigned)((peer->ip4 >> 24) & 0xFF),
               (unsigned)((peer->ip4 >> 16) & 0xFF),
               (unsigned)((peer->ip4 >> 8) & 0xFF),
               (unsigned)((peer->ip4 >> 0) & 0xFF));
    return ESP_OK;
}

static void handle_builtin_cmd(nocti_wifi_t *w,
                               const uint8_t *rx,
                               size_t n,
                               const nocti_wifi_peer_t *from)
{
    if (!w->cfg.enable_builtin_cmd) return;

    const char *resp = "NOCTI ACK\n";

    SemaphoreHandle_t m = (SemaphoreHandle_t)w->mutex;
    xSemaphoreTake(m, portMAX_DELAY);

    if (starts_with(rx, n, "PING")) {
        resp = "PONG\n";
    } else if (starts_with(rx, n, "HELLO")) {
        (void)set_target_locked(w, from);
        resp = "NOCTI HELLO OK\n";
    } else if (starts_with(rx, n, "INFO")) {
        resp = "NOCTI INFO ctrl=3333 stream=3334\n";
    } else if (starts_with(rx, n, "START")) {
        // slash: START mengunci target dan mengaktifkan streaming
        // slash: session_id naik agar receiver bisa memisahkan sesi
        (void)set_target_locked(w, from);

        if (w->cfg.reset_session_on_start) {
            w->session_id += 1u;
            w->stream_seq = 0u;
        }

        w->streaming_enabled = true;
        resp = "START_OK\n";
    } else if (starts_with(rx, n, "STOP")) {
        w->streaming_enabled = false;
        resp = "STOP_OK\n";
    }

    xSemaphoreGive(m);

    (void)nocti_wifi_send_ctrl_to(w, from, resp, strlen(resp));
}

esp_err_t nocti_wifi_send_ctrl_to(nocti_wifi_t *w,
                                 const nocti_wifi_peer_t *to,
                                 const void *data,
                                 size_t len)
{
    if (!w || !to || !data || len == 0) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (w->sock_tx < 0) return ESP_FAIL;

    struct sockaddr_in dst = {0};
    dst.sin_family = AF_INET;
    dst.sin_port = htons(to->port);
    dst.sin_addr.s_addr = ip4_net_from_host(to->ip4);

    int r = sendto(w->sock_tx, data, (int)len, 0, (struct sockaddr *)&dst, sizeof(dst));
    return (r >= 0) ? ESP_OK : ESP_FAIL;
}

static esp_err_t send_stream_to_target(nocti_wifi_t *w, const void *data, size_t len)
{
    if (!w || !data || len == 0) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!w->has_target) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

    struct sockaddr_in dst = {0};
    dst.sin_family = AF_INET;
    dst.sin_port = htons(w->cfg.udp_stream_port);
    dst.sin_addr.s_addr = ip4_net_from_host(w->target.ip4);

    int r = sendto(w->sock_tx, data, (int)len, 0, (struct sockaddr *)&dst, sizeof(dst));
    return (r >= 0) ? ESP_OK : ESP_FAIL;
}

// slash: versi iovec agar tidak perlu memcpy payload
static esp_err_t send_stream_to_target_iov(nocti_wifi_t *w,
                                           const struct iovec *iov,
                                           int iovcnt)
{
    if (!w || !iov || iovcnt <= 0) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!w->has_target) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE;

    struct sockaddr_in dst = {0};
    dst.sin_family = AF_INET;
    dst.sin_port = htons(w->cfg.udp_stream_port);
    dst.sin_addr.s_addr = ip4_net_from_host(w->target.ip4);

    struct msghdr msg;
    memset(&msg, 0, sizeof(msg));
    msg.msg_name = &dst;
    msg.msg_namelen = sizeof(dst);
    msg.msg_iov = (struct iovec *)iov;
    msg.msg_iovlen = (size_t)iovcnt;

    int r = sendmsg(w->sock_tx, &msg, 0);
    return (r >= 0) ? ESP_OK : ESP_FAIL;
}

// ============================================================
// UDP header builder v1 (zero copy)
// slash: payload tidak disalin, hanya header yang dibuat
// ============================================================

static void build_udp_stream_hdr_v1(nocti_wifi_t *w,
                                    size_t payload_len,
                                    nocti_udp_hdr_v1_t *out_hdr)
{
    // slash: asumsi dipanggil saat stream aktif, payload_len sudah valid
    SemaphoreHandle_t m = (SemaphoreHandle_t)w->mutex;
    xSemaphoreTake(m, portMAX_DELAY);

    w->stream_seq += 1u;
    uint32_t seq = w->stream_seq;
    uint32_t session_id = w->session_id;

    xSemaphoreGive(m);

    memset(out_hdr, 0, sizeof(*out_hdr));
    out_hdr->magic = NOCTI_UDP_MAGIC;
    out_hdr->ver = (uint8_t)NOCTI_UDP_VER_V1;
    out_hdr->type = (uint8_t)NOCTI_UDP_TYPE_STREAM;
    out_hdr->flags = 0;

    out_hdr->session_id = session_id;
    out_hdr->seq = seq;
    out_hdr->timestamp_us = (uint64_t)esp_timer_get_time();

    out_hdr->payload_len = (uint16_t)payload_len;
    out_hdr->crc16 = 0;
}

static void ctrl_task_entry(void *arg)
{
    nocti_wifi_t *w = (nocti_wifi_t *)arg;
    EventGroupHandle_t ev = (EventGroupHandle_t)w->ev;

    xEventGroupWaitBits(ev, EV_AP_STARTED, pdFALSE, pdTRUE, portMAX_DELAY);

    int sock = socket(AF_INET, SOCK_DGRAM, IPPROTO_IP);
    if (sock < 0) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "ctrl socket failed");
        vTaskDelete(NULL);
        return;
    }

    struct sockaddr_in bind_addr = {0};
    bind_addr.sin_family = AF_INET;
    bind_addr.sin_port = htons(w->cfg.udp_ctrl_port);
    bind_addr.sin_addr.s_addr = htonl(INADDR_ANY);

    if (bind(sock, (struct sockaddr *)&bind_addr, sizeof(bind_addr)) != 0) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "ctrl bind failed");
        close(sock);
        vTaskDelete(NULL);
        return;
    }

    w->sock_ctrl = sock;
    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "UDP CTRL listening on port %u", (unsigned)w->cfg.udp_ctrl_port);

    while (!w->stop_req) {

        uint8_t rx[256];
        struct sockaddr_in from_addr = {0};
        socklen_t from_len = sizeof(from_addr);

        int n = recvfrom(sock, rx, sizeof(rx), 0, (struct sockaddr *)&from_addr, &from_len);
        if (n <= 0) {
            vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(20));
            continue;
        }

        nocti_wifi_peer_t from = {0};
        from.ip4 = ntohl(from_addr.sin_addr.s_addr);
        from.port = ntohs(from_addr.sin_port);

        handle_builtin_cmd(w, rx, (size_t)n, &from);

        if (w->cfg.cmd_cb) {
            w->cfg.cmd_cb(w, rx, (size_t)n, &from, w->cfg.cmd_cb_ctx);
        }
    }

    close(sock);
    w->sock_ctrl = -1;
    vTaskDelete(NULL);
}

static void adv_task_entry(void *arg)
{
    nocti_wifi_t *w = (nocti_wifi_t *)arg;
    EventGroupHandle_t ev = (EventGroupHandle_t)w->ev;

    xEventGroupWaitBits(ev, EV_STA_CONNECTED, pdFALSE, pdTRUE, portMAX_DELAY);

    uint32_t bcast_ip_host = 0;
    compute_broadcast_ip((esp_netif_t *)w->ap_netif, &bcast_ip_host);

    struct sockaddr_in dst = {0};
    dst.sin_family = AF_INET;
    dst.sin_port = htons(w->cfg.udp_ctrl_port);
    dst.sin_addr.s_addr = htonl(bcast_ip_host);

    uint32_t seq = 0;

    while (!w->stop_req) {
        if (!w->cfg.enable_advert) {
            vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(200));
            continue;
        }

        seq++;

        char msg[128];
        int m = snprintf(msg, sizeof(msg),
                         "NOCTI ADVERT seq=%lu ctrl=%u stream=%u\n",
                         (unsigned long)seq,
                         (unsigned)w->cfg.udp_ctrl_port,
                         (unsigned)w->cfg.udp_stream_port);

        sendto(w->sock_tx, msg, m, 0, (struct sockaddr *)&dst, sizeof(dst));
        vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(w->cfg.advert_period_ms));
    }

    vTaskDelete(NULL);
}

static void tx_task_entry(void *arg)
{
    nocti_wifi_t *w = (nocti_wifi_t *)arg;

    // slash: nol-copy dari stream
    // slash: item_ptr menunjuk ke memory internal ringbuffer
    const uint8_t *item_ptr = NULL;
    size_t item_len = 0;
    void *owner = NULL;

    while (!w->stop_req) {

        if (!w->cfg.enable_stream_tx || !w->cfg.stream) {
            vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(200));
            continue;
        }

        // slash: hanya kirim jika START sudah diterima
        if (!w->streaming_enabled || !w->has_target) {
            vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(20));
            continue;
        }

        // slash: reset token sebelum receive untuk menghindari reuse saat error
        owner = NULL;
        item_ptr = NULL;
        item_len = 0;

        esp_err_t r = nocti_stream_recv_item(w->cfg.stream,
                                             &item_ptr,
                                             &item_len,
                                             &owner,
                                             w->cfg.stream_read_timeout_ms);

        if (r != ESP_OK) continue;

        if (item_len == 0 || !item_ptr) {
            if (owner && item_ptr) {
                nocti_stream_return_item(owner, (void *)item_ptr);
            }
            continue;
        }

        if (w->cfg.use_udp_header) {
            // slash: kirim header dan payload sebagai dua segmen (iovec)
            // slash: ini menghindari memcpy payload besar

            nocti_udp_hdr_v1_t h;
            build_udp_stream_hdr_v1(w, item_len, &h);

            // slash: hitung CRC dari header (crc16=0) lalu payload
            uint16_t crc = nocti_crc16_init();
            crc = nocti_crc16_ccitt_false_update(crc, &h, sizeof(h));
            crc = nocti_crc16_ccitt_false_update(crc, item_ptr, item_len);
            h.crc16 = crc;

            struct iovec iov[2];
            iov[0].iov_base = &h;
            iov[0].iov_len  = sizeof(h);
            iov[1].iov_base = (void *)item_ptr;
            iov[1].iov_len  = item_len;

            (void)send_stream_to_target_iov(w, iov, 2);
        } else {
            // slash: tanpa header UDP, langsung kirim bytes stream
            (void)send_stream_to_target(w, item_ptr, item_len);
        }

        // slash: wajib return item agar ringbuffer tidak leak
        if (owner && item_ptr) {
            nocti_stream_return_item(owner, (void *)item_ptr);
        }
    }

    vTaskDelete(NULL);
}

esp_err_t nocti_wifi_init(nocti_wifi_t *w, const nocti_wifi_cfg_t *cfg)
{
    if (!w || !cfg) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    memset(w, 0, sizeof(*w));
    w->cfg = *cfg;

    w->sock_ctrl = -1;
    w->sock_tx = -1;

    w->session_id = 0u;
    w->stream_seq = 0u;

    w->ev = xEventGroupCreate();
    if (!w->ev) return ESP_ERR_NO_MEM;

    w->mutex = xSemaphoreCreateMutex();
    if (!w->mutex) return ESP_ERR_NO_MEM;

    esp_err_t ret = wifi_softap_start(w);
    if (ret != ESP_OK) return ret;

    int sock_tx = socket(AF_INET, SOCK_DGRAM, IPPROTO_IP);
    if (sock_tx < 0) return ESP_FAIL;

    int enable = 1;
    setsockopt(sock_tx, SOL_SOCKET, SO_BROADCAST, &enable, sizeof(enable));
    w->sock_tx = sock_tx;

    TaskHandle_t t = NULL;

    xTaskCreatePinnedToCore(ctrl_task_entry, "nocti_udp_ctrl",
                            w->cfg.ctrl_task_stack_words,
                            w, w->cfg.ctrl_task_prio, &t, 0);
    w->ctrl_task = t;

    xTaskCreatePinnedToCore(adv_task_entry, "nocti_udp_adv",
                            w->cfg.adv_task_stack_words,
                            w, w->cfg.adv_task_prio, &t, 0);
    w->adv_task = t;

    if (w->cfg.enable_stream_tx && w->cfg.stream) {
        xTaskCreatePinnedToCore(tx_task_entry, "nocti_udp_tx",
                                w->cfg.tx_task_stack_words,
                                w, w->cfg.tx_task_prio, &t, 0);
        w->tx_task = t;
    }

    w->inited = true;

    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "Ready. Connect to SSID %s then send START to UDP %u",
               w->cfg.ap_ssid, (unsigned)w->cfg.udp_ctrl_port);

    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t nocti_wifi_stop(nocti_wifi_t *w)
{
    if (!w) return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    if (!w->inited) return ESP_OK;

    w->stop_req = true;

    if (w->sock_ctrl >= 0) {
        close(w->sock_ctrl);
        w->sock_ctrl = -1;
    }
    if (w->sock_tx >= 0) {
        close(w->sock_tx);
        w->sock_tx = -1;
    }

    esp_wifi_stop();
    w->inited = false;
    return ESP_OK;
}

bool nocti_wifi_is_sta_connected(const nocti_wifi_t *w)
{
    return w ? w->sta_connected : false;
}

bool nocti_wifi_has_target(const nocti_wifi_t *w)
{
    return w ? w->has_target : false;
}

bool nocti_wifi_is_streaming(const nocti_wifi_t *w)
{
    return w ? w->streaming_enabled : false;
}

uint32_t nocti_wifi_session_id(const nocti_wifi_t *w)
{
    return w ? w->session_id : 0u;
}

uint32_t nocti_wifi_stream_seq(const nocti_wifi_t *w)
{
    return w ? w->stream_seq : 0u;
}


// ========== FILE: D:\Yerri\Thesis\NOCTINIRSv2\main\app_main.c ==========

// ============================================================
// File: main/app_main.c
// NoctiNIRS combined bringup: fNIRS (ADS131M04) + Biopot (ADS1299) + WiFi UDP
//
// Tujuan:
// - START dari UDP CTRL (port 3333) memulai sesi baru
// - fNIRS berjalan via scheduler slot (LED multiplex) dan push FNIRS_FRAME ke nocti_stream
// - Biopot berjalan via task reader 250 SPS dan push BIOPOT_FRAME batching 10 sample
// - nocti_wifi TX task membaca nocti_stream lalu kirim via UDP STREAM (port 3334)
//
// slash:
// /belajar: UDP hanya transport. Fs biopot ditentukan ADS1299 (misal 250 Hz), bukan oleh UDP.
// /belajar: agar bentuk ECG terlihat di PC, decoder harus plot sampel 250 Hz, bukan data log decimate.
// /belajar: parsing receiver: UDP hdr v1 -> payload = 1 frame nocti_stream utuh.
// ============================================================

#include <string.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>

#include "freertos/FreeRTOS.h"
#include "freertos/task.h"

#include "esp_err.h"

#include "nocti_log.h"
#include "nocti_timebase.h"

#include "nocti_board.h"

#include "nocti_led_driver.h"
#include "nocti_fnirs.h"
#include "nocti_scheduler.h"

#include "nocti_biopot.h"

#include "nocti_stream.h"
#include "nocti_wifi.h"

static const char *TAG = "app_combo";

// ============================================================
// fNIRS schedule params (locked test)
// ============================================================

#define FNIRS_FRAME_FS_HZ          (10u)
#define FNIRS_SLOT_COUNT           (20u)
#define FNIRS_TICK_PERIOD_US       (5000u)
#define FNIRS_FRAME_PERIOD_US      (100000u)

// slash: window averaging
// /belajar: K fixed = jumlah sampel ADC yang kita ambil di dalam window LED ON
#define FNIRS_K_FIXED              (3u)

// slash: ring buffer untuk frame ADC kontinu
// /belajar: semakin besar, semakin aman dari jitter task dan WiFi, tapi makan RAM
#define FNIRS_ADC_RB_SIZE          (1024u)
// slash:
// /belajar: ring buffer ADC ini disimpan di RAM statik (bukan heap).
// /belajar: ukuran terlalu besar mengurangi heap untuk WiFi dan ringbuffer stream.
#define FNIRS_ADC_SEARCH_BACK      (64u)

#define FNIRS_SETTLE_US            (200u)
#define FNIRS_WINDOW_US            (3200u)
#define FNIRS_ON_TIME_US           (3700u)
#define FNIRS_OFF_GAP_US           (200u)

#define FNIRS_CURR_735_MA          (10u)
#define FNIRS_CURR_850_MA          (10u)

// ============================================================
// Biopot batching params
// ============================================================

#define BIOPOT_BATCH_N             (NOCTI_BIOPOT_FRAME_MAX_SAMPLES)

// ============================================================
// Globals
// ============================================================

static nocti_board_t  g_board;
static nocti_stream_t g_stream;
static nocti_wifi_t   g_wifi;

static TaskHandle_t g_biopot_task = NULL;
static TaskHandle_t g_fnirs_adc_task = NULL;

static volatile bool g_run = false;
static volatile bool g_biopot_run = false;
static volatile bool g_fnirs_adc_run = false;

static uint32_t g_session_id = 0;

// ============================================================
// fNIRS frame accumulator
// ============================================================

static nocti_payload_fnirs_frame_v1_t g_fnirs_frame;
static uint16_t g_fnirs_frame_id = 0;

// slash: slot timing cache
// /belajar: kita simpan t_sample, lalu hitung average di SLOT_END (setelah window selesai)
static nocti_time_us_t g_slot_t0_us[FNIRS_SLOT_COUNT];
static uint32_t g_slot_pending_mask = 0;

// slash: ADC ring buffer (producer baca ADS131 terus menerus)
typedef struct {
    nocti_time_us_t ts_us;
    int32_t ch[4];
    bool crc_ok;
} fnirs_adc_frame_t;

static fnirs_adc_frame_t g_adc_rb[FNIRS_ADC_RB_SIZE];
static volatile uint32_t g_adc_wr = 0;

static uint32_t g_adc_err_cnt = 0;
static uint32_t g_adc_crc_bad_cnt = 0;

static inline void fnirs_frame_reset(void)
{
    memset(&g_fnirs_frame, 0, sizeof(g_fnirs_frame));
    g_fnirs_frame.version = NOCTI_PAYLOAD_FNIRS_FRAME_V1_VERSION;
    g_fnirs_frame.flags = NOCTI_FNIRS_FRAME_F_NONE;
    g_fnirs_frame.frame_id = g_fnirs_frame_id;

    g_fnirs_frame.valid_mask = 0;
    g_fnirs_frame.underk_mask = 0;
    g_fnirs_frame.crc_mask = 0;
    memset(g_fnirs_frame.avg, 0, sizeof(g_fnirs_frame.avg));
    memset(g_fnirs_frame.n_used, 0, sizeof(g_fnirs_frame.n_used));
}

static inline uint8_t wl_to_slot_subindex(nocti_wavelength_t wl)
{
    return (wl == NOCTI_WL_735) ? 0u : 1u;
}

// ============================================================
// Helpers
// ============================================================

static bool starts_with(const uint8_t *buf, size_t n, const char *s)
{
    size_t m = strlen(s);
    if (n < m) return false;
    return (memcmp(buf, s, m) == 0);
}

static uint16_t ads1299_fs_from_dr_bits(uint8_t dr_bits)
{
    switch (dr_bits & 0x07u) {
        case 0x00: return 16000u;
        case 0x01: return 8000u;
        case 0x02: return 4000u;
        case 0x03: return 2000u;
        case 0x04: return 1000u;
        case 0x05: return 500u;
        case 0x06: return 250u;
        case 0x07: return 125u;
        default:   return 250u;
    }
}

static void push_fnirs_cfg_once(nocti_time_us_t now_us)
{
    nocti_payload_fnirs_cfg_v1_t cfg;
    memset(&cfg, 0, sizeof(cfg));
    cfg.version = NOCTI_PAYLOAD_FNIRS_CFG_V1_VERSION;
    cfg.map_mode = (uint8_t)NOCTI_FNIRS_MAP_LEDID2_WL;
    cfg.frame_fs_hz = (uint8_t)FNIRS_FRAME_FS_HZ;
    cfg.slot_count = (uint8_t)FNIRS_SLOT_COUNT;
    cfg.k_fixed = (uint8_t)FNIRS_K_FIXED;
    cfg.current_735_mA = (uint8_t)FNIRS_CURR_735_MA;
    cfg.current_850_mA = (uint8_t)FNIRS_CURR_850_MA;
    cfg.settle_us = (uint16_t)FNIRS_SETTLE_US;
    cfg.window_us = (uint16_t)FNIRS_WINDOW_US;

    (void)nocti_stream_push_fnirs_cfg_v1(&g_stream, now_us, &cfg);
}

// ============================================================
// fNIRS ADC producer task
// ============================================================

static inline uint32_t adc_wr_snapshot(void)
{
    // slash: baca wr secara aman, supaya consumer tidak baca entry yang sedang ditulis
    // /belajar: g_adc_wr hanya maju setelah entry selesai ditulis
    return g_adc_wr;
}

static void fnirs_adc_task_entry(void *arg)
{
    (void)arg;

    while (1) {
        ulTaskNotifyTake(pdTRUE, portMAX_DELAY);

        while (g_fnirs_adc_run) {
            nocti_fnirs_frame_t fr;
            esp_err_t err = nocti_fnirs_read_frame(&fr);
            if (err != ESP_OK) {
                g_adc_err_cnt++;
                continue;
            }

            if (!fr.crc_ok) g_adc_crc_bad_cnt++;

            // slash: commit entry, lalu advance write index
            // /belajar: consumer boleh baca index < wr_snapshot tanpa lock
            uint32_t w = g_adc_wr;
            fnirs_adc_frame_t *dst = &g_adc_rb[w % FNIRS_ADC_RB_SIZE];
            dst->ts_us = fr.timestamp_us;
            dst->ch[0] = fr.ch[0];
            dst->ch[1] = fr.ch[1];
            dst->ch[2] = fr.ch[2];
            dst->ch[3] = fr.ch[3];
            dst->crc_ok = fr.crc_ok;
            g_adc_wr = w + 1u;
        }
    }
}

static uint8_t fnirs_collect_and_average(nocti_time_us_t t0_us,
                                        nocti_time_us_t t1_us,
                                        uint8_t k_fixed,
                                        int32_t out_avg[4],
                                        bool *out_any_crc_bad)
{
    if (out_any_crc_bad) *out_any_crc_bad = false;
    if (!out_avg) return 0;

    // slash: init accumulator
    int64_t acc[4] = {0, 0, 0, 0};
    uint8_t n_used = 0;

    // slash: snapshot posisi write terakhir
    uint32_t wr = adc_wr_snapshot();

    // slash: ambil "K terbaru" di dalam window
    // /belajar: ini menghindari buffer besar di stack scheduler
    // /belajar: selama semua sampel berada di window LED ON, rata rata tetap valid
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < FNIRS_ADC_SEARCH_BACK; i++) {
        if (wr == 0) break;
        uint32_t idx = wr - 1u - i;
        const fnirs_adc_frame_t *e = &g_adc_rb[idx % FNIRS_ADC_RB_SIZE];

        if (e->ts_us < t0_us) break;
        if (e->ts_us > t1_us) continue;

        acc[0] += (int64_t)e->ch[0];
        acc[1] += (int64_t)e->ch[1];
        acc[2] += (int64_t)e->ch[2];
        acc[3] += (int64_t)e->ch[3];
        if (out_any_crc_bad && !e->crc_ok) *out_any_crc_bad = true;

        n_used++;
        if (n_used >= k_fixed) break;
    }

    if (n_used == 0) {
        out_avg[0] = 0;
        out_avg[1] = 0;
        out_avg[2] = 0;
        out_avg[3] = 0;
        return 0;
    }

    out_avg[0] = (int32_t)(acc[0] / (int64_t)n_used);
    out_avg[1] = (int32_t)(acc[1] / (int64_t)n_used);
    out_avg[2] = (int32_t)(acc[2] / (int64_t)n_used);
    out_avg[3] = (int32_t)(acc[3] / (int64_t)n_used);
    return n_used;
}

static void push_biopot_cfg_once(nocti_time_us_t now_us,
                                const nocti_biopot_cfg_t *bio_cfg,
                                uint8_t ch_enable_mask,
                                uint8_t bias_sensp_mask,
                                uint8_t bias_sensn_mask)
{
    nocti_payload_biopot_cfg_v1_t cfg;
    memset(&cfg, 0, sizeof(cfg));
    cfg.version = NOCTI_PAYLOAD_BIOPOT_CFG_V1_VERSION;
    cfg.fs_hz = ads1299_fs_from_dr_bits(bio_cfg->rate.dr_bits);
    cfg.n_ch = NOCTI_BIOPOT_NUM_CH;
    cfg.fmt = (uint8_t)NOCTI_BIOPOT_FMT_RAW_COUNTS;
    cfg.ch_enable_mask = ch_enable_mask;
    cfg.bias_sensp_mask = bias_sensp_mask;
    cfg.bias_sensn_mask = bias_sensn_mask;
    cfg.flags = bio_cfg->enable_bias ? (uint8_t)NOCTI_BIOPOT_CFG_F_BIAS_EN : 0u;
    cfg.vref_mV = (uint16_t)(bio_cfg->vref_volts * 1000.0f);

    for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        cfg.gain[i] = (uint8_t)bio_cfg->ch[i].gain;
    }

    (void)nocti_stream_push_biopot_cfg_v1(&g_stream, now_us, &cfg);
}

// ============================================================
// fNIRS scheduler callback
// ============================================================

static void sched_cb(const nocti_sched_event_t *ev, void *user)
{
    (void)user;
    if (!ev) return;

    if (!g_run) return;

    if (ev->phase == NOCTI_EV_SLOT_BEGIN) {
        (void)nocti_led_driver_slot_begin(&ev->slot);
        return;
    }

    if (ev->phase == NOCTI_EV_SLOT_END) {
        (void)nocti_led_driver_slot_end(&ev->slot);

        // slash: finalize slot di akhir window (SLOT_END timestamp sudah berada di akhir window)
        uint8_t led_id = ev->slot.led_id;
        nocti_wavelength_t wl = ev->slot.wavelength;
        uint8_t slot_idx = (uint8_t)(led_id * 2u + wl_to_slot_subindex(wl));

        if (slot_idx < FNIRS_SLOT_COUNT) {
            uint32_t bit = (1u << slot_idx);
            if (g_slot_pending_mask & bit) {
                nocti_time_us_t t0 = g_slot_t0_us[slot_idx];
                nocti_time_us_t t1 = ev->t_event_us; // slash: window end

                bool any_crc_bad = false;
                int32_t avg[4];
                uint8_t n = fnirs_collect_and_average(t0, t1, (uint8_t)FNIRS_K_FIXED, avg, &any_crc_bad);

                g_fnirs_frame.n_used[slot_idx] = n;

                if (n > 0) {
                    g_fnirs_frame.avg[slot_idx][0] = avg[0];
                    g_fnirs_frame.avg[slot_idx][1] = avg[1];
                    g_fnirs_frame.avg[slot_idx][2] = avg[2];
                    g_fnirs_frame.avg[slot_idx][3] = avg[3];
                    g_fnirs_frame.valid_mask |= bit;
                }

                if (n < (uint8_t)FNIRS_K_FIXED) {
                    g_fnirs_frame.underk_mask |= bit;
                }

                if (any_crc_bad) {
                    g_fnirs_frame.crc_mask |= bit;
                }

                g_slot_pending_mask &= ~bit;
            }
        }

        if (ev->slot_index == (FNIRS_SLOT_COUNT - 1u)) {
            uint32_t expect_mask = (FNIRS_SLOT_COUNT >= 32) ? 0xFFFFFFFFu : ((1u << FNIRS_SLOT_COUNT) - 1u);
            if ((g_fnirs_frame.valid_mask & expect_mask) != expect_mask) {
                g_fnirs_frame.flags |= NOCTI_FNIRS_FRAME_F_PARTIAL;
            }

            (void)nocti_stream_push_fnirs_frame_v1(&g_stream, ev->t_event_us, &g_fnirs_frame);
            (void)nocti_stream_maybe_push_time64(&g_stream, ev->t_event_us);

            g_fnirs_frame_id++;
            fnirs_frame_reset();
        }
        return;
    }

    if (ev->phase == NOCTI_EV_DARK_TICK) {
        (void)nocti_stream_maybe_push_time64(&g_stream, ev->t_event_us);
        return;
    }

    if (ev->phase != NOCTI_EV_SLOT_SAMPLE) return;

    // slash: di SAMPLE kita hanya catat t0 window
    // /belajar: baca ADC dilakukan oleh task producer, bukan di callback scheduler
    uint8_t led_id = ev->slot.led_id;
    nocti_wavelength_t wl = ev->slot.wavelength;
    uint8_t slot_idx = (uint8_t)(led_id * 2u + wl_to_slot_subindex(wl));
    if (slot_idx >= FNIRS_SLOT_COUNT) return;

    g_slot_t0_us[slot_idx] = ev->t_event_us;
    g_slot_pending_mask |= (1u << slot_idx);
}

// ============================================================
// Biopot reader task
// ============================================================

typedef struct {
    nocti_biopot_cfg_t cfg;
    uint8_t ch_enable_mask;
    uint8_t bias_sensp_mask;
    uint8_t bias_sensn_mask;
} biopot_runtime_t;

static biopot_runtime_t g_bio_rt;

static void biopot_task_entry(void *arg)
{
    (void)arg;

    while (1) {
        ulTaskNotifyTake(pdTRUE, portMAX_DELAY);
        if (!g_biopot_run) continue;

        nocti_payload_biopot_frame_v1_t fr;
        memset(&fr, 0, sizeof(fr));
        fr.version = NOCTI_PAYLOAD_BIOPOT_FRAME_V1_VERSION;
        fr.flags = NOCTI_BIOPOT_FRAME_F_NONE;
        fr.frame_id = 0;
        fr.n_samples = 0;
        fr.ch_enable_mask = g_bio_rt.ch_enable_mask;
        fr._rsv0 = 0;

        bool dropped_any = false;

        while (g_biopot_run) {
            nocti_biopot_sample_t s;
            esp_err_t err = nocti_biopot_read_sample(&s);
            if (err != ESP_OK) {
                dropped_any = true;
                vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(2));
                continue;
            }

            uint8_t k = fr.n_samples;
            if (k < BIOPOT_BATCH_N) {
                fr.status24[k] = (uint32_t)(s.status24 & 0x00FFFFFFu);
                for (int ch = 0; ch < 8; ch++) {
                    fr.ch[k][ch] = s.ch[ch];
                }
                fr.n_samples = (uint8_t)(k + 1u);
            }

            if (fr.n_samples >= BIOPOT_BATCH_N) {
                if (dropped_any) fr.flags |= NOCTI_BIOPOT_FRAME_F_DROPPED;

                esp_err_t pr = nocti_stream_push_biopot_frame_v1(&g_stream, s.timestamp_us, &fr);
                if (pr != ESP_OK) {
                    dropped_any = true;
                } else {
                    dropped_any = false;
                }

                (void)nocti_stream_maybe_push_time64(&g_stream, s.timestamp_us);

                uint16_t next_id = (uint16_t)(fr.frame_id + 1u);
                memset(&fr, 0, sizeof(fr));
                fr.version = NOCTI_PAYLOAD_BIOPOT_FRAME_V1_VERSION;
                fr.flags = NOCTI_BIOPOT_FRAME_F_NONE;
                fr.frame_id = next_id;
                fr.n_samples = 0;
                fr.ch_enable_mask = g_bio_rt.ch_enable_mask;
                fr._rsv0 = 0;
            }
        }

        if (fr.n_samples > 0) {
            fr.flags |= NOCTI_BIOPOT_FRAME_F_PARTIAL;
            if (dropped_any) fr.flags |= NOCTI_BIOPOT_FRAME_F_DROPPED;
            (void)nocti_stream_push_biopot_frame_v1(&g_stream, (nocti_time_us_t)nocti_timebase_now_us(), &fr);
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================
// WiFi command callback: hook START STOP
// ============================================================

static void wifi_cmd_cb(nocti_wifi_t *self,
                        const uint8_t *data,
                        size_t len,
                        const nocti_wifi_peer_t *from,
                        void *user_ctx)
{
    (void)self;
    (void)from;
    (void)user_ctx;
    if (!data || len == 0) return;

    if (starts_with(data, len, "START")) {
        g_session_id = nocti_wifi_session_id(&g_wifi);
        g_run = true;

        (void)nocti_stream_start_session_now(&g_stream, g_session_id);

        nocti_time_us_t now_us = (nocti_time_us_t)nocti_timebase_now_us();
        push_fnirs_cfg_once(now_us);
        push_biopot_cfg_once(now_us, &g_bio_rt.cfg, g_bio_rt.ch_enable_mask, g_bio_rt.bias_sensp_mask, g_bio_rt.bias_sensn_mask);

        g_fnirs_frame_id = 0;
        fnirs_frame_reset();

        // slash: reset slot state
        memset(g_slot_t0_us, 0, sizeof(g_slot_t0_us));
        g_slot_pending_mask = 0;

        // slash: start ADC producer dulu supaya window cepat terisi
        g_adc_wr = 0;
        g_adc_err_cnt = 0;
        g_adc_crc_bad_cnt = 0;
        g_fnirs_adc_run = true;
        if (g_fnirs_adc_task) xTaskNotifyGive(g_fnirs_adc_task);

        (void)nocti_biopot_start();
        g_biopot_run = true;
        if (g_biopot_task) xTaskNotifyGive(g_biopot_task);

        (void)nocti_scheduler_start();

        NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "START session_id=%lu", (unsigned long)g_session_id);
        return;
    }

    if (starts_with(data, len, "STOP")) {
        // slash: gate callback dulu supaya tidak ada I2C dari sched_cb saat stop
        g_run = false;

        // slash: stop ADC producer
        g_fnirs_adc_run = false;

        nocti_scheduler_stop();
        (void)nocti_led_driver_all_off();

        g_biopot_run = false;
        (void)nocti_biopot_stop();

        NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "STOP");
        return;
    }
}

// ============================================================
// Main
// ============================================================

void app_main(void)
{
    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "boot");

    if (!nocti_timebase_init()) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "timebase init failed");
        return;
    }

    // ============================================================
    // Stream init lebih awal (sebelum driver lain)
    // slash:
    // /belajar: xRingbufferCreate butuh alokasi kontigu.
    // /belajar: kalau kita init stream setelah banyak driver init,
    //           heap bisa terfragmentasi dan alokasi besar gagal.
    // ============================================================

    {
        nocti_stream_cfg_t scfg;
        memset(&scfg, 0, sizeof(scfg));

        // slash:
        // /belajar: total ringbuffer dibuat lebih kecil agar ada ruang untuk WiFi stack.
        // /belajar: biopot lebih besar karena throughput lebih tinggi.
        scfg.rb_ctrl_bytes   = (size_t)(8u  * 1024u);
        scfg.rb_fnirs_bytes  = (size_t)(48u * 1024u);
        scfg.rb_biopot_bytes = (size_t)(64u * 1024u);

        esp_err_t err = nocti_stream_init_now(&g_stream, &scfg);
        if (err != ESP_OK) {
            NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "stream init failed: %s", esp_err_to_name(err));
            return;
        }
    }

    nocti_board_cfg_t bcfg;
    nocti_board_cfg_set_defaults(&bcfg);

    // slash: naikkan I2C speed untuk PCA9956B supaya BEGIN dan END lebih cepat
    // /belajar: PCA9956B support Fast mode plus, jadi 1 MHz biasanya aman jika pullup memadai
    bcfg.i2c_clk_hz = 1000000u;

    bcfg.spi_host_ads1299 = SPI3_HOST;
    bcfg.spi_host_ads131  = SPI2_HOST;

    bcfg.spi_ads1299.clock_hz = 8u * 1000u * 1000u;
    bcfg.spi_ads1299.spi_mode = 1u;
    bcfg.spi_ads1299.use_dma = false;

    bcfg.spi_ads131.clock_hz = 8u * 1000u * 1000u;
    bcfg.spi_ads131.spi_mode = 1u;
    bcfg.spi_ads131.use_dma = false;

    esp_err_t err = nocti_board_init_all(&g_board, &bcfg);
    if (err != ESP_OK) {
        (void)nocti_stream_deinit(&g_stream);
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "board init failed: %s", esp_err_to_name(err));
        return;
    }

    const nocti_board_pins_t *pins = nocti_board_pins(&g_board);

    nocti_led_driver_cfg_t lcfg;
    nocti_led_driver_cfg_set_defaults(&lcfg);
    lcfg.i2c = nocti_board_i2c(&g_board);
    lcfg.i2c_addr_7bit = nocti_board_i2c_addrs(&g_board)->pca9956b_7bit;
    lcfg.rext_ohm = 1000u;
    lcfg.use_nocti_default_map = true;
    lcfg.default_current_ma_735 = FNIRS_CURR_735_MA;
    lcfg.default_current_ma_850 = FNIRS_CURR_850_MA;
    lcfg.apply_current_in_slot = false;
    lcfg.force_off_before_on = false;

    err = nocti_led_driver_init(&lcfg);
    if (err != ESP_OK) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "led driver init failed: %s", esp_err_to_name(err));
        return;
    }
    (void)nocti_led_driver_all_off();

    nocti_fnirs_cfg_t fcfg;
    nocti_fnirs_cfg_set_defaults(&fcfg);

    fcfg.spi = nocti_board_spi_ads131(&g_board);
    fcfg.gpio_drdy  = pins->ads131_drdy;
    fcfg.gpio_reset = pins->ads131_sync_reset;
    fcfg.gpio_sync  = pins->ads131_sync_reset;

    // slash: producer ADC perlu timeout yang relatif pendek supaya stop cepat
    // /belajar: ADS131 drdy period jauh di bawah ini, jadi 5000 us cukup untuk menunggu 1 frame
    fcfg.drdy_timeout_us = 5000u;

    fcfg.crc_check_enable = true;
    fcfg.crc_retry = 1;

    fcfg.do_adc_register_init = true;
    fcfg.reg_mode = 0u;
    fcfg.reg_clock = 0x0F0Eu; // slash: CH0-CH3 enable, TBM=0, OSR=2048 (100b), PWR=HR (10b)
    fcfg.reg_gain = 0u;
    
    fcfg.queue_len = 0;

    err = nocti_fnirs_init(&fcfg);
    if (err != ESP_OK) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "fnirs init failed: %s", esp_err_to_name(err));
        return;
    }

    nocti_biopot_cfg_t bio;
    nocti_biopot_cfg_set_defaults(&bio);

    bio.spi = nocti_board_spi_ads1299(&g_board);
    bio.gpio_drdy  = pins->ads1299_drdy;
    bio.gpio_start = pins->ads1299_start;
    bio.gpio_reset = pins->ads1299_reset;
    bio.gpio_pwdn  = pins->ads1299_pwdn;

    bio.startup_delay_ms = 150;
    bio.drdy_timeout_us  = 10000;
    bio.spi_timeout_ms   = 20;
    bio.rate.dr_bits     = 0x06;

    bio.enable_internal_vref = true;
    bio.enable_bias = true;
    bio.vref_volts = 4.5f;

    for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        bio.ch[i].enable = false;
        bio.ch[i].srb2   = false;
        bio.ch[i].mux    = NOCTI_BIOPOT_MUX_SHORTED;
        bio.ch[i].gain   = NOCTI_BIOPOT_GAIN_12;
    }
    bio.ch[7].enable = true;
    bio.ch[7].mux    = NOCTI_BIOPOT_MUX_NORMAL;
    bio.ch[7].gain   = NOCTI_BIOPOT_GAIN_12;

    err = nocti_biopot_init(&bio);
    if (err != ESP_OK) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "biopot init failed: %s", esp_err_to_name(err));
        return;
    }

    (void)nocti_biopot_set_bias_sense_override(true, 0x80u, 0x80u);

    memset(&g_bio_rt, 0, sizeof(g_bio_rt));
    g_bio_rt.cfg = bio;
    g_bio_rt.ch_enable_mask   = 0x80u;
    g_bio_rt.bias_sensp_mask  = 0x80u;
    g_bio_rt.bias_sensn_mask  = 0x80u;

    static nocti_slot_t slots[FNIRS_SLOT_COUNT];
    uint8_t idx = 0;
    for (uint8_t led = 0; led < 10u; led++) {
        slots[idx].led_id = led;
        slots[idx].wavelength = NOCTI_WL_735;
        slots[idx].on_time_us = FNIRS_ON_TIME_US;
        slots[idx].settle_us = FNIRS_SETTLE_US;
        slots[idx].sample_window_us = FNIRS_WINDOW_US;
        slots[idx].off_gap_us = FNIRS_OFF_GAP_US;
        slots[idx].current_mA = FNIRS_CURR_735_MA;
        idx++;

        slots[idx].led_id = led;
        slots[idx].wavelength = NOCTI_WL_850;
        slots[idx].on_time_us = FNIRS_ON_TIME_US;
        slots[idx].settle_us = FNIRS_SETTLE_US;
        slots[idx].sample_window_us = FNIRS_WINDOW_US;
        slots[idx].off_gap_us = FNIRS_OFF_GAP_US;
        slots[idx].current_mA = FNIRS_CURR_850_MA;
        idx++;
    }

    nocti_scheduler_cfg_t sched;
    memset(&sched, 0, sizeof(sched));
    sched.slots = slots;
    sched.slot_count = FNIRS_SLOT_COUNT;
    sched.tick_period_us = FNIRS_TICK_PERIOD_US;
    sched.frame_period_us = FNIRS_FRAME_PERIOD_US;
    sched.dark_us_total = 0;
    sched.dark_mode = NOCTI_DARK_INTERLEAVED;
    sched.priority = 15; // slash: scheduler harus paling tinggi supaya timing LED stabil
    sched.stack_words = 4096 / sizeof(StackType_t);

    if (!nocti_scheduler_init(&sched, sched_cb, NULL)) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "scheduler init failed");
        return;
    }

    BaseType_t ok = xTaskCreate(
        fnirs_adc_task_entry,
        "fnirs_adc",
        4096,
        NULL,
        13, // slash: cukup tinggi, tapi di bawah scheduler
        &g_fnirs_adc_task
    );
    if (ok != pdPASS) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "fnirs adc task create failed");
        return;
    }

    ok = xTaskCreate(
        biopot_task_entry,
        "biopot",
        4096,
        NULL,
        12,
        &g_biopot_task
    );
    if (ok != pdPASS) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "biopot task create failed");
        return;
    }

    nocti_wifi_cfg_t wcfg;
    nocti_wifi_cfg_set_defaults(&wcfg);

    wcfg.ap_ssid = "REFERENCE_WIFI_SSID";
    wcfg.ap_pass = "REFERENCE_WIFI_PASSWORD";
    wcfg.ap_channel = 6;
    wcfg.ap_max_conn = 1;

    wcfg.udp_ctrl_port = 3333;
    wcfg.udp_stream_port = 3334;
    wcfg.enable_builtin_cmd = true;
    wcfg.enable_advert = true;
    wcfg.advert_period_ms = 1000;

    wcfg.stream = &g_stream;
    wcfg.enable_stream_tx = true;
    wcfg.stream_read_timeout_ms = 50;
    wcfg.use_udp_header = true;
    wcfg.reset_session_on_start = true;

    wcfg.cmd_cb = wifi_cmd_cb;
    wcfg.cmd_cb_ctx = NULL;

    wcfg.ctrl_task_prio = 9;
    wcfg.tx_task_prio = 8;
    wcfg.adv_task_prio = 7;

    err = nocti_wifi_init(&g_wifi, &wcfg);
    if (err != ESP_OK) {
        NOCTI_LOGE(TAG, "wifi init failed: %s", esp_err_to_name(err));
        return;
    }

    NOCTI_LOGI(TAG, "ready: connect to SSID NOCTINIRS then send START to UDP 3333");
    fnirs_frame_reset();

    while (1) {
        vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(2000));

        uint32_t d_fn = nocti_stream_drop_fnirs(&g_stream);
        uint32_t d_bp = nocti_stream_drop_biopot(&g_stream);
        if (d_fn || d_bp) {
            NOCTI_LOGW(TAG, "drop fnirs=%lu biopot=%lu", (unsigned long)d_fn, (unsigned long)d_bp);
        }
    }
}
